# Code review: Glyphbound, changes after the owner's first play-test (read-only)

You are the second-opinion reviewer for **Glyphbound**, a first-person RPG shooter in Unreal Engine 5.9.0 (C++), made for a 4-day game jam. Do not modify any file. Do not build or run anything.

## Context
- Read `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md` first (short). The design is in `Design/GDD.md`; the changelog entry v0.14 at the top lists what the owner asked for after playing the first build.
- The change under review is everything between git commit `798a4e5` and `HEAD` (`git diff 798a4e5 HEAD --stat`, then read the files). It was written by four AI implementers in parallel (reports in `Prompts/feedback1_notes.md`) and then compiled and integrated by a fifth. It builds, the tests pass and a scripted smoke run passes, so a missing include is NOT what we want from you.
- Code: `Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/`. Data: `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json`.
- Engine source for checking APIs: `C:\Program Files\Epic Games\UE_5.9\Engine\Source`.

## What changed (review these areas)
1. **Owned weapons** (`Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.*`, `World/GBPickup.*`, `World/GBChest.cpp`, `Items/GBInventoryComponent.cpp`, `World/GBProgressSubsystem.h`): a picked-up weapon joins the owned list and goes to the hands only if its slot is empty; the player equips owned weapons from the Weapons page of the inventory. Ammo and element of stored weapons must survive swaps, checkpoints, death and era travel.
2. **Held ice shield** (`Spells/GBSpell_Shield.*`, `Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.*`, `Player/GBPlayerCharacter.*`, `Player/GBInputSet.*`): the spell summons a shield for a duration; the Block input (Q / L2, hold) raises it; only a raised shield blocks, and only from the front. Look for: the shield blocking when lowered, never expiring, surviving death or era travel wrongly, the move-speed modifier left on after it ends, blocking while a menu is open, the L2 conflict with the spell modifier on gamepad.
3. **Mouse in menus** (`Player/GBPlayerController.*`, `UI/GBHUD*.cpp`): cursor shown in menus, click handling through hit rects, input mode switched on open / close. Look for: the cursor or the UI input mode left on after closing a menu or after death / respawn, a click acting twice, clicks acting on a stale hit rect of a previous frame or menu, the game staying paused.
4. **Kill-Z** (`Core/GBGameMode.*`, `Player/GBPlayerCharacter.*`, `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.*`): falling below `KillZ` kills the player (once) and enemies (through the attribute component so encounters progress).
5. **Damage numbers** (`UI/GBHUD_DamageNumbers.cpp`, `Combat/GBCombatLibrary.*` global event): look for delegate lifetime problems (HUD destroyed and still bound, bound twice after a respawn), unbounded growth, numbers for the player's own damage taken.
6. **Explosions and textured structures** (`Combat/GBExplosionEffect.*`, `Combat/GBProjectile.*`, `Spells/GBSpell_Projectile.*`, `Spells/GBSpell_Wall.*`, `Core/GBPlaceholder.cpp`, `UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.*`, `Tools/Jam/make_materials.py`): every projectile explosion path must spawn the shockwave exactly once and never damage twice; knockback only on hostile characters; the material `/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelSurface` must load in a packaged build (see `Glyphbound/Config/DefaultGame.ini`) and missing-material fallbacks must not crash.
7. **Enemy pathing** (`AI/GBSteering.*`, `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.*`, `World/GBTestArena.*`, `Config/DefaultEngine.ini`): navmesh built at runtime plus a local steering fallback and a stuck check. Look for: state machine dead ends (an enemy that can never attack because of the new line-of-sight rule, e.g. the trace hitting its own capsule, the player's shield, another enemy or an era-hidden prop), steering toward ledges, timers never reset, enemies frozen after era travel.

## What to report (real defects only, most severe first)
- Runtime bugs a player would hit, state that leaks between death / respawn / era travel / menus, and contract breaks between the modules above.
- Data and code mismatches: a key in a JSON file that the code spells differently or never reads, and the reverse.
- Things that work in the Editor but break in a packaged build.
- Real balance numbers hardcoded where data should decide.

Do not report: style, naming, formatting, missing comments, micro-optimizations, multiplayer issues (single player), or the number of tests.

## Output format
A numbered list, most severe first, at most 20 items. For each:
- `file:line` (or lines),
- what is wrong, in one or two sentences,
- why it matters when playing (what the player would see),
- the smallest fix you would make.
Finish with one short paragraph: the three things you would fix first, and anything you checked and found solid.

# Glyphbound: code architecture

> Written by the architect (Claude Code) on 2026-11-12. Source of truth for how the C++ is organized.
> Design lives in `Design/GDD.md`; this file says how the code implements it. Keep it short and current.

## Principles
1. **Data, not code.** Every gameplay number lives in `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json` and is read through `UGBDataSubsystem`. Code never hardcodes damage, costs, speeds, durations, sizes or timings. The JSON ships as loose files, so numbers can be tuned in a packaged build with a text editor (`gb.ReloadData` reloads while playing).
2. **One stat system.** A number is a *named stat*: base value from data, then modifiers. `UGBStatsComponent` evaluates `(Base + adds) * multipliers` (or an override). Skill-tree nodes, armor, status effects, air passives and the weapon enchant are all just groups of modifiers with a source name. Nothing else changes numbers.
3. **One damage path.** Everything that hurts goes through `UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, FGBDamageInfo)`. Shields, resistances, teams, on-hit effects and kill credit hook in there once.
4. **Spells are data + a behavior.** A spell is a `FGBSpellDefinition` (params with `Base/Min/Max/ScaledBy`) plus the class of an `AGBSpellActor` behavior. The QTE result (power, reach) scales the params tagged for it. A new spell that fits an existing behavior is a JSON entry, no code.
5. **Shared pieces.** One projectile (`AGBProjectile`) for staff bolts, spell projectiles, enemy shots and grenades. One status-effect component for slows, burns, potions and buffs. One interact interface for portals, chests, pickups and the seal.
6. **Text in git.** No binary assets are required to run: input actions, the test arena, placeholder shapes and placeholder pixel sprites are all built in C++ from text. `Content/` is ignored by git except `Content/Data/`.
7. **No GAS.** Unreal's Gameplay Ability System was considered and rejected for the jam: it needs a lot of boilerplate and asset setup, and the game's needs (costs, cooldowns, timed effects, stat upgrades) are covered by principles 2-4 in a few hundred lines we fully control.
8. **PIE is human-only.** Agents build, run the pure-logic tests and package; a person plays.

## Module layout (`Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound`, one runtime module, prefix `GB`)
Includes are relative to the module root: `#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"`.

| Folder | What | Key types |
|---|---|---|
| `Core/` | Shared enums, stat names, placeholders, game mode | `GBTypes.h`, `GBStatNames.h`, `GBPlaceholder.h`, `AGBGameMode`, `UGBGameInstance` |
| `Data/` | Definitions and the JSON loader | `GBDataTypes.h`, `UGBDataSubsystem` |
| `Stats/` | Stats + modifiers, health/mana | `UGBStatsComponent`, `UGBAttributeComponent` |
| `Combat/` | Damage, status effects, projectile | `UGBCombatLibrary`, `UGBStatusEffectComponent`, `AGBProjectile` |
| `Player/` | Character, controller, input | `AGBPlayerCharacter`, `AGBPlayerController`, `UGBInputSet` |
| `Weapons/` | Held items and slots | `UGBEquipmentComponent`, `AGBHeldItem`, `AGBStaff`, `AGBGun`, `AGBSword` |
| `Spells/` | Spellbook, cast resolution, behaviors | `UGBSpellbookComponent`, `FGBSpellCast`, `AGBSpellActor`, `AGBSpell_*` |
| `QTE/` | Quick-time event | `FGBQTESession`, `FGBGestureRecognizer`, `UGBQTEComponent` |
| `World/` | Eras, progress, interactables, test arena | `UGBEraSubsystem`, `UGBEraComponent`, `UGBProgressSubsystem`, `IGBInteractable`, `AGBPortal`, `AGBChest`, `AGBPickup`, `AGBCheckpointVolume`, `AGBTestArena` |
| `AI/` | Enemies and encounters | `AGBEnemyCharacter`, `AGBEncounter` |
| `Items/` | Inventory, consumables, armor, recipes | `UGBInventoryComponent` |
| `Progression/` | Skill trees | `UGBSkillTreeComponent` |
| `UI/` | Canvas HUD (two skins), menus, pixel sprites | `AGBHUD`, `UGBPixelSpriteLibrary` |
| `Tests/` | Pure-logic automation tests | `Glyphbound.<Area>.<Name>` |

## Data files (`Glyphbound/Content/Data`)
`Player.json`, `QTE.json`, `Game.json`, `Spells.json`, `Weapons.json`, `StatusEffects.json`, `Items.json` (items + recipes), `Enemies.json`, `Encounters.json`, `Skills.json`, `Sprites.json`. Schemas are the structs in `Data/GBDataTypes.h` (field name = JSON key). Stat and parameter names are the constants in `Core/GBStatNames.h`.

Modifier keys: an actor stat (`MoveSpeed`), `Weapon.<Id>.<Stat>`, `Weapon.<Slot>.<Stat>` (`Weapon.Staff`, `Weapon.Gun`, `Weapon.Sword`), `Weapon.All.<Stat>`, `Spell.<Id>.<Param>`, `Spell.All.<Param>`, `QTE.TimeDilation`...

Standard status effect ids (defined in `StatusEffects.json`, referenced from other files): `Burn`, `Shock`, `Slow`, `Chill`, `HealthPotion`, `ManaPotion`, `HealthInjection`, `Enchant_Fire`, `Enchant_Lightning`, `Enchant_Ice`.

## How the main flows run
- **Cast:** input -> `UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastSlot` (checks staff in hand, known, cooldown, mana; spends mana) -> `UGBQTEComponent::StartQTE` (slow time, sequence = power, gesture = reach; or instant when off) -> `GBSpell::ResolveCast` (data -> QTE scaling -> modifiers) -> spawn the behavior actor -> it deals damage through `UGBCombatLibrary`.
- **Shoot:** input -> `AGBGun` (fire interval, magazine, spread, recoil, pellets; every number via `GetStat`) -> hitscan -> headshot and distance falloff math in `UGBCombatLibrary` -> `DealDamage`.
- **Era travel:** `AGBPortal::Interact` -> `UGBEraSubsystem::RequestTravel` (fade out, swap era for `UGBEraComponent` actors and `_Past` / `_Future` sub-levels, teleport, restore health/mana/ammo, save checkpoint, fade in).
- **Progress:** encounters set flags in `UGBProgressSubsystem` when cleared; `UGBEraComponent` reads flags, which is how era-gated zones and past-to-future unlocks are built. Death restores the last checkpoint snapshot; flags are never rolled back (enemies do not respawn).
- **Upgrades:** `UGBSkillTreeComponent::Unlock` adds the node's modifiers to the player's stats; weapons and spells pick them up on their next read.

## Conventions
- Game text in English. Code comments in English. ASCII only in source files.
- Native multicast delegates (C++ project; no Blueprint graphs needed).
- No custom collision channels: projectiles and queries use the default channels and filter by team in code.
- Pointers to definitions are not kept across frames (data can be reloaded): keep the id, look it up.
- Tests: only pure logic that is easy to break and hard to see by playing; each one must be able to fail; names `Glyphbound.<Area>.<Name>` where a name is never also a prefix of another.
- Build: `powershell -File Tools/Jam/Build-Jam.ps1` (Editor closed). One build at a time per machine.

# Implementers' reports for the first play-test changes (input for the integration pass)

Condensed by the architect. The architect also added, directly: the public declarations for the new contracts (`UGBEquipmentComponent` owned weapons, `UGBSpellbookComponent` shield API, `AGBHUD::MenuClick`, `UGBInputSet::Block` / `MenuClick`, `GBPlaceholder::SetupTexturedShape`, `FGBPlayerSnapshot::EquippedWeapons`), the global event `UGBCombatLibrary::OnDamageDealt()` (declared and fired in `DealDamage`), `Player.json` JumpVelocity 800, `Game.json` KillZ -2500, the IceShield entry of `Spells.json` (not sustained, Duration 20 s by power, RaisedMoveSpeedMult 0.6), and `/Game/Glyphbound/Materials` in the cook list of `Config/DefaultGame.ini`.

## UI (HUD mouse, weapons page, shield, damage numbers)
- Files: `UI/GBHUD.h`, `UI/GBHUD.cpp`, `UI/GBHUD_Menus.cpp`, `UI/GBHUDShared.h`, new `UI/GBHUD_DamageNumbers.cpp`.
- `AGBHUD::MenuClick` on the death screen calls `AGBGameMode::RespawnPlayer()` itself. REQUIRED: the player controller must not ALSO respawn on a click in the Death menu (one of the two only), or a click respawns twice.
- Game.json "Values" keys TO ADD (code defaults in parentheses): DamageNumberLifetime (0.9), DamageNumberRiseSpeed (90), DamageNumberDriftSpeed (40), DamageNumberTextSize (22), DamageNumberBigScale (1.7), DamageNumberBigHealthFraction (0.25), DamageNumberFadeStart (0.6), DamageNumberMaxAlive (40), DamageNumberMergeWindow (0.08).
- Risks on first compile: a nested non-reflected `enum class` and structs inside the UCLASS body of `AGBHUD`; `static_assert` on a function-local static array size; `FMath::InterpEaseOut`; `Canvas->Project`.
- Mouse coordinates: the HUD multiplies mouse positions by `Canvas->ClipX / Canvas->SizeX` (DPI scale).

## GEAR (owned weapons, held shield, kill-Z, mouse mode)
- Files: `Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.*`, `World/GBPickup.*`, `Spells/GBSpell_Shield.*`, `Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.*`, `Player/GBInputSet.cpp`, `Player/GBPlayerCharacter.*`, `Player/GBPlayerController.*`, `Core/GBGameMode.*`, new `Tests/GBWeaponsTests.cpp` (`Glyphbound.Weapons.SnapshotSlots`).
- Added public API: `UGBEquipmentComponent::GetWeaponAmmo(Id, OutAmmo)`, `GetWeaponElement(Id)`, `SetWeaponElement(Id, Element)`, static `ChooseEquippedWeapons(...)`; `UGBSpellbookComponent::GetShieldActor()`; `AGBSpell_Shield::SetRaised / IsRaised / IsShieldActive / GetShieldHealth / GetShieldMaxHealth / GetTimeRemaining`, `GBShieldParam::RaisedMoveSpeedMult`; `AGBPlayerCharacter::FellOutOfWorld`; `AGBPlayerController::TickActor`; `GBGameValue::KillZ`.
- REQUIRED fixes in files nobody owned this round:
  - `Items/GBInventoryComponent.cpp`, forge recipe (`TryCombine`, around line 462): `GiveWeapon` now returns null when the weapon is stored (slot taken) or already owned and stored. Set the element with `Equipment->SetWeaponElement(Recipe.ResultWeapon, Recipe.ResultElement)` and build the message from `OwnsWeapon`, not from the returned pointer.
  - `Spells/GBSpellActor.h`: the class comment still calls the shield a "sustained frontal block"; make it "summoned shield, raised with the Block input".
  - `World/GBChest.cpp`: when a weapon from a chest was stored (not put in the hands), say "<name> added to the inventory".
- Death screen: the controller's `InputMenuClick` respawns on any click AND `AGBHUD::MenuClick` also respawns there. Make it happen once (keep it in one place).
- Mouse mode: menus use `FInputModeGameAndUI` (no lock, cursor visible); closing uses `FInputModeGameOnly` with `SetConsumeCaptureMouseDown(true)` and re-adds the gameplay context with `bIgnoreAllPressedKeysUntilRelease`. Not verified: that left clicks reach Enhanced Input in GameAndUI mode while the game is paused.
- Kill-Z: `FellOutOfWorld` is called every frame below KillZ; only the first call kills; movement is disabled until `RespawnAt`. The game mode applies `KillZ` relative to the arena floor.
- Gamepad: L2 is Block and also the spell modifier; exception made: L2 + face button lowers the shield and casts.
- Not verified: `TWeakObjectPtr<AGBSpell_Shield>` with a forward-declared type in the spellbook header.

## Late change by the owner: QTE time limit is 4 s
`QTE.json` "QTE.TimeLimit" went from 3 to 4. In the smoke script (`Core/GBDevCommands.cpp`) the step "after the QTE time limit" must run at least 4.5 real seconds after the step that casts with the QTE on: check the step times and move it (and the following steps) if needed.

## STRUCTURES (pixel-textured 3D structures, explosions)
- Files: `Tools/Jam/make_materials.py`, `Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1`, `Core/GBPlaceholder.cpp`, `UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.*`, new `Combat/GBExplosionEffect.*`, `Combat/GBProjectile.*`, `Spells/GBSpell_Projectile.*`, `Spells/GBSpell_Wall.*`, `Sprites.json`, `Spells.json`.
- The material `/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelSurface` is created by `Make-Materials.ps1` (already run once on this machine: the .uasset exists; re-run it only if `make_materials.py` changes). It runs the Editor commandlet WITHOUT `-NullRHI` (with `-AllowCommandletRendering`), and the Editor must be closed. World-space projection built by hand (the engine's WorldAlignedTexture ignores the texture's nearest filter).
- Added: `UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::GetTileTexture`; `GBPlaceholder::SetupTexturedShape` body (`SetShapeColor` also sets "Tint"); `AGBExplosionEffect::Spawn(World, Location, Radius, Color, Duration)`; `FGBProjectileConfig::ExplosionKnockback`, `ExplosionEffectTime`; `GBSpellParam::ExplosionKnockback`, `ExplosionEffectTime`, `WallTileSize`, `WallGlow`; `GBSpellString::WallTexture` (JSON keys "TileSize", "Glow", "Texture").
- Every explosion path spawns the shockwave and pushes hostile characters when `ExplosionKnockback > 0`.
- Tiles added: `tile_ice`, `tile_stone`, `tile_stone_floor`, `tile_wood`.
- Not verified by compiling: `LoadObject` flags, `ELightUnits`, `LaunchCharacter` call.

## AI (enemy pathing, arena, smoke pathing check)
- Files: new `AI/GBSteering.h/.cpp` (pure decision `GBSteering::ChooseOffset(TConstArrayView<bool> Blocked, EGBSteerMode Mode, int32 PreferredSide)`, modes Free / HoldSide / GoAround), new `Tests/GBAITests.cpp` (`Glyphbound.AI.SteerChoice`, 13 cases), `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h/.cpp`, `World/GBTestArena.h/.cpp`, `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp`, `Game.json`, `Config/DefaultEngine.ini` (`[/Script/NavigationSystem.RecastNavMesh] RuntimeGeneration=Dynamic`).
- Added public API: `AGBEnemyCharacter::DescribeMovement()`, `AGBEnemyCharacter::FellOutOfWorld` override (dies through `Attributes->ApplyDamage`, no drops), static `AGBTestArena::GetPathTestPoints(OutEnemySpawn, OutPlayerSpot)`, `AGBTestArena::BuildNavigation()`, new `GBEnemyKey` names.
- Game.json values ALREADY ADDED by this implementer (check they are there): EnemyNavRetryDelay 1.0, EnemySteerFeelerLength 150, EnemySteerAngleStep 25, EnemySteerCandidatesPerSide 6, EnemySteerSideHoldTime 1.0, EnemyStuckTime 0.8, EnemyStuckDistance 50, EnemyGoAroundTime 1.5.
- Behavior: chase by navmesh path when there is nav data; local steering (capsule sweeps along 2N+1 candidate directions) when there is none, the path request fails or the enemy is stuck. Melee enemies now need line of sight to start a windup; ranged enemies abort the windup when they lose sight and reposition during Recover.
- Navmesh: `BuildStatic` spawns an `ANavMeshBoundsVolume` at runtime and gives its brush component a box through `BrushBodySetup->AggGeom` + `UpdateBounds()` + `NavSys->OnNavigationBoundsUpdated`; after `BuildGameplay`, `BuildNavigation()` calls the blocking `NavSys->Build()`. THIS IS THE MAIN RISK (read from the engine source, never run). If the smoke line says `navData=0`, try to make the navmesh work (check how the navigation system is created for a world without placed nav data, e.g. `UNavigationSystemV1` world settings / `bAutoCreateNavigationData`, `SpawnMissingNavigationData`); if it cannot work in reasonable time, the steering fallback alone must still pass the path check, and you say so in the report.
- Arena: shell and cover have ONE hidden collision body present in both eras plus a PastOnly pixel-textured look (`SetupTexturedShape`, tiles `tile_stone_floor` / `tile_stone` / `tile_wood`) and a FutureOnly plain 3D look, neither colliding. Wall height 1100 (was 600). Jump course computed from Player.json when the arena is built (logged at startup).
- Smoke: pathing check starts at 7.8 s and reports at 13.8 s; every later step moved by 6.4 s (travel 14.2 s, quit 31.9 s). Lines: `SMOKE navigation system=%d navData=%d`, `SMOKE path check start: ...`, `SMOKE path check end: ...`, `SMOKE path check passed`, or an error `SMOKE path check FAILED: ...`. `navTime` versus `steerTime` in the end line tells which method the orc used. `Smoke-Jam.ps1` may need a longer timeout now: check it and raise it if the run is cut.
- Not verified: `UBrushComponent::BrushBodySetup` access, spawning `ANavMeshBoundsVolume` in a packaged (game target) build, cost of the synchronous `Build()`, the default nav agent radius (34) against the brute's capsule (60).
# Game Design Document (working draft)

> Epic AI MegaJam 2026 Â· Theme: **DIRECTOR'S CUT** Â· Unreal Engine 5.9.0
> Status: **v0.14, 2026-11-12** â€” owner's vision plus eleven rounds of answers. Anything marked **TBD** is not decided yet; see "Open questions" at the end.
> Audience: **the architect** = whichever Claude Code chat is currently implementing the game. There is normally only one at a time, and it is replaced when its context fills up or its tasks are done. This document is the source of truth that every new architect chat reads first. Implementation is delegated as `CLAUDE.md` says ("Implementador").
> Project code name: **Glyphbound** (provisional; the game title is still TBD). Written in English because it is part of the public dev log (Glass Box) and the jam requires English.

## Changelog
- v0.14 (2026-11-12): owner's first play-test. Magic structures (ice wall) are 3D with pixel textures; the fireball always explodes with a shockwave; both jumps higher; picked-up staffs go to the inventory and are equipped by hand; the ice shield is summoned by the spell and raised with a Block key; enemies must path around obstacles; menus work with the mouse; falling out of the map kills; floating damage numbers.
- v0.13 (2026-11-12): architect's day-1 technical decisions recorded in 0.1 (C++ only, no GAS, JSON data, code-built test arena, canvas HUD); new section 18 lists every provisional rule and number the architect had to pick. Code structure in `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md`.
- v0.12 (2026-11-12): architect autonomy on every technical decision; iteration time is a priority (see `CLAUDE.md`).
- v0.11 (2026-11-12): the 3 s QTE limit covers the whole QTE.
- v0.10 (2026-11-12): QTE lasts 3 s and the player keeps moving in the direction they were moving; CENTRAL has an NVIDIA RTX 4080 Laptop GPU, so DLSS can be tested here.
- v0.9 (2026-11-12): code name Glyphbound; pixel-art look defined (3D geometry with low-res textures, 8-angle sprites); new Â§0.1 technical direction (C++, placeholders, PIE rule, data tables, travel fade, checkpoint-only saving, single player, size budget, NVIDIA award); camera locked during the QTE.
- v0.8 (2026-11-12): enemies do not respawn; provisional game order; story questions parked; open questions narrowed to what the prototype and base systems need.
- v0.7 (2026-11-12): full story (the entity, the false voice, the Valkyrie); elemental zones in the castle unlock the rest of each element; air passives learned the same way; shotgun and rifle dropped by enemies; era travel restores everything (bonfire-like); remaining keys confirmed.
- v0.6 (2026-11-12): art rule clarified for characters (style of the world they belong to); story premise; opening sequence with the first gun; starting spells; enchant only with a gun in hand; key changes (F quick-use, G interact, L1 enchant on gamepad); counter-style ammo refilled on era travel; automatic checkpoints; no sprint; one full-armor slot.
- v0.5 (2026-11-12): sword is a third slot; magazines, reload, limited ammo dropped by enemies; health and mana regenerate, no stamina; state carries across eras; checkpoints; one skill point pool; controls filled in; opening sequence; armor from chests; difficulty = hard only for now.
- v0.4 (2026-11-12): weapon skill tree contents; two held slots (one gun + one staff) and how to switch; no spells with a gun except the enchant; enchant key; air passives always on; ice shield QTE; staffs from chests; sword blade is pixel art with a medieval HUD; final boss mixes both styles.
- v0.3 (2026-11-12): art rule follows the object; the player is always pixel art; 5-spell loadout and controls; staffs and basic magic attack; gun aim; enchant has a QTE, player-chosen element, not on the sword; QTE grading and mana; two UI styles (90s RPG vs. modern cyberpunk) for HUD and skill trees.
- v0.2 (2026-11-12): theme reading, art rule, shared map with era-gated zones, cross-era style mixing, QTE = power + reach, weapon enchant spell, skill points from encounters, enemy waves and sub-bosses, no early melee, architect definition.
- v0.1 (2026-11-12): first dump of the owner's pitch.

## 0. Baseline
At its core this is a **classic first-person RPG plus a classic first-person shooter**. Anything not stated here behaves as players expect from those two genres (magazines and reload, ammo pickups, standard shooter gamepad layout, etc.). This document only lists what is specific to our game.

## 0.1 Technical direction
- **Engine:** Unreal Engine 5.9.0, pinned until submission. **Single player only.**
- **Architect autonomy:** nobody on the team is a programmer. **Every technical and engineering decision is the architect's**, taken without asking the owner: code architecture, C++ vs. Blueprints, plugins, tools, data formats, optimization. Decide, build, and log the decision and its reason in the dev log. When a design detail is missing, pick a reasonable provisional value, mark it provisional here, and keep going; ask the owner only when the game's vision would change.
- **Time first:** iteration speed matters more than elegance. Unused plugins off, Live Coding for `.cpp` changes, only the touched tests on each change, the full suite (under 30 s) once before the daily package. Rules in `CLAUDE.md` ("Tiempo de iteraciÃ³n").
- **Code (decided by the architect, 2026-11-12):** C++ only, one module, no Blueprint graphs; structure in `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md`. **Unreal's Gameplay Ability System is not used**: a small stat-and-modifier system, one damage function and one status-effect component cover the same needs with far less setup.
- **Tuning data (decided by the architect):** every gameplay number lives in text files, `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json` (spells, weapons, enemies, items, skills, player, QTE...). They ship as loose files: the owner can change numbers in a packaged build with a text editor, and `gb.ReloadData` in the console reloads them while playing. This replaces the data tables suggested earlier (git only carries text in this team, and AI agents edit text reliably).
- **Test level (decided by the architect):** the day-1 test arena is built in code on the engine's empty map, so no binary map asset is needed yet. Real levels come later from the level designers.
- **Placeholders first:** pixel art and 3D models are made by other team members on machines on the local network. **The architect never waits for art:** it uses quickly generated placeholders and swaps them later.
- **Placeholder numbers:** the architect puts provisional values (damage, mana, cooldowns, QTE length and time limitâ€¦) in data tables; the owner tunes them later.
- **PIE is human-only:** Play In Editor is always run by a person. AI agents do not run PIE; they compile, package and run tests, then ask a human to play-test. (Also in `CLAUDE.md`.)
- **Era travel:** a fade with an effect when crossing a portal; the transition hides loading the other era's version of the map.
- **Saving:** checkpoints only. No save to disk between sessions.
- **Size:** the jam page says "keep it under 1 GB" for desktop zips, but **bigger builds are allowed** if uploaded with butler (installed on CENTRAL) or Blendo Itch Uploader. The Official Rules PDF sets no size limit. So 1 GB is a target, not a hard limit.
- **NVIDIA RTX Award:** we intend to compete. It is the "best Render Royalty entry built in Unreal Engine" that uses at least one of: an NvRTX feature (RTX Mega Geometry, RTX Direct Illumination, ReSTIR GI, ReSTIR PT path tracing, LSS Hair), **DLSS 4.5 (Unreal Engine)**, or NVIDIA ACE; plus the NVIDIA questions at submission and listing the tool in "Tools you used".
- **Modifier rule (Official Rules Â§10):** a team can win **only one modifier** (Glass Box, Render Royalty, Best Teamâ€¦) unless no other eligible winner remains. We still enter all that apply.

## 1. One-line pitch
A fast first-person **shooter/RPG hybrid**: the player, a medieval mage, jumps through fixed portals between a **pixel-art medieval past** full of magic and a **3D cyberpunk future** full of guns, on the same map in two times, and wins by **mixing spells and firearms** rather than sticking to one style.

Working title: **TBD**. Project code name: **Glyphbound**.

## 2. Pillars
1. **Two eras, one fight.** Past = magic, pixel art. Future = guns, 3D cyberpunk. Both feed the same character.
2. **Combos over specialization.** Spells and guns can be used in both eras, and the game rewards combining them. Weapon and spell mechanics have synergies.
3. **Fast and dynamic combat:** spells, ranged spells, shooting, and melee once the elemental sword is unlocked.
4. **Magic feels crafted, not triggered:** casting uses a two-part slowed-time quick-time event that scales the spell (see Â§7).

## 3. Art rule (never broken)
- **Everything medieval, wizards and magic is pixel art (2D).**
- **Everything cyberpunk and futuristic is 3D.**
- **The rule follows the object, not the era.** A 3D gun stays 3D in the past; a pixel-art fireball stays pixel art in the future.
- **Characters take the style of the world they belong to.** An orc of the castle is a classic pixel-art fantasy orc (80s, 90s, early 2000s games). An orc of the future is a 3D, heavily armored sci-fi orc. They are different enemies, not the same orc in two styles.
- **The player is a medieval mage, so the player is always pixel art, hands included.** In first person, the hands are always pixel art, even when they hold a 3D gun.
- No exceptions.

### The pixel-art look
- **Visual reference:** retro first-person fantasy shooters with modern lighting (the owner's reference: *Wizordum*). Reference only, nothing copied.
- **Pixel-art environments are 3D geometry with low-resolution pixel textures** (nearest filtering, no smoothing), not flat 2D.
- **Pixel-art characters are sprites that always face the camera.** Target: **8 view angles**; fewer if time runs short.
- **Headshots on sprites:** the head is the top part of the sprite.
- **Structures are never flat sprites** (owner, 2026-11-12). Anything that is a structure, the castle and also what magic builds (the ice wall), is a 3D object with 2D pixel textures. Camera-facing sprites are for characters, items, projectiles and effects (flames, bolts).

## 4. World and travel
- **One map, two times** (past and future). The player travels between them **only through predefined portals** placed on the map.
- **Era-gated zones:** some zones are reachable in one era and not in the other.
- **Cross-era unlocks:** some zones must be unlocked in the past so they open in the future (cause in the past, effect in the future).
- These interactions are few (short jam) but they exist and are part of level design.
- **Nothing is lost when changing era.** Health, mana, ammo, inventory, spells and weapons all carry over. Mana exists and works in the future; guns work in the past.
- **Era travel restores everything** (health, mana, ammo), a bit like the rest points of classic action RPGs. Portals are the safe points of the map.

- A third, separate map unlocks late: a **dimension between space and time** where the true final boss lives (see Â§11).

### Story
- The player is a **mage** who travels to the castle out of **curiosity**: he had heard it was a mysterious place.
- On reaching the **seal** in the castle he is **trapped** and thrown into the future.
- In the future he hears **a woman's voice asking for help**. That voice is his reason to fix the problem.
- **The twist:** the voice is **not the Valkyrie**. It is **the Entity**, the true final boss, pretending to be her.
- **The Entity:** a fusion of an **apocalyptic artificial intelligence** and a **ghost that embodies disease**. It is locked in the dimension between space and time and wants out. To escape it needs two things: someone to **open the portal** and a **physical body to possess**.
- **The Valkyrie:** someone the Entity **manipulated across time**. The plan failed, and she ended up **trapped in both eras** (runes in the past, cryogenic tube in the future).
- **What the player does:** defeats the Valkyrie in both eras, gathers what is needed, and opens the portal: **exactly the Entity's plan**. The player walks in with the body it wants to possess. The only thing the Entity did not plan for: **the player beats it**.
- Cutscenes: **few and short**, each one written for its situation.

### Opening sequence
1. The player starts in the **medieval castle** (past) with a **staff and the starting spells** (Â§6).
2. Kills a **small group of pixel-art orcs**.
3. **Interacts with the seal** â†’ **short cutscene #1** (trapped, sent forward in time).
4. Appears in the **future**.
5. Fights **3D armed orcs**. One of the first ones **drops the first gun**.
6. The player **picks up the gun** â†’ **short cutscene #2**. The player **cannot progress** without picking it up.
7. Back to the **castle**.
8. **Elemental zones**, in the order the player chooses (Â§6).
9. **Valkyrie, past.**
10. **Valkyrie, future.**
11. **Final portal** â†’ the Entity (Â§11).

Steps 7â€“11 are **provisional** (owner, 2026-11-12).

## 5. Player progression
- **No character levels / no XP leveling.**
- Progression is through **skill trees**: one for spells and one for weapons (see Â§13 for their look).
  - **Spell tree:** upgrades spells.
  - **Weapon tree:** damage, fire rate, **recoil** (better aim), **damage by distance** (shotgun stronger up close, rifle stronger far away), **headshot damage**.
- **One shared pool of skill points** for both trees.
- **Skill points** are given at specific points, mainly for defeating enemies:
  - defeating each **sub-boss**;
  - defeating each **boss**;
  - **clearing a wave** of enemies.

## 6. Magic (4 elements + weapon enchant)
Each element has 3 entries. Air is passives only; the other three are active spells.

| Element | 1 | 2 | 3 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Fire** | Fireball (projectile; **always explodes with a shockwave**, on an enemy, on the world or at the end of its range) | Fire wall | Flamethrower (sustained DPS) |
| **Lightning** | Lightning bolt (projectile) | Lightning rune trap on the ground; triggers when an enemy walks over it | Static-electricity ball: moves and damages every enemy around it |
| **Ice** | Ice shield (**summoned by the spell, then raised by holding the Block key**, like the shield of a first-person RPG; it only protects while raised; cooldown and limits) | Ice wall | Ice floor: slows enemies, light crowd control |
| **Air** (passives) | Double jump | Improved dash | Increased movement speed |

### Spell loadout
- The player has **5 spells equipped** at a time and can **equip and unequip** them freely.
- **Air passives are always active**: they are not equipped and do not take a slot.
- **Starting spells:** Fireball (very early tier), Lightning bolt (early tier), Ice wall.

### Learning the other spells: elemental zones
- The **medieval castle** has **elemental zones**, one per element.
- Each zone has a **challenge**: a wave, a sub-boss or a boss.
- After the challenge the player gets the element's **knowledge** (from a chest, a book or similar) and **unlocks the missing spells of that element** (fire: fire wall + flamethrower; lightning: rune trap + static ball; ice: ice shield + ice floor).
- **Air passives** are learned the same way, in the air zone.
- The spell tree then upgrades what the player knows.
- **Spells can only be cast with the staff in hand.** With a gun in hand, the only spell available is the weapon enchant. To cast, the player switches to the staff mid-fight.
- Keys: see Â§9.

### Ice shield (owner, 2026-11-12)
- Casting it (with its QTE) **summons** the shield for a time. It is not a force field in front of the player.
- The player **raises it by holding a secondary key** (Block) and lowers it by letting go. It protects from the front **only while raised**.
- Provisional details (architect): Block is **Q** on keyboard and **L2** on gamepad; it needs the staff or the sword in hand (with a gun, L2 is aim); while raised the player moves slower and cannot attack or cast; the shield has health and breaks when it runs out; other spells can be cast while it is summoned and lowered.

### Weapon enchant (always equipped)
- A spell that **cannot be unequipped**.
- **Only usable with a gun in hand** (it enchants the gun).
- Animation: the **left hand draws a magic glyph over the weapon held in the right hand**.
- Effect: the weapon is **temporarily buffed with magic** of one element.
- **Element:** chosen by the player when equipping the spell.
- **Uses the QTE** like every other spell (Â§7).

+bool UGBEquipmentComponent::FindWeaponSlot(FName WeaponId, EGBHeldSlot& OutSlot) const
+{
+	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
+	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
+	if (!Def)
+	{
+		return false;
+	}
+	OutSlot = Def->Slot;
+	return true;
+}
+
+AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::FindEquippedItem(FName WeaponId) const
+{
+	if (WeaponId.IsNone())
+	{
+		return nullptr;
+	}
+	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
+	{
+		AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get();
+		if (IsValid(Item) && Item->GetWeaponId() == WeaponId)
+		{
+			return Item;
+		}
+	}
+	return nullptr;
+}
+
+bool UGBEquipmentComponent::OwnsWeapon(FName WeaponId) const
+{
+	return !WeaponId.IsNone() && OwnedWeapons.Contains(WeaponId);
+}
+
+bool UGBEquipmentComponent::IsWeaponEquipped(FName WeaponId) const
+{
+	return FindEquippedItem(WeaponId) != nullptr;
+}
+
+FGBAmmoState UGBEquipmentComponent::ComputeFullAmmo(FName WeaponId) const
+{
+	FGBAmmoState Full;
+	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
+	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
+	if (!Def || Def->Slot != EGBHeldSlot::Gun)
+	{
+		return Full;
+	}
+	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
+	// Same keys and defaults as AGBHeldItem::GetStat / AGBGun, so a stored gun refills to what it would hold in hand.
+	auto ReadStat = [Def, Stats, WeaponId](FName Stat, float DefaultValue)
+	{
+		const float* Found = Def->Stats.Find(Stat);
+		const float Base = Found ? *Found : DefaultValue;
+		if (!Stats)
+		{
+			return Base;
+		}
+		TArray<FName, TInlineAllocator<3>> Keys;
+		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponKey(WeaponId, Stat));
+		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponSlotKey(EGBHeldSlot::Gun, Stat));
+		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponAllKey(Stat));
+		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Base);
+	};
+	Full.InMagazine = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(ReadStat(GBStat::MagazineSize, 1.f)));
+	Full.SpareMagazines = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(ReadStat(GBStat::SpareMagazines, 0.f)));
+	return Full;
+}
+
+bool UGBEquipmentComponent::GetWeaponAmmo(FName WeaponId, FGBAmmoState& OutAmmo) const
+{
+	if (!OwnsWeapon(WeaponId))
+	{
+		return false;
+	}
+	if (const AGBHeldItem* Item = FindEquippedItem(WeaponId))
+	{
+		if (!Item->UsesAmmo())
+		{
+			return false;
+		}
+		OutAmmo = Item->GetAmmo();
+		return true;
+	}
+	EGBHeldSlot Slot = EGBHeldSlot::Staff;
+	if (!FindWeaponSlot(WeaponId, Slot) || Slot != EGBHeldSlot::Gun)
+	{
+		return false;
+	}
+	const FGBAmmoState* Stored = StoredAmmo.Find(WeaponId);
+	OutAmmo = Stored ? *Stored : ComputeFullAmmo(WeaponId);
+	return true;
+}
+
+EGBElement UGBEquipmentComponent::GetWeaponElement(FName WeaponId) const
+{
+	if (const AGBHeldItem* Item = FindEquippedItem(WeaponId))
+	{
+		return Item->GetElement();
+	}
+	if (const EGBElement* Stored = StoredElements.Find(WeaponId))
+	{
+		return *Stored;
+	}
+	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
+	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
+	return Def ? Def->Element : EGBElement::Physical;
+}
+
+void UGBEquipmentComponent::SetWeaponElement(FName WeaponId, EGBElement InElement)
+{
+	if (!OwnsWeapon(WeaponId))
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	if (AGBHeldItem* Item = FindEquippedItem(WeaponId))
+	{
+		Item->SetElement(InElement);
+	}
+	else
+	{
+		StoredElements.Add(WeaponId, InElement);
+	}
+	OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
+}

+void UGBEquipmentComponent::RestoreItemState(AGBHeldItem* Item)
+{
+	if (!IsValid(Item))
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	const FName WeaponId = Item->GetWeaponId();
+	if (const EGBElement* Element = StoredElements.Find(WeaponId))
+	{
+		Item->SetElement(*Element);
+	}
+	if (const FGBAmmoState* Ammo = StoredAmmo.Find(WeaponId))
+	{
+		Item->SetAmmo(*Ammo); // a gun never held has no entry and stays full from SpawnItem
+	}
+	// While in a slot, the actor is the only source of truth.
+	StoredElements.Remove(WeaponId);
+	StoredAmmo.Remove(WeaponId);
+}

+bool UGBEquipmentComponent::EquipOwnedWeapon(FName WeaponId)
+{
+	if (bSwitchLocked || !OwnsWeapon(WeaponId) || IsWeaponEquipped(WeaponId))
+	{
+		return false;
+	}
+	EGBHeldSlot Slot = EGBHeldSlot::Staff;
+	if (!FindWeaponSlot(WeaponId, Slot))
+	{
+		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("EquipOwnedWeapon: '%s' is no longer in Weapons.json"), *WeaponId.ToString());
+		return false;
+	}
+
+	AGBHeldItem* Old = GetItemInSlot(Slot);
+	// The new item comes up if it replaces the one in the hands (or the hands are empty).
+	const bool bBringUp = ActiveSlot == Slot || GetActiveItem() == nullptr;
+
+	AGBHeldItem* NewItem = SpawnItem(WeaponId);
+	if (!NewItem)
+	{
+		return false;
+	}
+	if (Old)
+	{
+		StoreItemState(Old);
+		Old->Destroy();
+	}
+	Items.Add(Slot, NewItem);
+	RestoreItemState(NewItem);
+
+	if (bBringUp)
+	{
+		EquipSlot(Slot); // cancels a sustained spell, broadcasts both delegates
+		return true;
+	}
+
+	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner()))
+	{
+		if (UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook())
+		{
+			Spellbook->CancelSustained();
+		}
+	}
+	OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
+	return true;
+}

+// ---- Snapshot ----
+
+TMap<EGBHeldSlot, FName> UGBEquipmentComponent::ChooseEquippedWeapons(const TArray<FName>& Owned, const TArray<FName>& Equipped,
+	TFunctionRef<bool(FName, EGBHeldSlot&)> SlotOf)
+{
+	TMap<EGBHeldSlot, FName> Result;
+	auto TryPlace = [&Result, &SlotOf](FName WeaponId)
+	{
+		EGBHeldSlot Slot = EGBHeldSlot::Staff;
+		if (!WeaponId.IsNone() && SlotOf(WeaponId, Slot) && !Result.Contains(Slot))
+		{
+			Result.Add(Slot, WeaponId);
+		}
+	};
+	for (const FName& WeaponId : Equipped)
+	{
+		if (Owned.Contains(WeaponId))
+		{
+			TryPlace(WeaponId);
+		}
+	}
+	for (const FName& WeaponId : Owned)
+	{
+		TryPlace(WeaponId);
+	}
+	return Result;
+}

+void AGBPlayerCharacter::FellOutOfWorld(const UDamageType& /*DmgType*/)
+{
+	// Never Super: the engine would destroy the pawn. The checkpoint respawn brings the player back.
+	if (IsDead())
+	{
+		FreezeMovement(); // already dead (or killed by the first call): just keep it from falling forever
+		return;
+	}
+	if (!Attributes)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+
+	EndDash();
+	FreezeMovement();
+
+	// Friendly fire and invulnerability do not matter here: the hit goes straight to the attributes.
+	const bool bWasInvulnerable = Attributes->IsInvulnerable();
+	Attributes->SetInvulnerable(false);
+	FGBDamageInfo Fall;
+	Fall.Amount = FallOutOfWorldDamage;
+	Fall.Element = EGBElement::Physical;
+	Fall.SourceId = FellOutOfWorldSourceId;
+	Attributes->ApplyDamage(Fall); // HitDirection is zero, so a raised shield cannot block it
+	Attributes->SetInvulnerable(bWasInvulnerable);
+
+	if (!IsDead())
+	{
+		// Only a data setup with DamageTaken 0 gets here: respawn directly rather than fall forever.
+		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: fell out of the world but could not be killed (DamageTaken 0?); respawning at the checkpoint."));
+		if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
+		{
+			if (Progress->RestoreCheckpoint(this))
+			{
+				return;
+			}
+		}
+		UnfreezeMovement();
+	}
+}
+
+void AGBPlayerCharacter::FreezeMovement()
+{
+	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
+	{
+		MoveComp->StopMovementImmediately();
+		if (!bMovementFrozen)
+		{
+			MoveComp->DisableMovement();
+		}
+	}
+	bMovementFrozen = true;
+}
+
+void AGBPlayerCharacter::UnfreezeMovement()
+{
+	if (!bMovementFrozen)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	bMovementFrozen = false;
+	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
+	{
+		// Falling settles into walking on the first floor check.

+void AGBPlayerController::ApplyMouseMode(bool bMenuOpen)
+{
+	if (!IsLocalController())
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	if (bMenuOpen)
+	{
+		bShowMouseCursor = true;
+		FInputModeGameAndUI Mode;
+		Mode.SetLockMouseToViewportBehavior(EMouseLockMode::DoNotLock);
+		Mode.SetHideCursorDuringCapture(false);
+		SetInputMode(Mode);
+		return;
+	}
+	bShowMouseCursor = false;
+	// The click that gives the mouse back to the game is swallowed, so it never fires the weapon.
+	FInputModeGameOnly Mode;
+	Mode.SetConsumeCaptureMouseDown(true);
+	SetInputMode(Mode);
+}

+void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuClick()
+{
+	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
+	{
+		// Like confirm: a click anywhere respawns.
+		if (AGBGameMode* GameMode = AGBGameMode::Get(this))
+		{
+			GameMode->RespawnPlayer();
+		}
+		return;
+	}
+	float MouseX = 0.f;
+	float MouseY = 0.f;
+	if (!GetMousePosition(MouseX, MouseY))
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
+	{
+		HUD->MenuClick(FVector2D(MouseX, MouseY));
+	}

+void AGBSpell_Shield::SetRaised(bool bInRaised)
+{
+	const bool bNewRaised = bInRaised && IsShieldActive();
+	if (bNewRaised == bRaised)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	bRaised = bNewRaised;
+
+	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
+	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = IsValid(Caster) ? UGBStatsComponent::Find(Caster) : nullptr;
+	if (!Stats)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	if (bRaised)
+	{
+		FGBModifierDef Slow;
+		Slow.Stat = GBStat::MoveSpeed;
+		Slow.Op = EGBModOp::Multiply;
+		Slow.Value = RaisedMoveSpeedMult;
+		TArray<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers;
+		Modifiers.Add(Slow);
+		Stats->AddModifiers(RaisedSource, Modifiers);
+	}
+	else
+	{
+		Stats->RemoveModifiers(RaisedSource);
+	}
+}
+
+float AGBSpell_Shield::GetTimeRemaining() const
+{
+	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
+	if (bEnded || !bSpellStarted || !World)
+	{
+		return 0.f;
+	}
+	return static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(0.0, ExpireTime - World->GetTimeSeconds()));
+}

+// ---- Summoned shield ----
+
+AGBSpell_Shield* UGBSpellbookComponent::GetActiveShield() const
+{
+	AGBSpell_Shield* Current = Shield.Get();
+	return (Current && Current->IsShieldActive()) ? Current : nullptr;
+}
+
+AGBSpellActor* UGBSpellbookComponent::GetShieldActor() const
+{
+	return GetActiveShield();
+}
+
+void UGBSpellbookComponent::RegisterShield(AGBSpellActor* InShield)
+{
+	AGBSpell_Shield* NewShield = Cast<AGBSpell_Shield>(InShield);
+	if (!NewShield)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	AGBSpell_Shield* Old = Shield.Get();
+	if (Old == NewShield)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	Shield = NewShield;
+	if (Old)
+	{
+		// A new shield replaces the previous one. Its EndPlay unregisters, which is ignored now that it is not current.
+		Old->SetRaised(false);
+		Old->OnCastReleased();
+	}
+	SetBlockHeld(bBlockHeld);
+}
+
+void UGBSpellbookComponent::UnregisterShield(AGBSpellActor* InShield)
+{
+	if (InShield && Shield.Get() == InShield)
+	{
+		Shield.Reset();
+		bBlockHeld = false;
+	}
+}
+
+void UGBSpellbookComponent::EndShield()
+{
+	AGBSpell_Shield* Current = Shield.Get();
+	Shield.Reset();
+	bBlockHeld = false;
+	if (IsValid(Current))
+	{
+		Current->SetRaised(false);
+		Current->OnCastReleased();
+	}
+}
+
+bool UGBSpellbookComponent::CanRaiseBlock() const
+{
+	if (!PendingSpellId.IsNone())
+	{
+		return false;
+	}
+	if (const UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE())
+	{
+		if (QTE->IsActive())
+		{
+			return false;
+		}
+	}
+	if (const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment())
+	{
+		if (Equipment->IsHoldingGun())
+		{
+			return false;
+		}
+	}
+	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes();
+	return !(Attributes && Attributes->IsDead());
+}
+
+void UGBSpellbookComponent::SetBlockHeld(bool bHeld)
+{
+	AGBSpell_Shield* Current = GetActiveShield();
+	bBlockHeld = bHeld && Current && CanRaiseBlock();
+	if (Current)
+	{
+		Current->SetRaised(bBlockHeld);
+	}
+}
+
+bool UGBSpellbookComponent::HasShield() const
+{
+	return GetActiveShield() != nullptr;
+}
+
+bool UGBSpellbookComponent::IsBlockRaised() const
+{
+	const AGBSpell_Shield* Current = GetActiveShield();
+	return Current && Current->IsRaised();
+}
+
+float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetShieldHealthFraction() const
+{
+	const AGBSpell_Shield* Current = GetActiveShield();
+	if (!Current || Current->GetShieldMaxHealth() <= 0.f)
+	{
+		return 0.f;
+	}
+	return FMath::Clamp(Current->GetShieldHealth() / Current->GetShieldMaxHealth(), 0.f, 1.f);
+}
+
+float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetShieldTimeRemaining() const
+{
+	const AGBSpell_Shield* Current = GetActiveShield();
+	return Current ? Current->GetTimeRemaining() : 0.f;
 }

namespace
{
	// Pure geometry, not gameplay: how far the aimed point is pulled back from a wall, and how high above a point
	// the ground search starts (so a slope or a step under the aimed point is still found).
	constexpr float SpellActorAimPullBackCm = 20.f;
	constexpr float SpellActorGroundLiftCm = 100.f;
	constexpr float SpellActorMinTickInterval = 0.05f;
}

AGBSpellActor::AGBSpellActor()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = false;
	Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	RootComponent = Root;
}

void AGBSpellActor::InitSpell(const FGBSpellCast& InCast)
{
	SpellCast = InCast;
	bSpellInitialized = true;
	if (HasActorBegunPlay())
	{
		TryStartSpell();
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	if (!bSpellInitialized)
	{
		// Spawned without the spellbook (placed in a level, or InitSpell will come right after FinishSpawning).
		return;
	}
	TryStartSpell();
}

void AGBSpellActor::TryStartSpell()
{
	if (bSpellStarted || bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	bSpellStarted = true;
	StartSpell();
}

void AGBSpellActor::OnCastReleased()
{
	EndSpell();
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndSpell()
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	bEnded = true;
	GetWorldTimerManager().ClearAllTimersForObject(this);
	OnSpellEnded.Broadcast();
	Destroy();
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	// Destroyed by something else (level unload, era swap): the spellbook must still hear that the spell is over.
	if (!bEnded)
	{
		bEnded = true;
		OnSpellEnded.Broadcast();
	}
	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
	{
		World->GetTimerManager().ClearAllTimersForObject(this);
	}
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

bool AGBSpellActor::FindGround(const FVector& From, FVector& OutGround, float MaxDistance) const
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return false;
	}
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellFindGround), false, this);
	if (AActor* Caster = GetCaster())
	{
		Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
	}
	FHitResult Hit;
	const FVector End = From - FVector::UpVector * MaxDistance;
	if (World->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, From, End, FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		OutGround = Hit.ImpactPoint;
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

float AGBSpellActor::ReadParam(FName Param, float DefaultValue) const
{
	if (const float* Value = SpellCast.Values.Find(Param))
	{
		return *Value;
	}
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s' (%s): param '%s' is missing in Spells.json, using %.2f."),
		*SpellCast.SpellId.ToString(), *GetClass()->GetName(), *Param.ToString(), DefaultValue);
	return DefaultValue;
}

FLinearColor AGBSpellActor::GetSpellColor() const
{
	const FLinearColor ElementColor = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(SpellCast.Element));
	return UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(SpellCast.GetString(GBString::Color), ElementColor);
}

FName AGBSpellActor::GetSpriteId() const
{
	const FString Sprite = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::Sprite);
	return Sprite.IsEmpty() ? NAME_None : FName(*Sprite);
}

void AGBSpellActor::SetupSpellSprite(UBillboardComponent* Billboard, float DefaultHeight) const
{
	if (!Billboard)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FName SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this);
	if (SpriteId.IsNone() || !Library)
	{
		Billboard->SetHiddenInGame(true);
		Billboard->SetVisibility(false);
		return;
	}
	Library->SetupBillboard(Billboard, SpriteId, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, DefaultHeight));
}

void AGBSpellActor::GetCasterAim(FVector& OutOrigin, FVector& OutDirection) const
{
	OutOrigin = SpellCast.Origin;
	OutDirection = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Caster))
	{
		OutOrigin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
		OutDirection = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}
	else if (Caster)
	{
		FRotator EyeRotation;
		Caster->GetActorEyesViewPoint(OutOrigin, EyeRotation);
		OutDirection = EyeRotation.Vector();
	}
	if (OutDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		OutDirection = FVector::ForwardVector;
	}
}

FVector AGBSpellActor::FindAimedGroundPoint(float MaxRange) const
{
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	FVector Fallback = Caster ? Caster->GetActorLocation() : SpellCast.Origin;
	if (Caster)
	{
		Fallback.Z -= Caster->GetSimpleCollisionHalfHeight();
	}

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return Fallback;
	}

	const FVector Dir = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	FVector Point = SpellCast.Origin;
	if (MaxRange > 0.f && !Dir.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellAim), false, this);
		if (Caster)
		{
			Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
		}
		FHitResult Hit;
		const FVector End = SpellCast.Origin + Dir * MaxRange;
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, SpellCast.Origin, End, ECC_Visibility, Params))
		{
			Point = Hit.ImpactPoint - Dir * SpellActorAimPullBackCm;
		}
		else
		{
			Point = End;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		return FindGround(Fallback + FVector::UpVector * SpellActorGroundLiftCm, Point) ? Point : Fallback;
	}

	FVector Ground;
	if (FindGround(Point + FVector::UpVector * SpellActorGroundLiftCm, Ground))
	{
		return Ground;
	}
	return Fallback;
}

bool AGBSpellActor::IsHostileTarget(const AActor* Actor) const
{
	if (!Actor || Actor == this || Actor == GetCaster())
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (!UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Actor) || !UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Actor))
	{
		return false;
	}
	return UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(GetCaster(), Actor);
}

void AGBSpellActor::QueryHostiles(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FCollisionShape& Shape, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}
	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellArea), false, this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Center, Rotation, ObjectParams, Shape, Params);
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Actor = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (IsHostileTarget(Actor))
		{
			OutActors.AddUnique(Actor);
		}
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInSphere(const FVector& Center, float Radius, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), OutActors);
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesOnDisc(const FVector& GroundCenter, float Radius, float Height, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f || Height <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector BoxCenter = GroundCenter + FVector(0.f, 0.f, Height * 0.5f);
	QueryHostiles(BoxCenter, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(FVector(Radius, Radius, Height * 0.5f)), OutActors);
	// The box is only the broad phase: keep actors whose collision touches the disc horizontally.
	OutActors.RemoveAll([&GroundCenter, Radius](const AActor* Actor)
	{
		return FVector::Dist2D(Actor->GetActorLocation(), GroundCenter) > Radius + Actor->GetSimpleCollisionRadius();
	});
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInBox(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FVector& Extent, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Extent.GetMin() <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, Rotation, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(Extent), OutActors);
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndSpellAfter(float Seconds)
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (Seconds <= 0.f)
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimerForNextTick(this, &AGBSpellActor::EndSpell);
	}
	else
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(EndTimer, this, &AGBSpellActor::EndSpell, Seconds, false);
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::DamageTarget(AActor* Target, float Amount, const FVector& SourceLocation)
{
	if (!Target)
	{
		return;
	}
	FGBDamageInfo Info = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(NAME_None, this);
	Info.Amount = Amount;
	Info.HitLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
	Info.HitDirection = (Target->GetActorLocation() - SourceLocation).GetSafeNormal();
	UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
}

void AGBSpellActor::ApplyOnHitEffect(AActor* Target) const
{
	const FString EffectId = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::OnHitEffect);
	if (!Target || EffectId.IsEmpty())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(Target))
	{
		Effects->ApplyEffect(FName(*EffectId), GetCaster(),
			SpellCast.Get(GBStat::EffectDuration, 1.f), SpellCast.Get(GBStat::EffectStrength, 1.f));
	}
}

float AGBSpellActor::SafeInterval(float Interval)
{
	return FMath::Max(Interval, SpellActorMinTickInterval);
}
#include "Player/GBPlayerController.h"

#include "Player/GBInputSet.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Core/GBGameMode.h"
#include "UI/GBHUD.h"

#include "EnhancedInputComponent.h"
#include "EnhancedInputSubsystems.h"
#include "InputActionValue.h"
#include "Engine/LocalPlayer.h"

namespace
{
	// The menu context sits above the gameplay one, so during the death screen (both may be active) menu keys win.
	constexpr int32 GameplayContextPriority = 0;
	constexpr int32 MenuContextPriority = 1;
	constexpr float MenuAxisStepThreshold = 0.1f;

	bool IsPausingMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
	{
		return Menu == EGBMenu::Pause || Menu == EGBMenu::Inventory || Menu == EGBMenu::SkillTrees;
	}
}

AGBPlayerController::AGBPlayerController()
{
	// Hidden while playing; shown while a menu is open (ApplyMouseMode).
	bShowMouseCursor = false;
}

void AGBPlayerController::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	if (IsLocalController())
	{
		ApplyMouseMode(OpenMenu != EGBMenu::None);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::TickActor(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorTickFunction& ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickActor(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);
	if (bCenterCursorPending)
	{
		bCenterCursorPending = false;
		if (OpenMenu != EGBMenu::None)
		{
			CenterMouseCursor();
		}
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::ApplyMouseMode(bool bMenuOpen)
{
	if (!IsLocalController())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (bMenuOpen)
	{
		bShowMouseCursor = true;
		FInputModeGameAndUI Mode;
		Mode.SetLockMouseToViewportBehavior(EMouseLockMode::DoNotLock);
		Mode.SetHideCursorDuringCapture(false);
		SetInputMode(Mode);
		return;
	}
	bShowMouseCursor = false;
	// The click that gives the mouse back to the game is swallowed, so it never fires the weapon.
	FInputModeGameOnly Mode;
	Mode.SetConsumeCaptureMouseDown(true);
	SetInputMode(Mode);
}

void AGBPlayerController::CenterMouseCursor()
{
	int32 SizeX = 0;
	int32 SizeY = 0;
	GetViewportSize(SizeX, SizeY);
	if (SizeX > 0 && SizeY > 0)
	{
		SetMouseLocation(SizeX / 2, SizeY / 2);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::SetupInput(UGBInputSet* InInputSet)
{
	if (!InInputSet)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerController: SetupInput called without an input set."));
		return;
	}
	const bool bNewSet = InputSet != InInputSet;
	InputSet = InInputSet;

	if (UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem* Subsystem = ULocalPlayer::GetSubsystem<UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem>(GetLocalPlayer()))
	{
		if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
		{
			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext, GameplayContextPriority);
		}
		else
		{
			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext, MenuContextPriority);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerController: no Enhanced Input subsystem, input will not work."));
	}

	UEnhancedInputComponent* EIC = Cast<UEnhancedInputComponent>(InputComponent);
	if (!EIC)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("GBPlayerController: the controller input component is not an UEnhancedInputComponent."));
		return;
	}
	if (!bNewSet && EIC->GetActionEventBindings().Num() > 0)
	{
		return; // already bound to this set
	}
	EIC->ClearActionBindings();

	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->Inventory, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputInventory);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->SkillTrees, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSkillTrees);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->Pause, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputPause);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuNavigate, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuNavigate);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuConfirm, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuConfirm);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuBack, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuBack);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuTab, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuTab);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuClick, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuClick);
}

void AGBPlayerController::SetMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	if (Menu == OpenMenu)
	{
		return;
	}
	const EGBMenu OldMenu = OpenMenu;
	AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD());

	if (OldMenu != EGBMenu::None && HUD)
	{
		HUD->OnMenuClosed(OldMenu);
	}
	OpenMenu = Menu;

	UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem* Subsystem = ULocalPlayer::GetSubsystem<UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem>(GetLocalPlayer());
	if (Menu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		SetPause(false);
		if (Subsystem && InputSet)
		{
			Subsystem->RemoveMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext);
			// A key or button still held from the menu (the click that closed it) does nothing until released.
			FModifyContextOptions Options;
			Options.bIgnoreAllPressedKeysUntilRelease = true;
			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext, GameplayContextPriority, Options);
		}
		bCenterCursorPending = false;
		ApplyMouseMode(false);
		return;
	}

	if (OldMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		if (AGBPlayerCharacter* PlayerCharacter = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetPawn()))
		{
			PlayerCharacter->ReleaseHeldInputs();
		}
	}
	if (Subsystem && InputSet)
	{
		// The death screen also drops the gameplay keys: nothing to do in the world while dead.
		Subsystem->RemoveMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext);
		Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext, MenuContextPriority);
	}
	SetPause(IsPausingMenu(Menu));

	ApplyMouseMode(true);
	if (OldMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		CenterMouseCursor();
		bCenterCursorPending = true;
	}

	if (HUD)
	{
		HUD->OnMenuOpened(Menu);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::ToggleMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	SetMenu(OpenMenu == Menu ? EGBMenu::None : Menu);
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputInventory()
{
	if (OpenMenu != EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		ToggleMenu(EGBMenu::Inventory);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputSkillTrees()
{
	if (OpenMenu != EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		ToggleMenu(EGBMenu::SkillTrees);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputPause()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	SetMenu(OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None ? EGBMenu::Pause : EGBMenu::None);
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuNavigate(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		return;
	}
	// Value: x right, y up. One step per press along the dominant axis; the HUD wants y down.
	const FVector2D Axis = Value.Get<FVector2D>();
	if (Axis.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		return;
	}
	FIntPoint Step(0, 0);
	if (FMath::Abs(Axis.X) >= FMath::Abs(Axis.Y))
	{
		Step.X = Axis.X > 0.f ? 1 : -1;
	}
	else
	{
		Step.Y = Axis.Y > 0.f ? -1 : 1;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuNavigate(Step);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuConfirm()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		if (AGBGameMode* GameMode = AGBGameMode::Get(this))
		{
			GameMode->RespawnPlayer();
		}
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuConfirm();
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuBack()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None || OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD());
	if (!HUD || !HUD->MenuBack())
	{
		SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuTab(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None || OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float Axis = Value.Get<float>();
	if (FMath::Abs(Axis) < MenuAxisStepThreshold)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuTab(Axis > 0.f ? 1 : -1);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuClick()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		// Like confirm: a click anywhere respawns.
		if (AGBGameMode* GameMode = AGBGameMode::Get(this))
		{
			GameMode->RespawnPlayer();
		}
		return;
	}
	float MouseX = 0.f;
	float MouseY = 0.f;
	if (!GetMousePosition(MouseX, MouseY))
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuClick(FVector2D(MouseX, MouseY));
	}
}
// Floating damage numbers of AGBHUD: one per hit on anything but the local player, rising and fading out.
#include "UI/GBHUD.h"

#include "UI/GBHUDShared.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"

#include "Camera/PlayerCameraManager.h"
#include "Engine/Canvas.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "GameFramework/PlayerController.h"

namespace GBDamageNumberLocal
{
	/** Pure presentation: length and size of the "pop" of a big hit. */
	constexpr float PopTime = 0.15f;
	constexpr float PopExtra = 0.35f;

	struct FTuning
	{
		float Lifetime = 0.9f;
		float RiseSpeed = 90.f;
		float DriftSpeed = 40.f;
		float TextSize = 22.f;
		float BigScale = 1.7f;
		float BigHealthFraction = 0.25f;
		float FadeStart = 0.6f;
		int32 MaxAlive = 40;
		float MergeWindow = 0.08f;
	};

	FTuning ReadTuning(const UObject* Context)
	{
		FTuning T;
		if (const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(Context))
		{
			T.Lifetime = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberLifetime, T.Lifetime);
			T.RiseSpeed = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberRiseSpeed, T.RiseSpeed);
			T.DriftSpeed = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberDriftSpeed, T.DriftSpeed);
			T.TextSize = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberTextSize, T.TextSize);
			T.BigScale = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberBigScale, T.BigScale);
			T.BigHealthFraction = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberBigHealthFraction, T.BigHealthFraction);
			T.FadeStart = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberFadeStart, T.FadeStart);
			T.MaxAlive = FMath::RoundToInt(Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberMaxAlive, (float)T.MaxAlive));
			T.MergeWindow = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberMergeWindow, T.MergeWindow);
		}
		T.Lifetime = FMath::Max(0.05f, T.Lifetime);
		T.TextSize = FMath::Max(4.f, T.TextSize);
		T.BigScale = FMath::Max(1.f, T.BigScale);
		T.FadeStart = FMath::Clamp(T.FadeStart, 0.f, 0.99f);
		T.MaxAlive = FMath::Max(1, T.MaxAlive);
		T.MergeWindow = FMath::Max(0.f, T.MergeWindow);
		return T;
	}

	bool IsBigHit(float Amount, float MaxHealth, float Fraction)
	{
		return MaxHealth > 0.f && Fraction > 0.f && Amount >= Fraction * MaxHealth;
	}
}

double AGBHUD::GetWorldTime() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? World->GetTimeSeconds() : 0.0;
}

void AGBHUD::HandleDamageDealt(AActor* Target, const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float DamageDealt)
{
	using namespace GBDamageNumberLocal;
	// The delegate is global: ignore other worlds and the local player (the red flash covers the player).
	if (DamageDealt <= 0.f || (Target && Target->GetWorld() != GetWorld()) || (Target && Target == GetOwningPawn()))
	{
		return;
	}
	if (!Target && Info.HitLocation.IsZero())
	{
		return;
	}
	const FTuning Tuning = ReadTuning(this);
	const double Now = GetWorldTime();
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Target ? UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target) : nullptr;
	const float MaxHealth = Attributes ? Attributes->GetMaxHealth() : 0.f;

	// Hits on the same target in a very short window (pellets, ticks) become one number.
	if (Target && Tuning.MergeWindow > 0.f)
	{
		for (FDamageNumber& Number : DamageNumbers)
		{
			if (Number.Target.Get() == Target && Now - Number.StartTime <= Tuning.MergeWindow)
			{
				Number.Amount += DamageDealt;
				Number.bBig = Number.bBig || Info.bHeadshot || IsBigHit(Number.Amount, Number.TargetMaxHealth, Tuning.BigHealthFraction);
				return;
			}
		}
	}

	FDamageNumber Number;
	Number.Target = Target;
	if (!Info.HitLocation.IsZero())
	{
		Number.WorldPosition = Info.HitLocation;
	}
	else
	{
		FVector Origin;
		FVector Extent;
		Target->GetActorBounds(true, Origin, Extent);
		Number.WorldPosition = Origin + FVector(0.f, 0.f, Extent.Z);
	}
	Number.Drift = FMath::FRandRange(-1.f, 1.f);
	Number.Amount = DamageDealt;
	Number.TargetMaxHealth = MaxHealth;
	Number.Element = Info.Element;
	Number.bBig = Info.bHeadshot || IsBigHit(DamageDealt, MaxHealth, Tuning.BigHealthFraction);
	Number.StartTime = Now;
	DamageNumbers.Add(Number);
	while (DamageNumbers.Num() > Tuning.MaxAlive)
	{
		DamageNumbers.RemoveAt(0);
	}
}

void AGBHUD::DrawDamageNumbers()
{
	using namespace GBDamageNumberLocal;
	if (DamageNumbers.Num() == 0 || !Canvas)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FTuning Tuning = ReadTuning(this);
	const double Now = GetWorldTime();
	DamageNumbers.RemoveAll([Now, &Tuning](const FDamageNumber& Number) { return Now - Number.StartTime >= Tuning.Lifetime || Now < Number.StartTime; });

	const float S = UIScale();
	const APlayerCameraManager* Camera = PlayerOwner ? PlayerOwner->PlayerCameraManager.Get() : nullptr;
	const FVector CameraLocation = Camera ? Camera->GetCameraLocation() : FVector::ZeroVector;
	const FVector CameraForward = Camera ? Camera->GetCameraRotation().Vector() : FVector::ForwardVector;
	const float Outline = FMath::Max(1.f, 2.f * S);

	for (const FDamageNumber& Number : DamageNumbers)
	{
		if (Camera && FVector::DotProduct(Number.WorldPosition - CameraLocation, CameraForward) <= 0.f)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FVector Screen = Canvas->Project(Number.WorldPosition, true);
		if (Screen.Z <= 0.f)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const float Age = (float)(Now - Number.StartTime);
		const float Life01 = FMath::Clamp(Age / Tuning.Lifetime, 0.f, 1.f);
		const float Alpha = Life01 <= Tuning.FadeStart ? 1.f : 1.f - (Life01 - Tuning.FadeStart) / (1.f - Tuning.FadeStart);

		float SizePx = Tuning.TextSize;
		const FLinearColor Base = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor((uint8)Number.Element);
		FLinearColor Color = FMath::Lerp(Base, FLinearColor::White, 0.25f);
		if (Number.bBig)
		{
			// Scale up then settle, and a brighter, warmer color.
			const float Pop01 = FMath::Clamp(Age / PopTime, 0.f, 1.f);
			SizePx *= Tuning.BigScale * (1.f + PopExtra * FMath::Sin(Pop01 * UE_PI));
			Color = FMath::Lerp(Base, FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 0.6f, 1.f), 0.6f);
		}
		Color.A = Alpha;

		const float X = Screen.X + Number.Drift * Tuning.DriftSpeed * S * Age;
		const FString Text = FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(Number.Amount)));
		const FVector2D Size = MeasureLabel(Text, SizePx, true);
		const float Y = Screen.Y - Tuning.RiseSpeed * S * Age - Size.Y * 0.5f;

		// Dark outline so the number reads on any background.
		const FLinearColor Dark(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.85f * Alpha);
		DrawLabel(Text, Dark, X - Outline, Y, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
		DrawLabel(Text, Dark, X + Outline, Y, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
		DrawLabel(Text, Dark, X, Y - Outline, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
		DrawLabel(Text, Dark, X, Y + Outline, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
		DrawLabel(Text, Color, X, Y, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
	}
}

#include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"

#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Combat/GBExplosionEffect.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h"
#include "CollisionQueryParams.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Components/SphereComponent.h"
