namespace GBProjectileLocal
{
	/** Same lift as the radial damage obstruction trace (GBCombatLibrary.cpp), so a blast on the floor is not blocked by it. */
	constexpr float KnockbackTraceLiftCm = 5.f;
	/**
	 * Smallest upward part of the push direction. A walking character pushed only sideways is stopped at once by
	 * ground friction; a little lift makes it fly, so the push is felt even for a blast at the target's height.
	 */
	constexpr float KnockbackMinLift = 0.35f;
}

AGBProjectile::AGBProjectile()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	Collision = CreateDefaultSubobject<USphereComponent>(TEXT("Collision"));
	Collision->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
	Collision->SetCollisionObjectType(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToAllChannels(ECR_Ignore);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Overlap);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldDynamic, ECR_Overlap);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldStatic, ECR_Block);
	Collision->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
	Collision->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
	Collision->OnComponentHit.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionHit);
	Collision->OnComponentBeginOverlap.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionOverlap);
	RootComponent = Collision;

	Movement = CreateDefaultSubobject<UProjectileMovementComponent>(TEXT("Movement"));
	Movement->UpdatedComponent = Collision;
	Movement->bRotationFollowsVelocity = true;
	// The launch velocity is given in world space by InitProjectile.
	Movement->bInitialVelocityInLocalSpace = false;
	Movement->bShouldBounce = false;
	Movement->ProjectileGravityScale = 0.f;

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Collision);

	Mesh = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Mesh"));
	Mesh->SetupAttachment(Collision);
	Mesh->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Mesh->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Mesh->SetCastShadow(false);
	Mesh->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
}

AGBProjectile* AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(UWorld* World, const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
{
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const FVector LaunchDirection = Direction.IsNearlyZero() ? FVector::ForwardVector : Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	const FTransform SpawnTransform(LaunchDirection.Rotation(), Origin);

	AGBProjectile* Projectile = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBProjectile>(AGBProjectile::StaticClass(), SpawnTransform, InInstigator,
		Cast<APawn>(InInstigator), ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn);
	if (!Projectile)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Projectile: spawn failed"));
		return nullptr;
	}

	Projectile->InitProjectile(InConfig, LaunchDirection, InInstigator);
	Projectile->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);

	if (IsValid(InInstigator) && IsValid(Projectile))
	{
		Projectile->Collision->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(InInstigator, true);
		if (UPrimitiveComponent* InstigatorRoot = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(InInstigator->GetRootComponent()))
		{
			InstigatorRoot->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(Projectile, true);
		}
	}

	return IsValid(Projectile) ? Projectile : nullptr;
}

void AGBProjectile::InitProjectile(const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
{
	Config = InConfig;
	if (InInstigator)
	{
		Config.Damage.Instigator = InInstigator;
	}
	Config.Damage.Causer = this;
	StartLocation = GetActorLocation();

	const float Radius = FMath::Max(Config.Radius, 1.f);
	Collision->SetSphereRadius(Radius);

	Movement->InitialSpeed = Config.Speed;
	Movement->MaxSpeed = Config.Speed;
	Movement->ProjectileGravityScale = Config.GravityScale;
	Movement->bShouldBounce = Config.FuseTime > 0.f;
	Movement->Velocity = Direction.GetSafeNormal() * Config.Speed;

	const float VisualSize = Radius * 2.f * FMath::Max(Config.VisualScale, 0.01f);
	if (Config.bPixelArt)
	{
		Mesh->SetVisibility(false);
		if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* SpriteLibrary = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this))
		{
			SpriteLibrary->SetupBillboard(Sprite, Config.SpriteId, VisualSize);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Projectile: no pixel sprite library; sprite '%s' not shown"), *Config.SpriteId.ToString());
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
		Sprite->SetHiddenInGame(true);
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Mesh, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(VisualSize), Config.Color, /*bGlow*/ true);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
}

void AGBProjectile::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	// Undo the instigator's ignore entry so its list does not grow with every shot.
	AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
	if (IsValid(ProjectileInstigator))
	{
		if (UPrimitiveComponent* InstigatorRoot = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(ProjectileInstigator->GetRootComponent()))
		{
			InstigatorRoot->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(this, false);
		}
	}

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBProjectile::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	if (bFinished)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector Location = GetActorLocation();
	const float Age = GetGameTimeSinceCreation();
	const bool bOutOfRange = Config.MaxRange > 0.f && FVector::Dist(StartLocation, Location) >= Config.MaxRange;
	const bool bTooOld = Config.MaxLifetime > 0.f && Age > Config.MaxLifetime;
	const bool bFuseDone = Config.FuseTime > 0.f && Age >= Config.FuseTime;

	if (bOutOfRange || bTooOld || bFuseDone)
	{
		if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
		{
			Explode(Location);
		}
		Finish(nullptr, Location);
	}
}

bool AGBProjectile::IsValidTarget(const AActor* Other) const
{
	if (!IsValid(Other) || Other == this || Other->IsA<AGBProjectile>())
	{
		return false;
	}

	const AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
	if (Other == ProjectileInstigator)
	{
		return false;
	}

	if (!UGBCombatLibrary::CanDamage(ProjectileInstigator, Other))
	{
		return false;
	}

	for (const TWeakObjectPtr<AActor>& Damaged : DamagedActors)
	{
		if (Damaged.Get() == Other)
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	return true;
}

void AGBProjectile::HitTarget(AActor* Other, const FVector& ImpactPoint)
{
	DamagedActors.Add(Other);

	if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
	{
		Explode(ImpactPoint);
	}
	else
	{
		FGBDamageInfo Info = Config.Damage;
		Info.Causer = this;
		Info.HitLocation = ImpactPoint;
		Info.HitDirection = Movement->Velocity.GetSafeNormal();
		if (const AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = Cast<AGBEnemyCharacter>(Other))
		{
			Info.bHeadshot = Enemy->IsHeadshotLocation(ImpactPoint);
		}
		UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Other, Info);
	}

	// Neutral things with attributes (ice walls, destructibles) stop the shot even when it pierces.
	const bool bHitNeutral = UGBCombatLibrary::GetTeam(Other) == EGBTeam::Neutral;
	if (!Config.bPierce || bHitNeutral)
	{
		Finish(Other, ImpactPoint);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::OnCollisionOverlap(UPrimitiveComponent* OverlappedComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp,
	int32 OtherBodyIndex, bool bFromSweep, const FHitResult& SweepResult)
{
	if (bFinished || !IsValidTarget(OtherActor))
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector ImpactPoint = (bFromSweep && !SweepResult.bStartPenetrating) ? FVector(SweepResult.ImpactPoint) : GetActorLocation();
	HitTarget(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
}

void AGBProjectile::OnCollisionHit(UPrimitiveComponent* HitComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp,
	FVector NormalImpulse, const FHitResult& Hit)
{
	if (bFinished)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector ImpactPoint = Hit.ImpactPoint;

	if (IsValidTarget(OtherActor))
	{
		HitTarget(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
		return;
	}

	// The world: grenades keep bouncing until the fuse ends.
	if (Config.FuseTime > 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
	{
		Explode(ImpactPoint);
	}
	Finish(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
}

void AGBProjectile::Explode(const FVector& Origin)
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info = Config.Damage;
	Info.Causer = this;
	UGBCombatLibrary::DealRadialDamage(this, Origin, Config.ExplosionRadius, Info, TConstArrayView<AActor*>(), Config.bExplosionFalloff);

	// Seen and felt even when the blast hits nothing.
	AGBExplosionEffect::Spawn(GetWorld(), Origin, Config.ExplosionRadius, Config.Color, Config.ExplosionEffectTime);
	if (Config.ExplosionKnockback > 0.f)
	{
		ApplyExplosionKnockback(Origin);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::ApplyExplosionKnockback(const FVector& Origin)
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const float Radius = Config.ExplosionRadius;
	if (!World || Radius <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	FCollisionQueryParams QueryParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBExplosionKnockback), false, this);
	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), QueryParams);

	AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
	if (!IsValid(ProjectileInstigator))
	{
		ProjectileInstigator = nullptr;
	}

	TSet<ACharacter*> Visited;
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		ACharacter* Character = Cast<ACharacter>(Overlap.GetActor());
		if (!IsValid(Character) || Character == ProjectileInstigator || Visited.Contains(Character))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Visited.Add(Character);

		if (!UGBCombatLibrary::CanDamage(ProjectileInstigator, Character))
		{
			continue;
		}

		const FVector TargetLocation = Character->GetActorLocation();
		const float Distance = FVector::Dist(TargetLocation, Origin);
		if (Distance > Radius)
		{
			continue;
		}

		// Same rule as the blast damage: solid geometry between the blast and the target stops the push.
		FCollisionQueryParams TraceParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBKnockbackObstruction), true, this);
		TraceParams.AddIgnoredActor(Character);
		FHitResult Obstruction;
		const FVector TraceStart = Origin + FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBProjectileLocal::KnockbackTraceLiftCm);
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Obstruction, TraceStart, TargetLocation, ECC_Visibility, TraceParams)
			&& Obstruction.bBlockingHit && Obstruction.GetActor() && Obstruction.GetActor() != Character)
		{
			continue;
		}

		const float Strength = Config.ExplosionKnockback * FMath::Clamp(1.f - Distance / Radius, 0.f, 1.f);
		if (Strength <= 0.f)
		{
			continue;
		}

		FVector Direction = (TargetLocation - Origin).GetSafeNormal();
		if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
		{
			// Blast right on the target: push it back along the projectile's flight.
			Direction = Movement ? Movement->Velocity.GetSafeNormal() : FVector::ZeroVector;
		}
		Direction.Z = FMath::Max(Direction.Z, GBProjectileLocal::KnockbackMinLift);
		Direction = Direction.GetSafeNormal();
		Character->LaunchCharacter(Direction * Strength, /*bXYOverride*/ true, /*bZOverride*/ true);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::Finish(AActor* HitActor, const FVector& Location)
{
	if (bFinished)
	{
		return;
	}
	bFinished = true;

	if (Movement)
	{
		Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
	}
	OnImpact.Broadcast(this, HitActor, Location);
	Destroy();
}
#include "Combat/GBExplosionEffect.h"

#include "Components/PointLightComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Engine/Scene.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"

namespace
{
	// Look of the shockwave only (the size and the duration come from the caller's data).
	/** The sphere starts at this fraction of the blast radius. */
	constexpr float ExplosionStartScale = 0.15f;
	/** Brightness of the glow sphere relative to the explosion color. */
	constexpr float ExplosionGlowBoost = 1.5f;
	constexpr float ExplosionFlashCandelas = 800.f;
	/** The flash lights the surroundings beyond the blast itself. */
	constexpr float ExplosionFlashRadiusScale = 2.5f;
	/** Shortest animation, so a 0 in the data cannot divide by zero. */
	constexpr float ExplosionMinDuration = 0.05f;
	/** Extra seconds before the safety life span ends the actor, in case Tick never reaches the end. */
	constexpr float ExplosionLifeSpanMargin = 1.f;
}

AGBExplosionEffect::AGBExplosionEffect()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	USceneComponent* Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	Root->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
	RootComponent = Root;

	Sphere = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Sphere"));
	Sphere->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Sphere->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
	Sphere->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Sphere->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Sphere->SetCastShadow(false);
	Sphere->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);

	Flash = CreateDefaultSubobject<UPointLightComponent>(TEXT("Flash"));
	Flash->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Flash->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
	// Plain defaults here (no render state yet); InitEffect / ApplyAlpha drive the light at runtime.
	Flash->CastShadows = false;
	Flash->IntensityUnits = ELightUnits::Candelas;
	Flash->Intensity = 0.f;
}

AGBExplosionEffect* AGBExplosionEffect::Spawn(UWorld* World, const FVector& Location, float InRadius, const FLinearColor& InColor, float InDuration)
{
	if (!World || InRadius <= 0.f)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
	AGBExplosionEffect* Effect = World->SpawnActor<AGBExplosionEffect>(AGBExplosionEffect::StaticClass(), Location, FRotator::ZeroRotator, Params);
	if (!Effect)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Explosion effect: spawn failed"));
		return nullptr;
	}
	Effect->InitEffect(InRadius, InColor, InDuration);
	return Effect;
}

void AGBExplosionEffect::InitEffect(float InRadius, const FLinearColor& InColor, float InDuration)
{
	Radius = FMath::Max(InRadius, 1.f);
	Color = InColor;
	Duration = FMath::Max(InDuration, ExplosionMinDuration);
	Age = 0.f;

	SphereMaterial = GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Sphere, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(Radius * 2.f), Color, /*bGlow*/ true);
	// SetupShape sized the sphere to the full blast; the animation scales down from there.
	FullScale = Sphere->GetRelativeScale3D();
	Flash->SetLightColor(Color);
	Flash->SetAttenuationRadius(Radius * ExplosionFlashRadiusScale);

	SetLifeSpan(Duration + ExplosionLifeSpanMargin);
	ApplyAlpha(0.f);
}

void AGBExplosionEffect::ApplyAlpha(float Alpha)
{
	const float T = FMath::Clamp(Alpha, 0.f, 1.f);
	const float Remaining = 1.f - T;
	// Fast at first, slowing down at the edge, like a shockwave.
	const float Grow = ExplosionStartScale + (1.f - ExplosionStartScale) * (1.f - Remaining * Remaining);

	Sphere->SetRelativeScale3D(FullScale * Grow);
	// The glow material is additive: fading the color to black fades the sphere out.
	GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(Sphere, Color * (ExplosionGlowBoost * Remaining));
	Flash->SetIntensity(ExplosionFlashCandelas * Remaining * Remaining);
}

void AGBExplosionEffect::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	Age += DeltaSeconds;
	if (Age >= Duration)
	{
		Destroy();
		return;
	}
	ApplyAlpha(Age / Duration);
}
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"

#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/OverlapResult.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "CollisionQueryParams.h"

namespace GBCombatLocal
{
	/** The obstruction trace starts this far above the explosion origin, so the floor under it does not block it. */
	constexpr float RadialTraceLiftCm = 5.f;
}

FGBOnDamageDealt& UGBCombatLibrary::OnDamageDealt()
{
	static FGBOnDamageDealt Event;
	return Event;
}

float UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(AActor* Target, const FGBDamageInfo& Info, bool bAllowFriendlyFire)
{
	if (!IsValid(Target))
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target);
	if (!Attributes)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	AActor* DamageInstigator = Info.Instigator.Get();
	if (!IsValid(DamageInstigator))
	{
		DamageInstigator = nullptr;
	}

	if (!CanDamage(DamageInstigator, Target))
	{
		// Friendly fire explicitly allows same-team hits; anything without attributes or dead stays immune.
		if (!bAllowFriendlyFire || !UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target) || !IsAlive(Target))
		{
			return 0.f;
		}
	}

	FGBDamageInfo FinalInfo = Info;
	FinalInfo.Instigator = DamageInstigator;
	if (DamageInstigator)
	{
		if (const UGBStatsComponent* InstigatorStats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(DamageInstigator))
		{
			FinalInfo.Amount *= InstigatorStats->GetStat(GBStat::DamageDealt, 1.f);
		}
	}

	if (FinalInfo.Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	const float Dealt = Attributes->ApplyDamage(FinalInfo);
	if (Dealt > 0.f)
	{
		OnDamageDealt().Broadcast(Target, FinalInfo, Dealt);
	}

	if (Dealt > 0.f && !FinalInfo.OnHitEffect.IsNone())
	{
		if (UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(Target))
		{
			Effects->ApplyEffect(FinalInfo.OnHitEffect, DamageInstigator, FinalInfo.EffectDurationScale, FinalInfo.EffectStrengthScale);
		}
	}

	return Dealt;
}

int32 UGBCombatLibrary::DealRadialDamage(const UObject* WorldContextObject, const FVector& Origin, float Radius, const FGBDamageInfo& Info,
	TConstArrayView<AActor*> IgnoreActors, bool bLinearFalloff)
{
	if (Radius <= 0.f || !GEngine)
	{
		return 0;
	}

	UWorld* World = GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::LogAndReturnNull);
	if (!World)
	{
		return 0;
	}

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);

	FCollisionQueryParams QueryParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBRadialDamage), false);
	for (AActor* Ignored : IgnoreActors)
	{
		if (Ignored)
		{
			QueryParams.AddIgnoredActor(Ignored);
		}
	}

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), QueryParams);

	AActor* DamageInstigator = Info.Instigator.Get();
	if (!IsValid(DamageInstigator))
	{
		DamageInstigator = nullptr;
	}

	TSet<AActor*> Visited;
	int32 DamagedCount = 0;

	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Target = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (!IsValid(Target) || Visited.Contains(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Visited.Add(Target);

		if (IgnoreActors.Contains(Target) || !UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		if (!CanDamage(DamageInstigator, Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		const FVector TargetLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
		const float Distance = FVector::Dist(TargetLocation, Origin);
		if (Distance > Radius)
		{
			continue;
		}

		// Solid geometry between the blast and the target blocks the damage.
		FCollisionQueryParams TraceParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBRadialObstruction), true);
		TraceParams.AddIgnoredActor(Target);
		for (AActor* Ignored : IgnoreActors)
		{
			if (Ignored)
			{
				TraceParams.AddIgnoredActor(Ignored);
			}
		}
		FHitResult Obstruction;
		const FVector TraceStart = Origin + FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBCombatLocal::RadialTraceLiftCm);
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Obstruction, TraceStart, TargetLocation, ECC_Visibility, TraceParams)
			&& Obstruction.bBlockingHit && Obstruction.GetActor() && Obstruction.GetActor() != Target)
		{
			continue;
		}

		FGBDamageInfo TargetInfo = Info;
		TargetInfo.HitLocation = TargetLocation;
		TargetInfo.HitDirection = (TargetLocation - Origin).GetSafeNormal();
		if (bLinearFalloff)
		{
			TargetInfo.Amount *= FMath::Clamp(1.f - Distance / Radius, 0.f, 1.f);
		}

		if (DealDamage(Target, TargetInfo) > 0.f)
		{
			++DamagedCount;
		}
	}

	return DamagedCount;
}

EGBTeam UGBCombatLibrary::GetTeam(const AActor* Actor)
{
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Actor);
	return Attributes ? Attributes->Team : EGBTeam::Neutral;
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(const AActor* A, const AActor* B)
{
	if (!A || !B)
	{
		return false;
	}
	return AreTeamsHostile(GetTeam(A), GetTeam(B));
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::AreTeamsHostile(EGBTeam A, EGBTeam B)
{
	return (A == EGBTeam::Player && B == EGBTeam::Enemy) || (A == EGBTeam::Enemy && B == EGBTeam::Player);
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(const AActor* Actor)
{
	if (!IsValid(Actor))
	{
		return false;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Actor);
	return !Attributes || !Attributes->IsDead();
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::CanDamage(const AActor* Instigator, const AActor* Target)
{
	if (!IsValid(Target))
	{
		return false;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* TargetAttributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target);
	if (!TargetAttributes || TargetAttributes->IsDead())
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (!IsValid(Instigator))
	{
		return true;
	}
	const EGBTeam InstigatorTeam = GetTeam(Instigator);
	const EGBTeam TargetTeam = TargetAttributes->Team;
	if (InstigatorTeam == EGBTeam::Neutral || TargetTeam == EGBTeam::Neutral)
	{
		return true;
	}
	return AreTeamsHostile(InstigatorTeam, TargetTeam);
}

float UGBCombatLibrary::DistanceFalloffMultiplier(float Distance, float FalloffStart, float FalloffEnd, float NearMult, float FarMult)
{
	if (Distance <= FalloffStart)
	{
		return NearMult;
	}
	if (FalloffEnd <= FalloffStart || Distance >= FalloffEnd)
	{
		return FarMult;
	}
	const float Alpha = (Distance - FalloffStart) / (FalloffEnd - FalloffStart);
	return FMath::Lerp(NearMult, FarMult, Alpha);
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::IsHeadshot(float HitZ, float FeetZ, float BodyHeight, float HeadFraction)
{
	if (HeadFraction <= 0.f || BodyHeight <= 0.f)
	{
		return false;
	}
	const float HeadLineZ = FeetZ + BodyHeight * (1.f - FMath::Min(HeadFraction, 1.f));
	return HitZ >= HeadLineZ;
}

FVector UGBCombatLibrary::ApplySpread(const FVector& Direction, float SpreadHalfAngleDegrees, FRandomStream& Random)
{
	const FVector Dir = Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	if (Dir.IsNearlyZero() || SpreadHalfAngleDegrees <= 0.f)
	{
		return Dir;
	}
	return Random.VRandCone(Dir, FMath::DegreesToRadians(SpreadHalfAngleDegrees));
}

// Named (not anonymous) so unity builds cannot collide with another file's helpers.
namespace GBEnemyCharacterLocal
{
	const FName AttackModeMelee(TEXT("Melee"));
	const FName AttackModeRanged(TEXT("Ranged"));
	const TCHAR* const DefaultEnemyProjectileSprite = TEXT("enemy_bolt");

	// Pure layout / technical constants (not gameplay tuning).
	constexpr float GroundProbeDepth = 1000.f;     // cm below the requested spawn point searched for a floor
	constexpr float SpawnLift = 2.f;               // cm above the floor, so the capsule does not start inside it
	constexpr float ChestHeightFraction = 0.5f;    // of the capsule half height, above the center
	constexpr float SteerSweepRadiusScale = 0.9f;  // the steering sweep capsule is a bit thinner, so a wall it slides along is not "ahead"
	constexpr float StuckSpeedFraction = 0.25f;    // a slowed enemy is not stuck: the stuck distance never exceeds this share of its possible travel
	constexpr int32 MaxSteerCandidatesPerSide = 12;
	constexpr float TelegraphSpriteScale = 1.12f;
	constexpr float TelegraphTintBlend = 0.55f;    // towards white
}

AGBEnemyCharacter::AGBEnemyCharacter()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	Stats = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBStatsComponent>(TEXT("Stats"));
	Attributes = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBAttributeComponent>(TEXT("Attributes"));
	Attributes->Team = EGBTeam::Enemy;
	StatusEffects = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBStatusEffectComponent>(TEXT("StatusEffects"));
	EraComponent = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBEraComponent>(TEXT("Era"));

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(GetCapsuleComponent());
	Sprite->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Sprite->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);

	BodyMesh = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("BodyMesh"));
	BodyMesh->SetupAttachment(GetCapsuleComponent());
	BodyMesh->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	BodyMesh->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
	BodyMesh->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
	BodyMesh->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);

	// Pawn profile ignores Visibility; line-of-sight traces use it, so block it. Guns trace ECC_Camera: keep blocking it too.
	UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
	Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Block);
	Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Visibility, ECR_Block);
	Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Camera, ECR_Block);

	AIControllerClass = AAIController::StaticClass();
	AutoPossessAI = EAutoPossessAI::PlacedInWorldOrSpawned;

	bUseControllerRotationYaw = false;
	bUseControllerRotationPitch = false;
	bUseControllerRotationRoll = false;
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->bOrientRotationToMovement = true;
	}
}

AGBEnemyCharacter* AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(UWorld* World, FName InEnemyId, const FVector& Location, const FRotator& Rotation, EGBEraPresence Presence)
{
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(World);
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindEnemy(InEnemyId) : nullptr;
	if (!Def)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnEnemy: unknown enemy id '%s'"), *InEnemyId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	UClass* Class = AGBEnemyCharacter::StaticClass();
	if (!Def->ActorClass.IsEmpty())
	{
		if (UClass* Loaded = LoadClass<AGBEnemyCharacter>(nullptr, *Def->ActorClass))
		{
			Class = Loaded;
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnEnemy: class '%s' of '%s' not found, using AGBEnemyCharacter"), *Def->ActorClass, *InEnemyId.ToString());
		}
	}

	const AGBEnemyCharacter* CDO = Class->GetDefaultObject<AGBEnemyCharacter>();
	const float DefaultHalfHeight = (CDO && CDO->GetCapsuleComponent()) ? CDO->GetCapsuleComponent()->GetUnscaledCapsuleHalfHeight() : 0.f;
	const float* DefHalfHeight = Def->Stats.Find(GBStat::CapsuleHalfHeight);
	const float HalfHeight = DefHalfHeight ? FMath::Max(*DefHalfHeight, 1.f) : DefaultHalfHeight;

	// Stand on the floor under the requested point, if there is one.
	FVector Ground = Location;
	FHitResult Hit;
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBEnemySpawnGround), false);
	if (World->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, Location + FVector::UpVector * HalfHeight, Location - FVector::UpVector * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::GroundProbeDepth,
		FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		Ground = Hit.ImpactPoint;
	}

	const FTransform SpawnTransform(FRotator(0.f, Rotation.Yaw, 0.f), Ground + FVector::UpVector * (HalfHeight + GBEnemyCharacterLocal::SpawnLift));
	AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBEnemyCharacter>(Class, SpawnTransform, nullptr, nullptr,
		ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AdjustIfPossibleButAlwaysSpawn);
	if (!Enemy)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnEnemy: spawn of '%s' failed"), *InEnemyId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	Enemy->EnemyId = InEnemyId;
	Enemy->InitFromDefinition(*Def);
	Enemy->EraComponent->Presence = Presence;
	Enemy->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);
	return Enemy;
}

const FGBEnemyDefinition* AGBEnemyCharacter::GetDefinition() const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	return Data ? Data->FindEnemy(EnemyId) : nullptr;
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	// Placed in a level by hand (not through SpawnEnemy): read the definition now.
	if (!bInitialized)
	{
		if (const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
		{
			InitFromDefinition(*Def);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: unknown enemy id '%s'"), *GetName(), *EnemyId.ToString());
		}
	}

	EraComponent->OnPresenceChanged.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandlePresenceChanged);
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDataReloaded);
	}

	SteerSide = FMath::RandBool() ? 1 : -1;
	ResetStuck();
}

float AGBEnemyCharacter::GetGameValue(FName Key, float DefaultValue) const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	return Data ? Data->GetGameValue(Key, DefaultValue) : DefaultValue;
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.RemoveAll(this);
	}
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::InitFromDefinition(const FGBEnemyDefinition& Definition)
{
	bInitialized = true;
	bPixelArtLook = Definition.bPixelArt;
	AttackModeName = Definition.AttackMode;

	Stats->SetBaseStats(Definition.Stats);
	Attributes->InitializeFromStats();

	UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
	const float Radius = FMath::Max(Stats->GetStat(GBStat::CapsuleRadius, Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleRadius()), 1.f);
	const float HalfHeight = FMath::Max(Stats->GetStat(GBStat::CapsuleHalfHeight, Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleHalfHeight()), Radius);
	Capsule->SetCapsuleSize(Radius, HalfHeight);

	if (Definition.bPixelArt)
	{
		const FString SpriteId = Definition.Strings.FindRef(GBString::Sprite);
		if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this))
		{
			Library->SetupBillboard(Sprite, FName(*SpriteId), 2.f * HalfHeight);
		}
		SpriteBaseScale = Sprite->GetRelativeScale3D();
		Sprite->SetVisibility(true);
		BodyMesh->SetVisibility(false);
	}
	else
	{
		BodyColor = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Definition.Strings.FindRef(GBString::Color), FLinearColor::Gray);
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(BodyMesh, EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(2.f * Radius, 2.f * Radius, 2.f * HalfHeight), BodyColor);
		BodyMesh->SetVisibility(true);
		Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
	}

	Stats->OnStatsChanged.RemoveAll(this);
	Stats->OnStatsChanged.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::ApplyMovementStats);
	Attributes->OnDeath.RemoveAll(this);
	Attributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDeath);
	Attributes->OnDamaged.RemoveAll(this);
	Attributes->OnDamaged.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDamaged);

	ApplyMovementStats();
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDataReloaded()
{
	if (const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		AttackModeName = Def->AttackMode;
		Stats->SetBaseStats(Def->Stats);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::ApplyMovementStats()
{
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->MaxWalkSpeed = FMath::Max(0.f, Stats->GetStat(GBStat::MoveSpeed, Move->MaxWalkSpeed));
	}
}

AActor* AGBEnemyCharacter::FindTarget() const
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	if (Player && UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Player) && UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(this, Player))
	{
		return Player;
	}
	return nullptr;
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::IsRanged() const
{
	return AttackModeName.Equals(GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeRanged.ToString(), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::SetState(EGBEnemyState NewState)
{
	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup && NewState != EGBEnemyState::Windup)
	{
		SetTelegraph(false);
	}
	State = NewState;
	StateTime = 0.f;
	if (NewState == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
	{
		SetTelegraph(true);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::SetTelegraph(bool bOn)
{
	if (bPixelArtLook)
	{
		Sprite->SetRelativeScale3D(bOn ? SpriteBaseScale * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::TelegraphSpriteScale : SpriteBaseScale);
	}
	else
	{
		const FLinearColor Color = bOn ? BodyColor + (FLinearColor::White - BodyColor) * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::TelegraphTintBlend : BodyColor;
		GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(BodyMesh, Color);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead || !EraComponent->IsPresent())
	{
		return;
	}

	StateTime += DeltaSeconds;
	NavRetryTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, NavRetryTimer - DeltaSeconds);
	SteerSideHoldTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, SteerSideHoldTimer - DeltaSeconds);
	GoAroundTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, GoAroundTimer - DeltaSeconds);
	bWantsToMove = false;

	AActor* Goal = FindTarget();
	Target = Goal;
	if (!Goal)
	{
		if (State != EGBEnemyState::Idle)
		{
			StopMoving();
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Idle);
		}
		bProvoked = false;
		bRepositioning = false;
		ResetStuck();
		return;
	}

	const float AggroRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AggroRange, 0.f);
	const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);
	const bool bCanAttack = AttackRange > 0.f && Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackDamage, 0.f) > 0.f;
	const float Distance = FVector::Dist(GetActorLocation(), Goal->GetActorLocation());

	switch (State)
	{
	case EGBEnemyState::Idle:
		// AggroRange <= 0: never acts (training dummy), even when hit.
		if (AggroRange > 0.f && (bProvoked || Distance <= AggroRange))
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Chase:
		// Line of sight for melee too: an orc on the other side of a low wall keeps going around it instead of swinging at stone.
		if (bCanAttack && Distance <= AttackRange && HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			StopMoving();
			FaceTowards(Goal);
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Windup);
		}
		else
		{
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Windup:
		// A shot needs a clear line: the player stepped behind cover, so reposition instead of shooting the wall.
		if (IsRanged() && !HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
			break;
		}
		FaceTowards(Goal);
		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackWindup, 0.f))
		{
			PerformAttack();
			if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
			{
				SetState(EGBEnemyState::Recover);
			}
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Recover:
		// Ranged enemies do not wait out the interval behind a wall: they move until they see the player again.
		if (IsRanged() && !HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
			bRepositioning = true;
		}
		else
		{
			if (bRepositioning)
			{
				StopMoving();
				bRepositioning = false;
			}
			FaceTowards(Goal);
		}
		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackInterval, 0.f))
		{
			bRepositioning = false;
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
		break;

	default:
		break;
	}

	UpdateStuck(DeltaSeconds);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::MoveTowards(AActor* Goal, float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (!Goal || GetCharacterMovement()->MaxWalkSpeed <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	bWantsToMove = true;

	bool bPathing = false;
	AAIController* AI = Cast<AAIController>(GetController());
	if (AI && NavRetryTimer <= 0.f)
	{
		const UNavigationSystemV1* Nav = UNavigationSystemV1::GetCurrent(GetWorld());
		if (Nav && Nav->GetDefaultNavDataInstance() != nullptr)
		{
			const UPathFollowingComponent* PathFollowing = AI->GetPathFollowingComponent();
			if (AI->GetMoveStatus() != EPathFollowingStatus::Idle && PathFollowing && PathFollowing->GetMoveGoal() == Goal)
			{
				bPathing = true;
			}
			else
			{
				// Melee stops a bit inside its reach; ranged keeps closing in until it has line of sight.
				const float Acceptance = IsRanged() ? 0.f : Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f) * 0.5f;
				const EPathFollowingRequestResult::Type Result = AI->MoveToActor(Goal, Acceptance, true, true, false);
				bPathing = Result == EPathFollowingRequestResult::RequestSuccessful;
				if (Result == EPathFollowingRequestResult::Failed)
				{
					NavRetryTimer = GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::NavRetryDelay, 1.f);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	if (!bPathing)
	{
		if (AI && AI->GetMoveStatus() != EPathFollowingStatus::Idle)
		{
			AI->StopMovement();
		}
		SteerTowards(Goal);
	}
	bSteering = !bPathing;
	if (bPathing)
	{
		NavMoveTime += DeltaSeconds;
	}
	else
	{
		SteerMoveTime += DeltaSeconds;
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::SteerTowards(const AActor* Goal)
{
	const FVector ToGoal = (Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation()).GetSafeNormal2D();
	if (ToGoal.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Feeler = FMath::Max(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerFeelerLength, 150.f), 1.f);
	const float AngleStep = FMath::Clamp(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerAngleStep, 25.f), 1.f, 180.f);
	const int32 PerSide = FMath::Clamp(FMath::RoundToInt(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerCandidatesPerSide, 6.f)), 1, GBEnemyCharacterLocal::MaxSteerCandidatesPerSide);

	TArray<bool, TInlineAllocator<2 * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::MaxSteerCandidatesPerSide + 1>> Blocked;
	Blocked.SetNum(2 * PerSide + 1);
	for (int32 Offset = -PerSide; Offset <= PerSide; ++Offset)
	{
		Blocked[Offset + PerSide] = IsDirectionBlocked(ToGoal.RotateAngleAxis(Offset * AngleStep, FVector::UpVector), Feeler, Goal);
	}

	const EGBSteerMode Mode = GoAroundTimer > 0.f ? EGBSteerMode::GoAround
		: (SteerSideHoldTimer > 0.f ? EGBSteerMode::HoldSide : EGBSteerMode::Free);
	const TOptional<int32> Choice = GBSteering::ChooseOffset(Blocked, Mode, SteerSide);
	if (!Choice.IsSet())
	{
		// Boxed in on every side but the back: step back; the stuck check then commits to a side.
		AddMovementInput(-ToGoal, 1.f);
		return;
	}
	if (Choice.GetValue() != 0)
	{
		SteerSide = Choice.GetValue() > 0 ? 1 : -1;
		SteerSideHoldTimer = FMath::Max(SteerSideHoldTimer, GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerSideHoldTime, 1.f));
	}
	AddMovementInput(ToGoal.RotateAngleAxis(Choice.GetValue() * AngleStep, FVector::UpVector), 1.f);
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::IsDirectionBlocked(const FVector& Direction, float Distance, const AActor* Goal) const
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
	const UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement();
	if (!World || !Capsule || !Move)
	{
		return false;
	}

	// Bottom lifted by the step height (the character walks over anything lower), top unchanged, a bit thinner.
	const float StepHeight = FMath::Max(Move->MaxStepHeight, 0.f);
	const float Radius = Capsule->GetScaledCapsuleRadius() * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::SteerSweepRadiusScale;
	const float HalfHeight = FMath::Max(Capsule->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight() - StepHeight * 0.5f, Radius);
	const FVector Start = GetActorLocation() + FVector::UpVector * (StepHeight * 0.5f);

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBEnemySteer), false, this);
	if (Goal)
	{
		Params.AddIgnoredActor(Goal);
	}
	// Other enemies are not obstacles to plan around: they move, and the capsules push each other aside.
	FCollisionResponseParams Response;
	Response.CollisionResponse.SetResponse(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Ignore);

	FHitResult Hit;
	if (!World->SweepSingleByChannel(Hit, Start, Start + Direction * Distance, FQuat::Identity, ECC_Pawn,
		FCollisionShape::MakeCapsule(Radius, HalfHeight), Params, Response))
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (Hit.bStartPenetrating)
	{
		// Already touching something: only directions into it are blocked.
		return FVector::DotProduct(Hit.Normal, Direction) < 0.f;
	}
	// A ramp or a slope it can walk up is not an obstacle.
	return Hit.ImpactNormal.Z < Move->GetWalkableFloorZ();
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::ResetStuck()
{
	StuckTimer = 0.f;
	StuckOrigin = GetActorLocation();
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::UpdateStuck(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (!bWantsToMove)
	{
		ResetStuck();
		return;
	}

	StuckTimer += DeltaSeconds;
	const float Window = FMath::Max(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::StuckTime, 0.8f), 0.1f);
	if (StuckTimer < Window)
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Moved = FVector::Dist2D(GetActorLocation(), StuckOrigin);
	const float Speed = GetCharacterMovement() ? GetCharacterMovement()->MaxWalkSpeed : 0.f;
	const float Threshold = FMath::Min(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::StuckDistance, 50.f), Speed * Window * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::StuckSpeedFraction);
	ResetStuck();
	if (Moved >= Threshold)
	{
		return;
	}

	++StuckCount;
	const float GoAround = FMath::Max(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::GoAroundTime, 1.5f), 0.f);
	if (!bSteering)
	{
		// The navmesh path is not getting anywhere (snagged on a corner, the mesh still building): steer for a while.
		NavRetryTimer = FMath::Max(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::NavRetryDelay, 1.f), GoAround);
		if (AAIController* AI = Cast<AAIController>(GetController()))
		{
			AI->StopMovement();
		}
	}
	else if (GoAroundTimer > 0.f)
	{
		// Already going around this way and still stuck (a dead end): try the other side.
		SteerSide = -SteerSide;
	}
	GoAroundTimer = GoAround;
	SteerSideHoldTimer = GoAround;
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Verbose, TEXT("%s stuck (moved %.0f cm): going around on side %d"), *GetName(), Moved, SteerSide);
}

FString AGBEnemyCharacter::DescribeMovement() const
{
	return FString::Printf(TEXT("navTime=%.1f steerTime=%.1f stuckEvents=%d side=%d goAround=%.1f state=%d"),
		NavMoveTime, SteerMoveTime, StuckCount, SteerSide, GoAroundTimer, static_cast<int32>(State));
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::FellOutOfWorld(const UDamageType& DamageType)
{
	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		Super::FellOutOfWorld(DamageType);
		return;
	}

	// Die like any other death so the encounter counts it; no drops down there.
	bFellOutOfWorld = true;
	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = Attributes->GetMaxHealth() + Attributes->GetHealth() + 1.f;
	Info.SourceId = FName(TEXT("FellOutOfWorld"));
	Attributes->SetInvulnerable(false);
	Attributes->ApplyDamage(Info);
	if (State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		// The hit was cancelled (a shield, DamageTaken 0): it still cannot come back from below the world.
		HandleDeath(Info);
	}
	SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::StopMoving()
{
	if (AController* C = GetController())
	{
		C->StopMovement();
	}
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->StopMovementImmediately();
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::FaceTowards(const AActor* Goal)
{
	if (!Goal)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector Direction = (Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation()).GetSafeNormal2D();
	if (!Direction.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		SetActorRotation(FRotator(0.f, Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f));
	}
}

FVector AGBEnemyCharacter::GetChestLocation() const
{
	return GetActorLocation() + FVector::UpVector * (GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight() * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::ChestHeightFraction);
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::HasLineOfSight(const AActor* Goal) const
{
	if (!Goal || !GetWorld())
	{
		return false;
	}
	FHitResult Hit;
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBEnemyLineOfSight), false, this);
	if (!GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, GetChestLocation(), Goal->GetActorLocation(), ECC_Visibility, Params))
	{
		return true;
	}
	// Allies in the way do not block: enemy shots ignore the enemy team.
	const AActor* HitActor = Hit.GetActor();
	return HitActor == Goal || (HitActor && !UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(this, HitActor) && UGBCombatLibrary::GetTeam(HitActor) == EGBTeam::Enemy);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::PerformAttack()
{
	AActor* Goal = Target.Get();
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	if (!Goal || !Def || !GetWorld())
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Damage = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackDamage, 0.f);
	const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);

	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = Damage;
	Info.Element = Def->Element;
	Info.Instigator = this;
	Info.Causer = this;
	Info.SourceId = EnemyId;
	Info.OnHitEffect = FName(*Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::OnHitEffect));

	const FName Mode(*Def->AttackMode);
	if (Mode == GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeMelee)
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const float Tolerance = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::MeleeReachTolerance, 1.2f) : 1.2f;
		const FVector ToGoal = Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation();
		if (ToGoal.Size() <= AttackRange * Tolerance && HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			Info.HitLocation = Goal->GetActorLocation();
			Info.HitDirection = ToGoal.GetSafeNormal();
			UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Goal, Info);
		}
	}
	else if (Mode == GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeRanged)
	{
		FGBProjectileConfig Config;
		Config.Damage = Info;
		Config.Damage.Causer = nullptr;
		Config.Speed = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, Config.Speed);
		Config.Radius = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, Config.Radius);
		Config.MaxRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AggroRange, 0.f) * 1.5f;
		Config.bPixelArt = Def->bPixelArt;
		if (Def->bPixelArt)
		{
			const FString SpriteId = Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::ProjectileSprite);
			Config.SpriteId = FName(SpriteId.IsEmpty() ? GBEnemyCharacterLocal::DefaultEnemyProjectileSprite : *SpriteId);
		}
		else
		{
			Config.Color = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Def->Strings.FindRef(GBEnemyKey::ProjectileColor),
				GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Def->Element)));
		}

		const FVector Forward = GetActorForwardVector();
		const FVector Origin = GetChestLocation() + Forward * (GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleRadius() + Config.Radius);
		FVector Direction = Goal->GetActorLocation() - Origin;
		if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
		{
			Direction = Forward;
		}
		AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(GetWorld(), Config, Origin, Direction, this);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: unknown AttackMode '%s'"), *EnemyId.ToString(), *Def->AttackMode);
	}
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::IsHeadshotLocation(const FVector& HitLocation) const
{
	const float HalfHeight = GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight();
	const float FeetZ = GetActorLocation().Z - HalfHeight;
	return UGBCombatLibrary::IsHeadshot(HitLocation.Z, FeetZ, 2.f * HalfHeight, Stats->GetStat(GBStat::HeadFraction, 0.f));
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDamaged(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage)
{
	if (State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		bProvoked = true;
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandlePresenceChanged()
{
	ResetStuck();
	if (!EraComponent->IsPresent() && State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		StopMoving();
		bRepositioning = false;
		if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		return;
	}
	SetState(EGBEnemyState::Dead);

	StopMoving();
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->DisableMovement();
	}
	DetachFromControllerPendingDestroy();
	SetActorEnableCollision(false);

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	if (Def && !bFellOutOfWorld)
	{
		AGBPickup::SpawnDrops(World, Def->Drops, GetActorLocation());
	}

	OnDied.Broadcast(this);

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const float CorpseTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::CorpseTime, 1.5f) : 1.5f;
	// SetLifeSpan(0) would mean "forever"; keep a tiny minimum instead of destroying inside the damage call.
	SetLifeSpan(FMath::Max(CorpseTime, 0.05f));
}

#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"

#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Engine/StaticMesh.h"
#include "Engine/Texture2D.h"
#include "Materials/MaterialInstanceDynamic.h"
#include "Materials/MaterialInterface.h"
#include "UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h"

namespace GBPlaceholderPrivate
{
	const FName ColorParam(TEXT("Color"));

	// Parameters of M_PixelSurface (Tools/Jam/make_materials.py builds it; keep the names in sync).
	const FName TextureParam(TEXT("Texture"));
	const FName TintParam(TEXT("Tint"));
	const FName TileSizeParam(TEXT("TileSize"));
	const FName GlowParam(TEXT("Glow"));
	const TCHAR* const PixelSurfacePath = TEXT("/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelSurface.M_PixelSurface");

	/** Smallest tile accepted from data, so a 0 in the JSON cannot divide by zero in the shader. */
	constexpr float MinTileSizeCm = 1.f;

	/** M_PixelSurface, or null when the asset was not generated (then the caller falls back to SetupShape). */
	UMaterialInterface* GetPixelSurfaceMaterial()
	{
		static TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface> Cached;
		static bool bKnownMissing = false;
		if (!Cached.IsValid() && !bKnownMissing)
		{
			Cached = LoadObject<UMaterialInterface>(nullptr, PixelSurfacePath, FStringView(), LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
			if (!Cached.IsValid())
			{
				// Logged once: run Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1 to create it.
				bKnownMissing = true;
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: material %s is missing (run Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1); textured shapes use plain tinted shapes."), PixelSurfacePath);
			}
		}
		return Cached.Get();
	}

	// The engine shapes are 100 cm across.
	constexpr float ShapeSizeCm = 100.f;

	UMaterialInterface* GetMaterial(bool bGlow)
	{
		static TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface> LitMaterial;
		static TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface> GlowMaterial;

		TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface>& Cached = bGlow ? GlowMaterial : LitMaterial;
		if (!Cached.IsValid())
		{
			const TCHAR* Path = bGlow
				? TEXT("/Engine/EngineMaterials/EmissiveMeshMaterial.EmissiveMeshMaterial")
				: TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/BasicShapeMaterial.BasicShapeMaterial");
			Cached = LoadObject<UMaterialInterface>(nullptr, Path);
			if (!Cached.IsValid())
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: cannot load material %s"), Path);
			}
		}
		return Cached.Get();
	}
}

UStaticMesh* GBPlaceholder::GetShapeMesh(EGBShape Shape)
{
	static TWeakObjectPtr<UStaticMesh> Cache[5];

	const int32 Index = FMath::Clamp(static_cast<int32>(Shape), 0, 4);
	if (!Cache[Index].IsValid())
	{
		static const TCHAR* const Paths[5] =
		{
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cube.Cube"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Sphere.Sphere"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cylinder.Cylinder"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cone.Cone"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Plane.Plane"),
		};
		Cache[Index] = LoadObject<UStaticMesh>(nullptr, Paths[Index]);
		if (!Cache[Index].IsValid())
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: cannot load mesh %s"), Paths[Index]);
		}
	}
	return Cache[Index].Get();
}

UMaterialInstanceDynamic* GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(UStaticMeshComponent* Component, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& SizeCm, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bGlow)
{
	if (!Component)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	Component->SetStaticMesh(GetShapeMesh(Shape));
	Component->SetRelativeScale3D(SizeCm / GBPlaceholderPrivate::ShapeSizeCm);

	UMaterialInterface* BaseMaterial = GBPlaceholderPrivate::GetMaterial(bGlow);
	if (!BaseMaterial)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	UMaterialInstanceDynamic* Material = UMaterialInstanceDynamic::Create(BaseMaterial, Component);
	Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::ColorParam, Color);
	Component->SetMaterial(0, Material);
	return Material;
}

UMaterialInstanceDynamic* GBPlaceholder::SetupTexturedShape(UStaticMeshComponent* Component, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& SizeCm, FName TextureId,
	float TileSizeCm, const FLinearColor& Tint, bool bGlow)
{
	if (!Component)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	UMaterialInterface* BaseMaterial = GBPlaceholderPrivate::GetPixelSurfaceMaterial();
	if (!BaseMaterial)
	{
		return SetupShape(Component, Shape, SizeCm, Tint);
	}

	Component->SetStaticMesh(GetShapeMesh(Shape));
	Component->SetRelativeScale3D(SizeCm / GBPlaceholderPrivate::ShapeSizeCm);

	UMaterialInstanceDynamic* Material = UMaterialInstanceDynamic::Create(BaseMaterial, Component);
	if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(Component))
	{
		Material->SetTextureParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::TextureParam, Library->GetTileTexture(TextureId));
	}
	else
	{
		// Not in a game world yet (the library lives on the game instance): the material's default texture shows.
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: no pixel sprite library for '%s' on %s; using the default texture."),
			*TextureId.ToString(), *GetNameSafe(Component->GetOwner()));
	}
	Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::TintParam, Tint);
	Material->SetScalarParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::TileSizeParam, FMath::Max(TileSizeCm, GBPlaceholderPrivate::MinTileSizeCm));
	Material->SetScalarParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::GlowParam, bGlow ? 1.f : 0.f);
	Component->SetMaterial(0, Material);
	return Material;
}

void GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(UStaticMeshComponent* Component, const FLinearColor& Color)
{
	if (!Component)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* Material = Cast<UMaterialInstanceDynamic>(Component->GetMaterial(0)))
	{
		// "Color" on SetupShape materials, "Tint" on SetupTexturedShape ones; the other name is ignored.
		Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::ColorParam, Color);
		Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::TintParam, Color);
	}
}

FLinearColor GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(uint8 ElementAsByte)
{
	switch (static_cast<EGBElement>(ElementAsByte))
	{
	case EGBElement::Physical:  return FLinearColor(0.55f, 0.55f, 0.55f);
	case EGBElement::Arcane:    return FLinearColor(0.55f, 0.20f, 1.00f);
	case EGBElement::Fire:      return FLinearColor(1.00f, 0.40f, 0.05f);
	case EGBElement::Lightning: return FLinearColor(1.00f, 0.90f, 0.15f);
	case EGBElement::Ice:       return FLinearColor(0.20f, 0.90f, 1.00f);
	case EGBElement::Air:       return FLinearColor(0.65f, 1.00f, 0.70f);
	default:                    return FLinearColor::White;
	}
}
#include "Spells/GBSpell_Wall.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Components/BoxComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Engine/CollisionProfile.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "TimerManager.h"

namespace
{
	// Visual and geometric limits only.
	constexpr int32 MaxWallSprites = 8;
	/** Pawns pressed against a blocking wall still count as touching it for damage ticks. */
	constexpr float WallTouchMarginCm = 15.f;
	constexpr float WallGroundSearchExtraCm = 2000.f;
}

AGBSpell_Wall::AGBSpell_Wall()
{
	Box = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBoxComponent>(TEXT("Box"));
	Box->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Box->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Box->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);

	Visual = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Visual"));
	Visual->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Visual->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Visual->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Visual->SetCastShadow(false);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::StartSpell()
{
	const float Distance = ReadParam(GBSpellParam::Distance, 300.f);
	const float Width = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Width, 300.f));
	const float Height = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Height, 200.f));
	const float Thickness = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Thickness, 50.f));
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 5.f);
	const bool bBlocking = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::Blocking, 0.f) > 0.5f;

	// Stand on the ground in front of the caster, facing the caster's yaw.
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	const FVector CasterLocation = Caster ? Caster->GetActorLocation() : SpellCast.Origin;
	const FRotator WallRotation(0.f, SpellCast.Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f);
	const FVector Point = CasterLocation + WallRotation.Vector() * Distance;
	FVector Ground;
	if (!FindGround(Point + FVector::UpVector * Height, Ground, Height + WallGroundSearchExtraCm))
	{
		Ground = Point;
		Ground.Z = CasterLocation.Z - (Caster ? Caster->GetSimpleCollisionHalfHeight() : 0.f);
	}
	SetActorLocationAndRotation(Ground + FVector::UpVector * (Height * 0.5f), WallRotation, false, nullptr, ETeleportType::TeleportPhysics);

	const FVector Size(Thickness, Width, Height);
	Box->SetBoxExtent(Size * 0.5f);

	const FString TextureId = SpellCast.GetString(GBSpellString::WallTexture);
	if (!TextureId.IsEmpty())
	{
		// A structure (ice wall): one solid pixel-textured box, no camera-facing sprites (GDD 3).
		const float TileSize = ReadParam(GBSpellParam::WallTileSize, 100.f);
		const bool bGlow = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::WallGlow, 0.f) > 0.5f;
		GBPlaceholder::SetupTexturedShape(Visual, EGBShape::Cube, Size, FName(*TextureId), TileSize, FLinearColor::White, bGlow);
		Visual->SetCastShadow(true);
	}
	else
	{
		// An effect (fire wall): a glowing box plus a row of camera-facing sprites (pixel-art flames).
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Visual, EGBShape::Cube, Size, GetSpellColor(), true);
	}

	if (TextureId.IsEmpty() && !GetSpriteId().IsNone())
	{
		const float SpriteHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, Height));
		const int32 Count = FMath::Clamp(FMath::RoundToInt(Width / SpriteHeight), 1, MaxWallSprites);
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count; ++Index)
		{
			UBillboardComponent* Sprite = NewObject<UBillboardComponent>(this);
			Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);
			Sprite->RegisterComponent();
			AddInstanceComponent(Sprite);
			Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, -Width * 0.5f + (Index + 0.5f) * Width / Count, 0.f));
			SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, Height);
			Sprites.Add(Sprite);
		}
	}

	if (bBlocking)
	{
		Box->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::BlockAllDynamic_ProfileName);
		// Projectiles only overlap WorldDynamic: without this they pass through the wall untouched.
		Box->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
		SetupHealth(ReadParam(GBStat::Health, 100.f));
	}

	const float DamagePerSecond = SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 0.f);
	if (DamagePerSecond > 0.f)
	{
		const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
		DamagePerTick = DamagePerSecond * Interval;
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Wall::DamageTick, Interval, true);
	}

	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::SetupHealth(float Health)
{
	UGBStatsComponent* WallStats = NewObject<UGBStatsComponent>(this, TEXT("WallStats"));
	WallStats->RegisterComponent();
	AddInstanceComponent(WallStats);
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth, FMath::Max(1.f, Health));
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::DamageTaken, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamageTaken, 1.f));
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::HealthRegen, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::HealthRegen, 0.f));
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::MaxMana, 0.f);

	UGBAttributeComponent* WallAttributes = NewObject<UGBAttributeComponent>(this, TEXT("WallAttributes"));
	// Neutral: nobody targets the wall on purpose, but shots and enemies that hit it wear it down.
	WallAttributes->Team = EGBTeam::Neutral;
	WallAttributes->RegisterComponent();
	AddInstanceComponent(WallAttributes);
	WallAttributes->InitializeFromStats();
	WallAttributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &AGBSpell_Wall::HandleWallDeath);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::HandleWallDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	// Not inside the damage call that killed it.
	EndSpellAfter(0.f);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::DamageTick()
{
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	const FVector Extent = Box->GetUnscaledBoxExtent() + FVector(WallTouchMarginCm);
	GatherHostilesInBox(GetActorLocation(), GetActorQuat(), Extent, Targets);
	for (AActor* Target : Targets)
	{
		DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
	}
}
#include "Spells/GBSpell_Projectile.h"
#include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"

namespace
{
	// Extra seconds of life on top of Range / Speed, so a projectile is never removed before reaching its range.
	constexpr float ProjectileSpellLifetimeMargin = 1.f;
}

void AGBSpell_Projectile::StartSpell()
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		EndSpell();
		return;
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(GBStat::Damage, this);
	Config.Speed = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Speed, 3000.f));
	Config.Radius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 12.f));
	Config.MaxRange = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 5000.f));
	Config.ExplosionRadius = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionRadius, 0.f));
	Config.bExplosionFalloff = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionFalloff, 1.f) > 0.5f;
	Config.ExplosionKnockback = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionKnockback, Config.ExplosionKnockback));
	Config.ExplosionEffectTime = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionEffectTime, Config.ExplosionEffectTime));
	Config.GravityScale = SpellCast.Get(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
	Config.bPierce = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::Pierce, 0.f) > 0.5f;
	Config.bPixelArt = true;
	Config.SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	Config.Color = GetSpellColor();
	Config.VisualScale = FMath::Max(0.01f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::VisualScale, 1.f));
	if (Config.MaxRange > 0.f)
	{
		Config.MaxLifetime = FMath::Max(Config.MaxLifetime, Config.MaxRange / Config.Speed + ProjectileSpellLifetimeMargin);
	}

	// Aim at what the crosshair is on, then fire from the hand so the shot does not start inside the camera.
	const FVector AimDir = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal().IsNearlyZero() ? GetActorForwardVector() : SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	const float TraceLength = Config.MaxRange > 0.f ? Config.MaxRange : Config.Speed * Config.MaxLifetime;
	FVector Target = SpellCast.Origin + AimDir * TraceLength;
	{
		FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellProjectileAim), false, this);
		if (AActor* Caster = GetCaster())
		{
			Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
		}
		FHitResult Hit;
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, SpellCast.Origin, Target, ECC_Visibility, Params))
		{
			Target = Hit.ImpactPoint;
		}
	}

	FVector Muzzle = SpellCast.Origin;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	}
	FVector ShotDir = (Target - Muzzle).GetSafeNormal();
	if (ShotDir.IsNearlyZero() || FVector::DotProduct(ShotDir, AimDir) <= 0.f)
	{
		// The target is between the camera and the hand (point-blank wall): just fire along the aim.
		ShotDir = AimDir;
	}

	if (!AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Muzzle, ShotDir, GetCaster()))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s': the projectile could not be spawned."), *SpellCast.SpellId.ToString());
	}
	EndSpell();
}

// Floating damage numbers of AGBHUD: one per hit on anything but the local player, rising and fading out.
#include "UI/GBHUD.h"

#include "UI/GBHUDShared.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"

#include "Camera/PlayerCameraManager.h"
#include "Engine/Canvas.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "GameFramework/PlayerController.h"

namespace GBDamageNumberLocal
{
	/** Pure presentation: length and size of the "pop" of a big hit. */
	constexpr float PopTime = 0.15f;
	constexpr float PopExtra = 0.35f;

	struct FTuning
	{
		float Lifetime = 0.9f;
		float RiseSpeed = 90.f;
		float DriftSpeed = 40.f;
		float TextSize = 22.f;
		float BigScale = 1.7f;
		float BigHealthFraction = 0.25f;
		float FadeStart = 0.6f;
		int32 MaxAlive = 40;
		float MergeWindow = 0.08f;
	};

	FTuning ReadTuning(const UObject* Context)
	{
		FTuning T;
		if (const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(Context))
		{
			T.Lifetime = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberLifetime, T.Lifetime);
			T.RiseSpeed = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberRiseSpeed, T.RiseSpeed);
			T.DriftSpeed = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberDriftSpeed, T.DriftSpeed);
			T.TextSize = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberTextSize, T.TextSize);
			T.BigScale = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberBigScale, T.BigScale);
			T.BigHealthFraction = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberBigHealthFraction, T.BigHealthFraction);
			T.FadeStart = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberFadeStart, T.FadeStart);
			T.MaxAlive = FMath::RoundToInt(Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberMaxAlive, (float)T.MaxAlive));
			T.MergeWindow = Data->GetGameValue(GBHudKey::DamageNumberMergeWindow, T.MergeWindow);
		}
		T.Lifetime = FMath::Max(0.05f, T.Lifetime);
		T.TextSize = FMath::Max(4.f, T.TextSize);
		T.BigScale = FMath::Max(1.f, T.BigScale);
		T.FadeStart = FMath::Clamp(T.FadeStart, 0.f, 0.99f);
		T.MaxAlive = FMath::Max(1, T.MaxAlive);
		T.MergeWindow = FMath::Max(0.f, T.MergeWindow);
		return T;
	}

	bool IsBigHit(float Amount, float MaxHealth, float Fraction)
	{
		return MaxHealth > 0.f && Fraction > 0.f && Amount >= Fraction * MaxHealth;
	}
}

double AGBHUD::GetWorldTime() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? World->GetTimeSeconds() : 0.0;
}

void AGBHUD::HandleDamageDealt(AActor* Target, const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float DamageDealt)
{
	using namespace GBDamageNumberLocal;
	// The delegate is global: ignore other worlds and the local player (the red flash covers the player).
	if (DamageDealt <= 0.f || (Target && Target->GetWorld() != GetWorld()) || (Target && Target == GetOwningPawn()))
	{
		return;
	}
	if (!Target && Info.HitLocation.IsZero())
	{
		return;
	}
	const FTuning Tuning = ReadTuning(this);
	const double Now = GetWorldTime();
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Target ? UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target) : nullptr;
	const float MaxHealth = Attributes ? Attributes->GetMaxHealth() : 0.f;

	// Hits on the same target in a very short window (pellets, ticks) become one number.
	if (Target && Tuning.MergeWindow > 0.f)
	{
		for (FDamageNumber& Number : DamageNumbers)
		{
			if (Number.Target.Get() == Target && Now - Number.StartTime <= Tuning.MergeWindow)
			{
				Number.Amount += DamageDealt;
				Number.bBig = Number.bBig || Info.bHeadshot || IsBigHit(Number.Amount, Number.TargetMaxHealth, Tuning.BigHealthFraction);
				return;
			}
		}
	}

	FDamageNumber Number;
	Number.Target = Target;
	if (!Info.HitLocation.IsZero())
	{
		Number.WorldPosition = Info.HitLocation;
	}
	else
	{
		FVector Origin;
		FVector Extent;
		Target->GetActorBounds(true, Origin, Extent);
		Number.WorldPosition = Origin + FVector(0.f, 0.f, Extent.Z);
	}
	Number.Drift = FMath::FRandRange(-1.f, 1.f);
	Number.Amount = DamageDealt;
	Number.TargetMaxHealth = MaxHealth;
	Number.Element = Info.Element;
	Number.bBig = Info.bHeadshot || IsBigHit(DamageDealt, MaxHealth, Tuning.BigHealthFraction);
	Number.StartTime = Now;
	DamageNumbers.Add(Number);
	while (DamageNumbers.Num() > Tuning.MaxAlive)
	{
		DamageNumbers.RemoveAt(0);
	}
}

void AGBHUD::DrawDamageNumbers()
{
	using namespace GBDamageNumberLocal;
	if (DamageNumbers.Num() == 0 || !Canvas)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FTuning Tuning = ReadTuning(this);
	const double Now = GetWorldTime();
	DamageNumbers.RemoveAll([Now, &Tuning](const FDamageNumber& Number) { return Now - Number.StartTime >= Tuning.Lifetime || Now < Number.StartTime; });

	const float S = UIScale();
	const APlayerCameraManager* Camera = PlayerOwner ? PlayerOwner->PlayerCameraManager.Get() : nullptr;
	const FVector CameraLocation = Camera ? Camera->GetCameraLocation() : FVector::ZeroVector;
	const FVector CameraForward = Camera ? Camera->GetCameraRotation().Vector() : FVector::ForwardVector;
	const float Outline = FMath::Max(1.f, 2.f * S);

	for (const FDamageNumber& Number : DamageNumbers)
	{
		if (Camera && FVector::DotProduct(Number.WorldPosition - CameraLocation, CameraForward) <= 0.f)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FVector Screen = Canvas->Project(Number.WorldPosition, true);
		if (Screen.Z <= 0.f)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const float Age = (float)(Now - Number.StartTime);
		const float Life01 = FMath::Clamp(Age / Tuning.Lifetime, 0.f, 1.f);
		const float Alpha = Life01 <= Tuning.FadeStart ? 1.f : 1.f - (Life01 - Tuning.FadeStart) / (1.f - Tuning.FadeStart);

		float SizePx = Tuning.TextSize;
		const FLinearColor Base = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor((uint8)Number.Element);
		FLinearColor Color = FMath::Lerp(Base, FLinearColor::White, 0.25f);
		if (Number.bBig)
		{
			// Scale up then settle, and a brighter, warmer color.
			const float Pop01 = FMath::Clamp(Age / PopTime, 0.f, 1.f);
			SizePx *= Tuning.BigScale * (1.f + PopExtra * FMath::Sin(Pop01 * UE_PI));
			Color = FMath::Lerp(Base, FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 0.6f, 1.f), 0.6f);
		}
		Color.A = Alpha;

		const float X = Screen.X + Number.Drift * Tuning.DriftSpeed * S * Age;
		const FString Text = FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(Number.Amount)));
		const FVector2D Size = MeasureLabel(Text, SizePx, true);
		const float Y = Screen.Y - Tuning.RiseSpeed * S * Age - Size.Y * 0.5f;

		// Dark outline so the number reads on any background.
		const FLinearColor Dark(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.85f * Alpha);
		DrawLabel(Text, Dark, X - Outline, Y, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
		DrawLabel(Text, Dark, X + Outline, Y, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
		DrawLabel(Text, Dark, X, Y - Outline, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
		DrawLabel(Text, Dark, X, Y + Outline, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
		DrawLabel(Text, Color, X, Y, SizePx, 0.5f, false, true);
	}
}

+float AGBHUD::DrawRaisedShield(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, float PixelSize, const FVector2D& Bob)
