EGBMenu AGBHUD::GetOpenMenu() const
{
	const AGBPlayerController* PC = GetGBController();
	return PC ? PC->GetOpenMenu() : EGBMenu::None;
}

void AGBHUD::OnMenuOpened(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	MenuState = FMenuState();
	ResetMenuMouse();
	if (Menu == EGBMenu::SkillTrees)
	{
		const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
		MenuState.SkillCursor = Nodes.Num() > 0 ? Nodes[0].Id : NAME_None;
	}
}

void AGBHUD::OnMenuClosed(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	MenuState = FMenuState();
	ResetMenuMouse();
}

void AGBHUD::SetInventoryPage(int32 NewPage)
{
	const FName KeepSkill = MenuState.SkillCursor;
	MenuState = FMenuState();
	MenuState.Page = GBHudMenuLocal::Wrap(NewPage, PageCount);
	MenuState.SkillCursor = KeepSkill;
}

// ======================================================================== mouse

void AGBHUD::ResetMenuMouse()
{
	MenuHits.Reset();
	MenuHitsMenu = EGBMenu::None;
	MenuHitsPage = -1;
	bMenuHitsChoosing = false;
	// The first position read after opening is only a baseline: an idle mouse must not steal the keyboard cursor.
	bHasMenuMouse = false;
}

void AGBHUD::AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind Kind, float X, float Y, float W, float H, int32 Index, FName Id)
{
	FMenuHit Hit;
	Hit.Kind = Kind;
	Hit.Min = FVector2D(X, Y);
	Hit.Max = FVector2D(X + FMath::Max(0.f, W), Y + FMath::Max(0.f, H));
	Hit.Index = Index;
	Hit.Id = Id;
	MenuHits.Add(Hit);
}

const AGBHUD::FMenuHit* AGBHUD::FindMenuHit(const FVector2D& CanvasPosition) const
{
	// Last drawn is on top.
	for (int32 i = MenuHits.Num() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
	{
		const FMenuHit& Hit = MenuHits[i];
		if (CanvasPosition.X >= Hit.Min.X && CanvasPosition.X <= Hit.Max.X && CanvasPosition.Y >= Hit.Min.Y && CanvasPosition.Y <= Hit.Max.Y)
		{
			return &Hit;
		}
	}
	return nullptr;
}

void AGBHUD::UpdateMenuMouse()
{
	APlayerController* PC = GetOwningPlayerController();
	float MouseX = 0.f;
	float MouseY = 0.f;
	if (!PC || !PC->GetMousePosition(MouseX, MouseY))
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector2D Mouse(MouseX * MouseToCanvas, MouseY * MouseToCanvas);
	const bool bMoved = bHasMenuMouse && !Mouse.Equals(LastMenuMouse, 0.5f);
	LastMenuMouse = Mouse;
	bHasMenuMouse = true;
	// Hover uses last frame's clickables, so the highlight moves in the same frame it is drawn.
	if (bMoved && MenuHitsMenu == GetOpenMenu())
	{
		if (const FMenuHit* Hit = FindMenuHit(Mouse))
		{
			ApplyMenuHover(*Hit);
		}
	}
}

void AGBHUD::ApplyMenuHover(const FMenuHit& Hit)
{
	switch (Hit.Kind)
	{
	case EMenuHitKind::PauseEntry:
	case EMenuHitKind::ItemRow:
	case EMenuHitKind::WeaponRow:
		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::SpellSlot:
		if (!MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
		{
			MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
		}
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::SpellChoice:
		MenuState.SubCursor = Hit.Index;
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::SkillNode:
		MenuState.SkillCursor = Hit.Id;
		break;
	default:
		// Tabs and the death screen only react to clicks.
		break;
	}
}

void AGBHUD::ActivateMenuHit(const FMenuHit& Hit)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayer();
	switch (Hit.Kind)
	{
	case EMenuHitKind::PauseEntry:
		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
		MenuConfirm();
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::Tab:
		if (Hit.Index != MenuState.Page)
		{
			SetInventoryPage(Hit.Index);
		}
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::ItemRow:
		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
		ConfirmItem(Player);
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::WeaponRow:
		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
		ConfirmWeapon(Player);
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::SpellSlot:
		// A click on another slot while choosing leaves the chooser and opens it for that slot.
		MenuState.bChoosingSpell = false;
		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
		ConfirmSpellSlot(Player);
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::SpellChoice:
		MenuState.SubCursor = Hit.Index;
		ConfirmSpellSlot(Player);
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::SkillNode:
		MenuState.SkillCursor = Hit.Id;
		ConfirmSkill(Player);
		break;
	case EMenuHitKind::DeathScreen:
		// The controller handles the respawn confirm (AGBPlayerController::InputMenuClick): nothing here,
		// so a click respawns exactly once.
		break;
	}
}

bool AGBHUD::MenuClick(const FVector2D& ScreenPosition)
{
	const EGBMenu Menu = GetOpenMenu();
	// Only the clickables of the screen that is showing right now (not of a page left this frame).
	if (Menu == EGBMenu::None || Menu != MenuHitsMenu || MenuHitsPage != MenuState.Page || bMenuHitsChoosing != MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
	{
		return false;
	}
	const FVector2D Position = ScreenPosition * MouseToCanvas;
	const FMenuHit* Found = FindMenuHit(Position);
	if (!Found)
	{
		return false;
	}
	// Copy: the action may change the menu, and the list is rebuilt on the next draw.
	const FMenuHit Hit = *Found;
	ActivateMenuHit(Hit);
	return true;
}

void AGBHUD::MenuNavigate(FIntPoint Direction)
{
	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayer();
	switch (GetOpenMenu())
	{
	case EGBMenu::Pause:
		if (Direction.Y != 0)
		{
			MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, 2);
		}
		break;
	case EGBMenu::SkillTrees:
		NavigateSkills(Direction);
		break;
	case EGBMenu::Inventory:
		if (MenuState.Page == PageSkills)
		{
			NavigateSkills(Direction);
		}
		else if (MenuState.Page == PageSpells)
		{
			if (Direction.Y == 0)
			{
				break;
			}
			if (MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
			{
				MenuState.SubCursor = Wrap(MenuState.SubCursor + Direction.Y, GetAssignableSpells(Player).Num());
			}
			else
			{
				MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, UGBSpellbookComponent::NumSlots + 1);
			}
		}
		else if (MenuState.Page == PageWeapons)
		{
			if (Direction.Y != 0)
			{
				MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, GetWeaponList(Player).Num());
			}
		}
		else if (Direction.Y != 0)
		{
			MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, GetInventoryList(Player).Num());
		}
		break;
	default:
		break;
	}
}

void AGBHUD::MenuConfirm()
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayer();
	switch (GetOpenMenu())
	{
	case EGBMenu::Pause:
		if (MenuState.Cursor == 0)
		{
			if (AGBPlayerController* PC = GetGBController())
			{
				PC->SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			UKismetSystemLibrary::QuitGame(this, GetOwningPlayerController(), EQuitPreference::Quit, false);
		}
		break;
	case EGBMenu::SkillTrees:
		ConfirmSkill(Player);
		break;
	case EGBMenu::Inventory:
		if (MenuState.Page == PageItems)
		{
			ConfirmItem(Player);
		}
		else if (MenuState.Page == PageWeapons)
		{
			ConfirmWeapon(Player);
		}
		else if (MenuState.Page == PageSpells)
		{
			ConfirmSpellSlot(Player);
		}
		else
		{
			ConfirmSkill(Player);
		}
		break;
	default:
		// Death: the controller handles the respawn confirm.
		break;
	}
}

bool AGBHUD::MenuBack()
{
	switch (GetOpenMenu())
	{
	case EGBMenu::Inventory:
		if (MenuState.Page == PageItems && MenuState.PendingCombine != NAME_None)
		{
			MenuState.PendingCombine = NAME_None;
			return true;
		}
		if (MenuState.Page == PageSpells && MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
		{
			MenuState.bChoosingSpell = false;
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	case EGBMenu::Death:
		// The death screen only closes by respawning.
		return true;
	default:
		return false;
	}
}

void AGBHUD::MenuTab(int32 Direction)
{
	if (Direction == 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	const EGBMenu Menu = GetOpenMenu();
	if (Menu == EGBMenu::Inventory)
	{
		SetInventoryPage(MenuState.Page + Direction);
	}
	else if (Menu == EGBMenu::SkillTrees)
	{
		// Jump to the first node of the other tree.
		const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
		const FSkillNodeRef* Current = Nodes.FindByPredicate([this](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Id == MenuState.SkillCursor; });
		const int32 OtherTree = Current ? 1 - Current->Tree : 0;
		if (const FSkillNodeRef* Other = Nodes.FindByPredicate([OtherTree](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Tree == OtherTree; }))
		{
			MenuState.SkillCursor = Other->Id;
		}
	}
}

// ======================================================================== lists

TArray<FName> AGBHUD::GetInventoryList(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	TArray<FName> Result;
	const UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player ? Player->GetInventory() : nullptr;
	if (!Inventory)
	{
		return Result;
	}
	if (const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
	const float LineH = FMath::Max(MeasureLabel(TEXT("Ag"), SizePx).Y, 1.f);
	float CurY = Y;
	FString Line;
	for (const FString& Word : Words)
	{
		const FString Candidate = Line.IsEmpty() ? Word : Line + TEXT(" ") + Word;
		if (!Line.IsEmpty() && MeasureLabel(Candidate, SizePx).X > MaxWidth)
		{
			DrawLabel(Line, Color, X, CurY, SizePx, 0.f, bShadow);
			CurY += LineH;
			Line = Word;
		}
		else
		{
			Line = Candidate;
		}
	}
	if (!Line.IsEmpty())
	{
		DrawLabel(Line, Color, X, CurY, SizePx, 0.f, bShadow);
		CurY += LineH;
	}
	return CurY - Y;
}

void AGBHUD::DrawMenus(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	const FGBHudSkinStyle& Style = GetSkinStyle(GetCurrentSkin());
	// Clickables are rebuilt while drawing, for the screen as it is now.
	MenuHits.Reset();
	MenuHitsMenu = GetOpenMenu();
	MenuHitsPage = MenuState.Page;
	bMenuHitsChoosing = MenuState.bChoosingSpell;
	switch (GetOpenMenu())
	{
	case EGBMenu::Pause:
		DrawPauseMenu(Style);
		break;
	case EGBMenu::Death:
		DrawDeathScreen();
		break;
	case EGBMenu::Inventory:
		DrawInventory(Player, Style);
		break;
	case EGBMenu::SkillTrees:
	{
		const float S = UIScale();
		DrawRect(GBHudMenuLocal::MenuDim, 0.f, 0.f, Canvas->ClipX, Canvas->ClipY);
		DrawLabel(TEXT("SKILL TREES"), GBHudMenuLocal::MenuWhite, Canvas->ClipX * 0.5f, Canvas->ClipY * 0.04f, 34.f, 0.5f, true, true);
		if (Player)
		{
			DrawSkillTrees(Player, Canvas->ClipY * 0.1f);
		}
		DrawLabel(TEXT("Arrows or mouse: move   Click or Enter: unlock   Q / E: other tree   Esc: close"), GBHudMenuLocal::MenuWhite,
			Canvas->ClipX * 0.5f, Canvas->ClipY - 40.f * S, 16.f, 0.5f);
		break;
	}
	default:
		break;
	}
}

void AGBHUD::DrawPauseMenu(const FGBHudSkinStyle& Style)
{
	const float S = UIScale();
	const float W = Canvas->ClipX;
	const float H = Canvas->ClipY;
	DrawRect(GBHudMenuLocal::MenuDim, 0.f, 0.f, W, H);

	const float PW = 440.f * S;
	const float PH = 260.f * S;
	const float PX = (W - PW) * 0.5f;
	const float PY = (H - PH) * 0.5f;
	const bool bShadow = !Style.bChunky;
	DrawPanel(Style, PX, PY, PW, PH);
	DrawLabel(TEXT("PAUSED"), Style.Text, W * 0.5f, PY + 24.f * S, 34.f, 0.5f, bShadow, true);

	static const TCHAR* Entries[] = { TEXT("Resume"), TEXT("Quit to desktop") };
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 2; ++i)
	{
		const float Y = PY + 100.f * S + i * 54.f * S;
		const bool bSelected = MenuState.Cursor == i;
		if (bSelected)
		{
			DrawRect(Style.Accent * FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, 0.35f), PX + 30.f * S, Y - 6.f * S, PW - 60.f * S, 42.f * S);
		}
		AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::PauseEntry, PX + 30.f * S, Y - 6.f * S, PW - 60.f * S, 42.f * S, i);
		DrawLabel(bSelected ? FString::Printf(TEXT("> %s <"), Entries[i]) : FString(Entries[i]), Style.Text, W * 0.5f, Y, 26.f, 0.5f, bShadow);
	}
	DrawLabel(TEXT("Click or Enter: select   Esc: resume"), Style.Text, W * 0.5f, PY + PH - 36.f * S, 15.f, 0.5f, bShadow);
}

void AGBHUD::DrawDeathScreen()
{
	const float W = Canvas->ClipX;
#include "Player/GBInputSet.h"

#include "InputAction.h"
#include "InputMappingContext.h"
#include "InputModifiers.h"
#include "InputCoreTypes.h"

namespace
{
	// Stick dead zones (input feel, not gameplay numbers).
	constexpr float GameplayStickDeadZone = 0.2f;
	constexpr float MenuStickDeadZone = 0.5f;

	/** Where a digital key pushes a 2D axis. */
	enum class EDir2D : uint8 { Right, Left, Up, Down };

	UInputAction* MakeAction(UObject* Outer, const TCHAR* Name, EInputActionValueType Type, bool bTriggerWhenPaused = false)
	{
		UInputAction* Action = NewObject<UInputAction>(Outer, MakeUniqueObjectName(Outer, UInputAction::StaticClass(), FName(Name)));
		Action->ValueType = Type;
		Action->bTriggerWhenPaused = bTriggerWhenPaused;
		return Action;
	}

	UInputModifierNegate* MakeNegate(UObject* Outer)
	{
		return NewObject<UInputModifierNegate>(Outer);
	}

	UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* MakeSwizzleYXZ(UObject* Outer)
	{
		UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* Swizzle = NewObject<UInputModifierSwizzleAxis>(Outer);
		Swizzle->Order = EInputAxisSwizzle::YXZ;
		return Swizzle;
	}

	UInputModifierDeadZone* MakeDeadZone(UObject* Outer, float Lower)
	{
		UInputModifierDeadZone* DeadZone = NewObject<UInputModifierDeadZone>(Outer);
		DeadZone->LowerThreshold = Lower;
		DeadZone->UpperThreshold = 1.f;
		DeadZone->Type = EDeadZoneType::Radial;
		return DeadZone;
	}

	// MapKey returns a reference into the context's array: every helper adds its modifiers before the next MapKey.

	void MapButton(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key)
	{
		Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
	}

	/** Axis1D: +1 or -1. */
	void MapAxis1D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, bool bNegative = false)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (bNegative)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a digital key: x = right, y = up / forward. */
	void MapDir2D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, EDir2D Dir)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Up || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeSwizzleYXZ(Context));
		}
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Left || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a stick, with a radial dead zone. */
	void MapStick(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, float DeadZone)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeDeadZone(Context, DeadZone));
	}

	void MapWASD(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action)
	{
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::W, EDir2D::Up);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::S, EDir2D::Down);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::A, EDir2D::Left);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::D, EDir2D::Right);
	}
}

void UGBInputSet::Build()
{
	if (GameplayContext)
	{
		return; // built once per player
	}

	using EType = EInputActionValueType;

	// ---- Actions ----
	Move = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Move"), EType::Axis2D);
	Look = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Look"), EType::Axis2D);
	LookStick = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_LookStick"), EType::Axis2D);
	SwitchWeapon = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SwitchWeapon"), EType::Axis1D);
	CycleQuickUse = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_CycleQuickUse"), EType::Axis1D);

	Jump = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Jump"), EType::Boolean);
	Dash = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Dash"), EType::Boolean);
	Primary = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Primary"), EType::Boolean);
	Secondary = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Secondary"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot1 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot1"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot2 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot2"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot3 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot3"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot4 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot4"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot5 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot5"), EType::Boolean);
	Enchant = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Enchant"), EType::Boolean);
	Reload = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Reload"), EType::Boolean);
	Interact = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Interact"), EType::Boolean);
	QuickUse = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QuickUse"), EType::Boolean);
	Block = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Block"), EType::Boolean);
	// These three open / close menus, so they must also fire while the game is paused.
	Inventory = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Inventory"), EType::Boolean, true);
	SkillTrees = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SkillTrees"), EType::Boolean, true);
	Pause = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Pause"), EType::Boolean, true);

	FaceUp = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceUp"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceLeft = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceLeft"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceDown = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceDown"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceRight = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceRight"), EType::Boolean);

	QTEUp = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTEUp"), EType::Boolean);
	QTELeft = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTELeft"), EType::Boolean);
	QTEDown = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTEDown"), EType::Boolean);
	QTERight = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTERight"), EType::Boolean);

	MenuNavigate = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuNavigate"), EType::Axis2D, true);
	MenuConfirm = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuConfirm"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuBack = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuBack"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuTab = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuTab"), EType::Axis1D, true);
	MenuClick = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuClick"), EType::Boolean, true);

	// ---- Gameplay context (GDD 9) ----
	GameplayContext = NewObject<UInputMappingContext>(this, MakeUniqueObjectName(this, UInputMappingContext::StaticClass(), TEXT("IMC_Gameplay")));
	UInputMappingContext* G = GameplayContext;

	MapWASD(G, Move);
	MapStick(G, Move, EKeys::Gamepad_Left2D, GameplayStickDeadZone);

	G->MapKey(Look, EKeys::Mouse2D);
	MapStick(G, LookStick, EKeys::Gamepad_Right2D, GameplayStickDeadZone);

	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::MouseWheelAxis);
	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::LeftAlt);
	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Up);

	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::LeftShift);
	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Right);
	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Left, true);

	MapButton(G, Jump, EKeys::SpaceBar);
	MapButton(G, Dash, EKeys::LeftControl);

	MapButton(G, Primary, EKeys::LeftMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, Primary, EKeys::Gamepad_RightTrigger);
	MapButton(G, Secondary, EKeys::RightMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, Secondary, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftTrigger);

	MapButton(G, SpellSlot1, EKeys::RightMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot1, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot2, EKeys::One);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot3, EKeys::Two);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot4, EKeys::Three);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot5, EKeys::Four);

	MapButton(G, Enchant, EKeys::T);
	MapButton(G, Enchant, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder);
	MapButton(G, Reload, EKeys::R);
	MapButton(G, Interact, EKeys::G);
	MapButton(G, QuickUse, EKeys::F);
	MapButton(G, QuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_RightShoulder);
	// L2 is also Secondary (aim with a gun, spell modifier with the staff): both meanings coexist, the character
	// and the spellbook decide (block is refused with a gun in hand).
	MapButton(G, Block, EKeys::Q);
	MapButton(G, Block, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftTrigger);

	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::Tab);
	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Left);
	MapButton(G, SkillTrees, EKeys::K);
	MapButton(G, Pause, EKeys::Escape);
	MapButton(G, Pause, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Right);

	MapButton(G, FaceUp, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Top);
	MapButton(G, FaceLeft, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Left);
	MapButton(G, FaceDown, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Bottom);
	MapButton(G, FaceRight, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Right);

	MapButton(G, QTEUp, EKeys::W);
	MapButton(G, QTELeft, EKeys::A);
	MapButton(G, QTEDown, EKeys::S);
	MapButton(G, QTERight, EKeys::D);

	// ---- Menu context ----
	MenuContext = NewObject<UInputMappingContext>(this, MakeUniqueObjectName(this, UInputMappingContext::StaticClass(), TEXT("IMC_Menu")));
	UInputMappingContext* M = MenuContext;

	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Up, EDir2D::Up);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Down, EDir2D::Down);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Left, EDir2D::Left);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Right, EDir2D::Right);
	MapWASD(M, MenuNavigate);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Up, EDir2D::Up);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Down, EDir2D::Down);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Left, EDir2D::Left);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Right, EDir2D::Right);
	MapStick(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_Left2D, MenuStickDeadZone);

	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::Enter);
	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::SpaceBar);
	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Bottom);

	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Escape);
	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Tab);
	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Right);

	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Q, true);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::E);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder, true);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_RightShoulder);

	MapButton(M, MenuClick, EKeys::LeftMouseButton);

	// Inside menus: the gamepad Menu / View buttons and K still toggle their screens (Esc and Tab are "back" here).
	MapButton(M, Pause, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Right);
	MapButton(M, Inventory, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Left);
	MapButton(M, SkillTrees, EKeys::K);
}

#include "World/GBEraSubsystem.h"

#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Engine/LevelStreaming.h"
#include "GameFramework/Controller.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "GameFramework/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

namespace GBEraSubsystemLocal
{
	// A hitch (loading, breakpoint) must not skip the whole fade in one frame.
	constexpr float MaxFadeStep = 0.1f;
}

UGBEraSubsystem* UGBEraSubsystem::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject || !GEngine)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	UWorld* World = GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull);
	return World ? World->GetSubsystem<UGBEraSubsystem>() : nullptr;
}

bool UGBEraSubsystem::DoesSupportWorldType(const EWorldType::Type WorldType) const
{
	return WorldType == EWorldType::Game || WorldType == EWorldType::PIE;
}

TStatId UGBEraSubsystem::GetStatId() const
{
	RETURN_QUICK_DECLARE_CYCLE_STAT(UGBEraSubsystem, STATGROUP_Tickables);
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::OnWorldBeginPlay(UWorld& InWorld)
{
	Super::OnWorldBeginPlay(InWorld);
	ApplyEra();
}

bool UGBEraSubsystem::IsPresentIn(EGBEraPresence Presence, EGBEra Era)
{
	switch (Presence)
	{
	case EGBEraPresence::PastOnly:   return Era == EGBEra::Past;
	case EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly: return Era == EGBEra::Future;
	default:                         return true;
	}
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::SetEraImmediate(EGBEra NewEra)
{
	CurrentEra = NewEra;
	ApplyEra();
}

bool UGBEraSubsystem::RequestTravel(EGBEra TargetEra, AActor* Traveller, bool bTeleport, const FTransform& Destination)
{
	if (bTravelling)
	{
		return false;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Era travel: no data subsystem, using default fade times."));
	}
	FadeOutTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::EraFadeOutTime, 0.35f) : 0.35f;
	HoldTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::EraHoldTime, 0.25f) : 0.25f;
	FadeInTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::EraFadeInTime, 0.45f) : 0.45f;

	bTravelling = true;
	TravelStage = 0;
	StageTime = 0.f;
	FadeAlpha = 0.f;
	PendingEra = TargetEra;
	PendingTraveller = Traveller;
	bPendingTeleport = bTeleport;
	PendingDestination = Destination;

	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Traveller))
	{
		Player->SetControlEnabled(false);
	}
	return true;
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::Tick(float DeltaTime)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaTime);

	if (!bTravelling)
	{
		return;
	}

	// The fade runs in real time: the spell QTE slows the world, the fade must not slow with it.
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const float RealDelta = FMath::Clamp(World ? World->DeltaRealTimeSeconds : DeltaTime, 0.f, GBEraSubsystemLocal::MaxFadeStep);
	StageTime += RealDelta;

	if (TravelStage == 0)
	{
		FadeAlpha = FadeOutTime > 0.f ? FMath::Clamp(StageTime / FadeOutTime, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
		if (StageTime >= FadeOutTime)
		{
			FadeAlpha = 1.f;
			PerformSwap();
			TravelStage = 1;
			StageTime = 0.f;
		}
	}
	else if (TravelStage == 1)
	{
		FadeAlpha = 1.f;
		if (StageTime >= HoldTime)
		{
			TravelStage = 2;
			StageTime = 0.f;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		FadeAlpha = FadeInTime > 0.f ? 1.f - FMath::Clamp(StageTime / FadeInTime, 0.f, 1.f) : 0.f;
		if (StageTime >= FadeInTime)
		{
			FinishTravel();
		}
	}
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::PerformSwap()
{
	// The traveller died while the screen was covered: undo the travel and fade back in (no teleport,
	// no restore, no checkpoint for a dead player).
	bool bTravellerDead = false;
	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* DeadPlayer = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(PendingTraveller.Get()))
	{
		bTravellerDead = DeadPlayer->IsDead();
	}
	else if (const UGBAttributeComponent* DeadAttributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(PendingTraveller.Get()))
	{
		bTravellerDead = DeadAttributes->IsDead();
	}
	if (bTravellerDead)
	{
		PendingEra = CurrentEra;
		return;
	}

	CurrentEra = PendingEra;
	ApplyEra();

	AActor* Traveller = PendingTraveller.Get();
	if (!Traveller)
	{
		return;
	}

	if (bPendingTeleport)
	{
		const FVector Location = PendingDestination.GetLocation();
		const FRotator Rotation = PendingDestination.Rotator();
		if (!Traveller->TeleportTo(Location, Rotation))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Era travel: teleport of %s to %s failed."), *Traveller->GetName(), *Location.ToString());
		}
		if (UMovementComponent* Movement = Traveller->FindComponentByClass<UMovementComponent>())
		{
			Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
		}
		if (APawn* Pawn = Cast<APawn>(Traveller))
		{
			if (AController* Controller = Pawn->GetController())
			{
				FRotator Control = Controller->GetControlRotation();
				Control.Yaw = Rotation.Yaw;
				Control.Roll = 0.f;
				Controller->SetControlRotation(Control);
			}
		}
	}

	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Traveller))
	{
		// GDD 4: era travel restores health, mana and ammo; portals are the checkpoints of the map.
		// Never for a dead player (death undoes the travel before this point).
		if (!Player->IsDead())
		{
			Player->RestoreAll();
			if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
			{
				Progress->SaveCheckpoint(Player);
			}
		}
	}
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::FinishTravel()
{
	FadeAlpha = 0.f;
	bTravelling = false;
	TravelStage = 0;
	StageTime = 0.f;

	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(PendingTraveller.Get()))
	{
		Player->SetControlEnabled(true);
	}
	PendingTraveller.Reset();

	OnTravelFinished.Broadcast(CurrentEra);
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::ApplyEra()
{
	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
	{
		for (ULevelStreaming* Level : World->GetStreamingLevels())
		{
			if (!Level)
			{
				continue;
			}
			const FString PackageName = Level->GetWorldAssetPackageName();
			const bool bPast = PackageName.EndsWith(TEXT("_Past"));
			const bool bFuture = PackageName.EndsWith(TEXT("_Future"));
			if (!bPast && !bFuture)
			{
				continue;
			}
			// Both versions stay loaded so the swap behind the fade is only a visibility change.
			Level->SetShouldBeLoaded(true);
			Level->SetShouldBeVisible(bPast ? CurrentEra == EGBEra::Past : CurrentEra == EGBEra::Future);
		}
	}

	OnEraChanged.Broadcast(CurrentEra);
}
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/GameInstance.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"

UGBProgressSubsystem* UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (const UGameInstance* DirectInstance = Cast<UGameInstance>(WorldContextObject))
	{
		return DirectInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBProgressSubsystem>();
	}
	const UWorld* World = GEngine ? GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull) : nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = World ? World->GetGameInstance() : nullptr;
	return GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBProgressSubsystem>() : nullptr;
}

bool UGBProgressSubsystem::HasFlag(FName Flag) const
{
	return Flags.Contains(Flag);
}

void UGBProgressSubsystem::SetFlag(FName Flag, bool bValue)
{
	if (Flag.IsNone())
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Progress: SetFlag called with an empty flag name."));
		return;
	}

	const bool bChanged = bValue ? !Flags.Contains(Flag) : Flags.Contains(Flag);
	if (!bChanged)
	{
		return;
	}

	if (bValue)
	{
		Flags.Add(Flag);
	}
	else
	{
		Flags.Remove(Flag);
	}
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Log, TEXT("Progress: flag %s = %s"), *Flag.ToString(), bValue ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false"));
	OnFlagChanged.Broadcast(Flag);
}

void UGBProgressSubsystem::SaveCheckpoint(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!Player)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Progress: SaveCheckpoint without a player."));
		return;
	}

	FGBPlayerSnapshot Snapshot;
	Player->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Snapshot.bValid = true;
	Checkpoint = MoveTemp(Snapshot);
	OnCheckpointSaved.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBProgressSubsystem::RestoreCheckpoint(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!Player || !Checkpoint.bValid)
	{
		return false;
	}
	Player->RespawnAt(Checkpoint);
	return true;
}

void UGBProgressSubsystem::ResetProgress()
{
	const TSet<FName> Cleared = MoveTemp(Flags);
	Flags.Reset();
	Checkpoint = FGBPlayerSnapshot();
	for (const FName& Flag : Cleared)
	{
		OnFlagChanged.Broadcast(Flag);
	}
}

#include "World/GBEraComponent.h"

#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "GameFramework/PawnMovementComponent.h"
#include "World/GBEraSubsystem.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

UGBEraComponent::UGBEraComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

void UGBEraComponent::Configure(EGBEraPresence InPresence, FName InRequiredFlag, FName InRemovedByFlag)
{
	Presence = InPresence;
	RequiredFlag = InRequiredFlag;
	RemovedByFlag = InRemovedByFlag;
	if (HasBegunPlay())
	{
		Refresh();
	}
}

bool UGBEraComponent::ComputePresent(EGBEraPresence InPresence, EGBEra CurrentEra, bool bRequiredFlagOk, bool bRemovedFlagSet)
{
	return UGBEraSubsystem::IsPresentIn(InPresence, CurrentEra) && bRequiredFlagOk && !bRemovedFlagSet;
}

void UGBEraComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		EraChangedHandle = Era->OnEraChanged.AddUObject(this, &UGBEraComponent::HandleEraChanged);
	}
	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		FlagChangedHandle = Progress->OnFlagChanged.AddUObject(this, &UGBEraComponent::HandleFlagChanged);
	}
	Refresh();
}

void UGBEraComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->OnEraChanged.Remove(EraChangedHandle);
	}
	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Progress->OnFlagChanged.Remove(FlagChangedHandle);
	}
	EraChangedHandle.Reset();
	FlagChangedHandle.Reset();

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void UGBEraComponent::HandleEraChanged(EGBEra NewEra)
{
	Refresh();
}

void UGBEraComponent::HandleFlagChanged(FName Flag)
{
	if (Flag == RequiredFlag || Flag == RemovedByFlag)
	{
		Refresh();
	}
}

void UGBEraComponent::Refresh()
{
	const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this);
	const EGBEra CurrentEra = Era ? Era->GetCurrentEra() : EGBEra::Past;

	const UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	const bool bRequiredOk = RequiredFlag.IsNone() || (Progress && Progress->HasFlag(RequiredFlag));
	const bool bRemoved = !RemovedByFlag.IsNone() && Progress && Progress->HasFlag(RemovedByFlag);

	ApplyPresence(ComputePresent(Presence, CurrentEra, bRequiredOk, bRemoved));
}

void UGBEraComponent::ApplyPresence(bool bNewPresent)
{
	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	if (!Owner || bNewPresent == bPresent)
	{
		return;
	}
	bPresent = bNewPresent;

	APawn* Pawn = Cast<APawn>(Owner);
	UMovementComponent* Movement = Pawn ? Pawn->GetMovementComponent() : nullptr;

	if (!bNewPresent)
	{
		// Remember the owner's own settings (it may have turned collision or tick off itself).
		bSavedHidden = Owner->IsHidden();
		bSavedCollision = Owner->GetActorEnableCollision();
		bSavedTick = Owner->IsActorTickEnabled();
		bHasSaved = true;

		// Remember every ticking component first (movement included), so the other era simulates nothing.
		SavedTickComponents.Reset();
		TArray<UActorComponent*> Components;
		Owner->GetComponents<UActorComponent>(Components);
		for (UActorComponent* Comp : Components)
		{
			if (Comp && Comp != this && Comp->IsComponentTickEnabled())
			{
				SavedTickComponents.Add(Comp);
				Comp->SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
			}
		}

		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(false);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		// An enemy of the other era must neither fall (its collision is off) nor walk.
		if (Movement)
		{
			Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(bHasSaved ? bSavedHidden : false);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(bHasSaved ? bSavedCollision : true);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(bHasSaved ? bSavedTick : true);
		// Re-enable exactly the components that were ticking (a burning enemy keeps burning on return).
		for (TWeakObjectPtr<UActorComponent>& Saved : SavedTickComponents)
		{
			if (UActorComponent* Comp = Saved.Get())
			{
				Comp->SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
			}
		}
		SavedTickComponents.Reset();
		if (Movement && !bHasSaved)
		{
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
		}
	}

	OnPresenceChanged.Broadcast();
}

AGBGameMode::AGBGameMode()
{
	DefaultPawnClass = AGBPlayerCharacter::StaticClass();
	PlayerControllerClass = AGBPlayerController::StaticClass();
	HUDClass = AGBHUD::StaticClass();

	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bTickEvenWhenPaused = true;
}

AGBGameMode* AGBGameMode::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject || !GEngine)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	UWorld* World = GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull);
	return World ? Cast<AGBGameMode>(World->GetAuthGameMode()) : nullptr;
}

void AGBGameMode::InitGame(const FString& MapName, const FString& Options, FString& ErrorMessage)
{
	Super::InitGame(MapName, Options, ErrorMessage);

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}

	bool bHasPlayerStart = false;
	for (TActorIterator<APlayerStart> It(World); It; ++It)
	{
		bHasPlayerStart = true;
		break;
	}

	AGBTestArena* ExistingArena = nullptr;
	for (TActorIterator<AGBTestArena> It(World); It; ++It)
	{
		ExistingArena = *It;
		break;
	}

	// The default map is the engine's empty Entry map (it has a PlayerStart but no floor): the test level
	// is built in code whenever the map is an engine map or has no PlayerStart of its own.
	const FString PackageName = UWorld::RemovePIEPrefix(World->GetPackage()->GetName());
	const bool bEngineMap = PackageName.StartsWith(TEXT("/Engine/"));

	if (ExistingArena)
	{
		ArenaPlayerStart = ExistingArena->GetPlayerStartActor();
		bHasArenaStart = ArenaPlayerStart.IsValid();
		if (bHasArenaStart)
		{
			ArenaStart = ArenaPlayerStart->GetActorTransform();
		}
		ApplyKillZ(ExistingArena);
		return;
	}
	if (bHasPlayerStart && !bEngineMap)
	{
		ApplyKillZ(nullptr);
		return;
	}

	// The default map is the engine's empty Entry map: build the test level in code before the player spawns.
	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
	AGBTestArena* Arena = World->SpawnActor<AGBTestArena>(AGBTestArena::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
	if (!Arena)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("GameMode: could not spawn the test arena."));
		ApplyKillZ(nullptr);
		return;
	}
	Arena->BuildStatic();
	ArenaStart = Arena->GetPlayerStartTransform();
	bHasArenaStart = true;
	ArenaPlayerStart = Arena->GetPlayerStartActor();
	ApplyKillZ(Arena);
}

void AGBGameMode::ApplyKillZ(const AGBTestArena* Arena)
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	AWorldSettings* WorldSettings = World ? World->GetWorldSettings() : nullptr;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!WorldSettings || !Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GameMode: no world settings or data; KillZ left as the level has it."));
		return;
	}

	// GetGameValue logs a warning once when the key is missing; the sentinel tells "missing" from any real height.
	const float Missing = -TNumericLimits<float>::Max();
	const float Value = Data->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::KillZ, Missing);
	if (Value == Missing)
	{
		return;
	}

	if (Arena)
	{
		// Floor top of the code-built arena is its actor's height (GBTestArena.cpp layout).
		WorldSettings->KillZ = static_cast<float>(Arena->GetActorLocation().Z) + Value;
		return;
	}

	// A real level that set its own KillZ keeps it.
	const AWorldSettings* Defaults = WorldSettings->GetClass()->GetDefaultObject<AWorldSettings>();
	if (Defaults && !FMath::IsNearlyEqual(WorldSettings->KillZ, Defaults->KillZ))
	{
		return;
	}
	WorldSettings->KillZ = Value;
}

AActor* AGBGameMode::ChoosePlayerStart_Implementation(AController* Player)
{
	// The arena built a real APlayerStart: use it, not the one that came with an engine map.
	if (APlayerStart* Start = ArenaPlayerStart.Get())
	{
		return Start;
	}
	return Super::ChoosePlayerStart_Implementation(Player);
}

void AGBGameMode::HandlePlayerDeath(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (bWaitingToRespawn)
	{
		return;
	}
	bWaitingToRespawn = true;

	if (Player)
	{
		if (AGBPlayerController* Controller = Cast<AGBPlayerController>(Player->GetController()))
		{
			Controller->SetMenu(EGBMenu::Death);
		}
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GameMode: no data subsystem, using the default respawn delay."));
	}
	const float Delay = FMath::Max(0.f, Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::RespawnDelay, 2.5f) : 2.5f);
	if (Delay <= 0.f)
	{
		RespawnPlayer();
		return;
	}

	RespawnRealTime = GetWorld()->GetRealTimeSeconds() + Delay;
	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(RespawnTimer, this, &AGBGameMode::RespawnPlayer, Delay, false);
}

void AGBGameMode::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	// Timers do not run while the game is paused (the death screen pauses it): fall back to real time.
	if (bWaitingToRespawn && GetWorld() && GetWorld()->GetRealTimeSeconds() >= RespawnRealTime)
	{
		RespawnPlayer();
	}
}

void AGBGameMode::RespawnPlayer()
{
	if (!bWaitingToRespawn)
	{
		return;
	}
	bWaitingToRespawn = false;

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}
	GetWorldTimerManager().ClearTimer(RespawnTimer);

	// Provisional rule (GDD 10): a fight in progress starts over; cleared encounters stay cleared.
	for (TActorIterator<AGBEncounter> It(World); It; ++It)
	{
		if (It->GetState() == EGBEncounterState::Active)
		{
			It->ResetEncounter();
		}
	}

	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	AGBPlayerController* Controller = Player ? Cast<AGBPlayerController>(Player->GetController()) : nullptr;
	if (!Controller)
	{
		Controller = Cast<AGBPlayerController>(World->GetFirstPlayerController());
	}

	UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	const bool bRestored = Player && Progress && Progress->RestoreCheckpoint(Player);

	if (Controller && Controller->GetOpenMenu() == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		Controller->SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
	}

	if (bRestored)
	{
		Player->SetControlEnabled(true);
		return;
	}

	// No checkpoint yet: start the map again (progress flags survive in the game instance).
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Log, TEXT("GameMode: no checkpoint to restore, reloading the map."));
	const FString MapPath = UWorld::RemovePIEPrefix(World->GetPackage()->GetName());
	UGameplayStatics::OpenLevel(this, FName(*MapPath));
}

#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"

namespace GBProjectileLocal
{
	/** Same lift as the radial damage obstruction trace (GBCombatLibrary.cpp), so a blast on the floor is not blocked by it. */
	constexpr float KnockbackTraceLiftCm = 5.f;
	/**
	 * Smallest upward part of the push direction. A walking character pushed only sideways is stopped at once by
	 * ground friction; a little lift makes it fly, so the push is felt even for a blast at the target's height.
	 */
	constexpr float KnockbackMinLift = 0.35f;
}

AGBProjectile::AGBProjectile()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	Collision = CreateDefaultSubobject<USphereComponent>(TEXT("Collision"));
	Collision->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
	Collision->SetCollisionObjectType(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToAllChannels(ECR_Ignore);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Overlap);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldDynamic, ECR_Overlap);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldStatic, ECR_Block);
	Collision->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
	Collision->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
	Collision->OnComponentHit.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionHit);
	Collision->OnComponentBeginOverlap.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionOverlap);
	RootComponent = Collision;

	Movement = CreateDefaultSubobject<UProjectileMovementComponent>(TEXT("Movement"));
	Movement->UpdatedComponent = Collision;
	Movement->bRotationFollowsVelocity = true;
	// The launch velocity is given in world space by InitProjectile.
	Movement->bInitialVelocityInLocalSpace = false;
	Movement->bShouldBounce = false;
	Movement->ProjectileGravityScale = 0.f;

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Collision);

	Mesh = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Mesh"));
	Mesh->SetupAttachment(Collision);
	Mesh->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Mesh->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Mesh->SetCastShadow(false);
	Mesh->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
}

AGBProjectile* AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(UWorld* World, const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
{
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const FVector LaunchDirection = Direction.IsNearlyZero() ? FVector::ForwardVector : Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	const FTransform SpawnTransform(LaunchDirection.Rotation(), Origin);

	AGBProjectile* Projectile = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBProjectile>(AGBProjectile::StaticClass(), SpawnTransform, InInstigator,
		Cast<APawn>(InInstigator), ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn);
	if (!Projectile)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Projectile: spawn failed"));
		return nullptr;
	}

	Projectile->InitProjectile(InConfig, LaunchDirection, InInstigator);
	Projectile->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);

	if (IsValid(InInstigator) && IsValid(Projectile))
	{
		Projectile->Collision->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(InInstigator, true);
		if (UPrimitiveComponent* InstigatorRoot = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(InInstigator->GetRootComponent()))
		{
			InstigatorRoot->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(Projectile, true);
		}
	}

	return IsValid(Projectile) ? Projectile : nullptr;
}

void AGBProjectile::InitProjectile(const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
{
	Config = InConfig;
	if (InInstigator)
	{
		Config.Damage.Instigator = InInstigator;
	}
	Config.Damage.Causer = this;
	StartLocation = GetActorLocation();

	const float Radius = FMath::Max(Config.Radius, 1.f);
	Collision->SetSphereRadius(Radius);

	Movement->InitialSpeed = Config.Speed;
	Movement->MaxSpeed = Config.Speed;
	Movement->ProjectileGravityScale = Config.GravityScale;
	Movement->bShouldBounce = Config.FuseTime > 0.f;
	Movement->Velocity = Direction.GetSafeNormal() * Config.Speed;

	const float VisualSize = Radius * 2.f * FMath::Max(Config.VisualScale, 0.01f);
	if (Config.bPixelArt)
	{
		Mesh->SetVisibility(false);
		if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* SpriteLibrary = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this))
		{
			SpriteLibrary->SetupBillboard(Sprite, Config.SpriteId, VisualSize);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Projectile: no pixel sprite library; sprite '%s' not shown"), *Config.SpriteId.ToString());
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
		Sprite->SetHiddenInGame(true);
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Mesh, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(VisualSize), Config.Color, /*bGlow*/ true);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
}

void AGBProjectile::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	// Undo the instigator's ignore entry so its list does not grow with every shot.
	AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
	if (IsValid(ProjectileInstigator))
	{
		if (UPrimitiveComponent* InstigatorRoot = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(ProjectileInstigator->GetRootComponent()))
		{
			InstigatorRoot->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(this, false);
		}
	}

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBProjectile::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	if (bFinished)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector Location = GetActorLocation();
	const float Age = GetGameTimeSinceCreation();
	const bool bOutOfRange = Config.MaxRange > 0.f && FVector::Dist(StartLocation, Location) >= Config.MaxRange;
	const bool bTooOld = Config.MaxLifetime > 0.f && Age > Config.MaxLifetime;
	const bool bFuseDone = Config.FuseTime > 0.f && Age >= Config.FuseTime;

	if (bOutOfRange || bTooOld || bFuseDone)
	{
		if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
		{
			Explode(Location);
		}
		Finish(nullptr, Location);
	}
}

bool AGBProjectile::IsValidTarget(const AActor* Other) const
{
	if (!IsValid(Other) || Other == this || Other->IsA<AGBProjectile>())
	{
		return false;
	}

	const AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
	if (Other == ProjectileInstigator)
	{
		return false;
	}

# make_materials.py - Unreal Editor Python script that (re)builds the project's few materials from text.
#
# The .uasset files are not in git (Content/ is ignored except Content/Data), so this script IS the source of the
# materials. Run it through Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1 (UnrealEditor-Cmd -run=pythonscript), Editor closed.
#
# M_PixelSurface (/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelSurface): the look of every medieval / magic structure
# (GDD 3: "pixel-art environments are 3D geometry with low-resolution pixel textures"). Used from C++ by
# GBPlaceholder::SetupTexturedShape through a dynamic material instance. Parameters:
#   Texture   (texture object) the tile, a transient texture set at runtime (wrap addressing, nearest filter)
#   TileSize  (scalar, cm)     one tile covers TileSize x TileSize cm in the world, on every face
#   Tint      (vector)         multiplies the texture color
#   Glow      (scalar)         emissive = color * Glow (0 = plain lit surface)
#
# Why the projection is built by hand instead of using the engine function WorldAlignedTexture: the texture sample
# inside that function uses the shared sampler "Wrap, World group settings", which takes its filtering from the
# World texture group (smooth). Pixel art needs the texture's own nearest filter, so our sample uses
# "From texture asset" and the world-space projection is rebuilt here: absolute world position / TileSize, and the
# projection plane is picked by the dominant axis of the vertex normal (hard switch, no blending between planes).

import unreal

MATERIAL_DIR = "/Game/Glyphbound/Materials"
PIXEL_SURFACE = "M_PixelSurface"
DEFAULT_TEXTURE = "/Engine/EngineMaterials/DefaultDiffuse.DefaultDiffuse"

MEL = unreal.MaterialEditingLibrary


def log(message):
    unreal.log("make_materials: " + message)


def fail(message):
    unreal.log_error("make_materials: ERROR " + message)
    raise RuntimeError(message)


def get_or_create_material(directory, name):
    path = directory + "/" + name
    if unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.does_asset_exist(path):
        material = unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.load_asset(path)
        if not isinstance(material, unreal.Material):
            fail("%s exists but is not a Material" % path)
        # Rebuild the graph from scratch so the asset always matches this script.
        MEL.delete_all_material_expressions(material)
        log("rebuilding %s" % path)
        return material
    if not unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.does_directory_exist(directory):
        unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.make_directory(directory)
    tools = unreal.AssetToolsHelpers.get_asset_tools()
    material = tools.create_asset(name, directory, unreal.Material, unreal.MaterialFactoryNew())
    if material is None:
        fail("could not create %s" % path)
    log("created %s" % path)
    return material


class Graph(object):
    def __init__(self, material):
        self.material = material
        self.x = -1600

    def node(self, expression_class, x, y, **properties):
        expression = MEL.create_material_expression(self.material, expression_class, x, y)
        if expression is None:
            fail("could not create %s" % expression_class.__name__)
        for key, value in properties.items():
            expression.set_editor_property(key, value)
        return expression

    def connect(self, source, source_output, target, target_inputs):
        """target_inputs: one input name or a list of accepted names (script name first, full name second)."""
        if isinstance(target_inputs, str):
            target_inputs = [target_inputs]
        for target_input in target_inputs:
            if MEL.connect_material_expressions(source, source_output, target, target_input):
                return
        fail("cannot connect %s.%r -> %s.%r (outputs %s, inputs %s)" % (
            source.get_class().get_name(), source_output, target.get_class().get_name(), target_inputs,
            list(MEL.get_material_expression_output_names(source)),
            list(MEL.get_material_expression_input_names(target))))

    def to_property(self, source, source_output, material_property):
        if not MEL.connect_material_property(source, source_output, material_property):
            fail("cannot connect %s.%r -> %s (outputs %s)" % (
                source.get_class().get_name(), source_output, material_property,
                list(MEL.get_material_expression_output_names(source))))


def mask(graph, source, x, y, r=False, g=False, b=False, a=False):
    expression = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionComponentMask, x, y, r=r, g=g, b=b, a=a)
    graph.connect(source, "", expression, ["", "Input"])
    return expression


def build_pixel_surface():
    material = get_or_create_material(MATERIAL_DIR, PIXEL_SURFACE)
    material.set_editor_property("blend_mode", unreal.BlendMode.BLEND_OPAQUE)
    material.set_editor_property("shading_model", unreal.MaterialShadingModel.MSM_DEFAULT_LIT)
    material.set_editor_property("two_sided", False)

    default_texture = unreal.load_asset(DEFAULT_TEXTURE)
    if default_texture is None:
        fail("cannot load the default texture %s" % DEFAULT_TEXTURE)

    g = Graph(material)

    # ---- Parameters ----
    texture = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionTextureObjectParameter, -1400, -500,
                     parameter_name="Texture", texture=default_texture,
                     sampler_type=unreal.MaterialSamplerType.SAMPLERTYPE_COLOR)
    tile_size = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionScalarParameter, -2000, 0,
                       parameter_name="TileSize", default_value=100.0)
    tint = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionVectorParameter, -600, -250,
                  parameter_name="Tint", default_value=unreal.LinearColor(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0))
    glow = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionScalarParameter, -400, 150,
                  parameter_name="Glow", default_value=0.0)

    # ---- World-space coordinates in tiles ----
    world_position = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionWorldPosition, -2000, -150)
    in_tiles = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionDivide, -1800, -100)
    g.connect(world_position, "", in_tiles, "A")
    g.connect(tile_size, "", in_tiles, "B")

    wx = mask(g, in_tiles, -1650, -250, r=True)
    wy = mask(g, in_tiles, -1650, -150, g=True)
    wz = mask(g, in_tiles, -1650, -50, b=True)
    # Texture rows go top to bottom: V = -Z keeps the sprite upright on walls.
    wz_down = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionMultiply, -1500, -50, const_b=-1.0)
    g.connect(wz, "", wz_down, "A")

    uv_x = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAppendVector, -1300, -250)  # faces looking along X
    g.connect(wy, "", uv_x, "A")
    g.connect(wz_down, "", uv_x, "B")
    uv_y = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAppendVector, -1300, -150)  # faces looking along Y
    g.connect(wx, "", uv_y, "A")
    g.connect(wz_down, "", uv_y, "B")
    uv_z = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAppendVector, -1300, -50)   # floors and ceilings
    g.connect(wx, "", uv_z, "A")
    g.connect(wy, "", uv_z, "B")

    # ---- Pick the plane by the dominant axis of the normal ----
    normal = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionVertexNormalWS, -2000, 250)
    normal_abs = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAbs, -1800, 250)
    g.connect(normal, "", normal_abs, ["", "Input"])
    nx = mask(g, normal_abs, -1650, 200, r=True)
    ny = mask(g, normal_abs, -1650, 300, g=True)
    nz = mask(g, normal_abs, -1650, 400, b=True)

    uv_side = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionIf, -1100, -150)
    g.connect(nx, "", uv_side, "A")
    g.connect(ny, "", uv_side, "B")
    g.connect(uv_x, "", uv_side, ["A > B", "AGreaterThanB"])
    g.connect(uv_y, "", uv_side, ["A == B", "AEqualsB"])
    g.connect(uv_y, "", uv_side, ["A < B", "ALessThanB"])

    side_max = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionMax, -1300, 300)
    g.connect(nx, "", side_max, "A")
    g.connect(ny, "", side_max, "B")

    uv = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionIf, -900, -100)
    g.connect(nz, "", uv, "A")
    g.connect(side_max, "", uv, "B")
    g.connect(uv_z, "", uv, ["A > B", "AGreaterThanB"])
    g.connect(uv_side, "", uv, ["A == B", "AEqualsB"])
    g.connect(uv_side, "", uv, ["A < B", "ALessThanB"])

    # ---- Sample with the texture's own filter and addressing (nearest + wrap for tiles) ----
    sample = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionTextureSample, -700, -300,
                    sampler_source=unreal.SamplerSourceMode.SSM_FROM_TEXTURE_ASSET,
                    sampler_type=unreal.MaterialSamplerType.SAMPLERTYPE_COLOR)
    g.connect(texture, "", sample, ["Tex", "TextureObject"])
    g.connect(uv, "", sample, ["UVs", "Coordinates"])

    tint_rgb = mask(g, tint, -450, -250, r=True, g=True, b=True)
    color = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionMultiply, -300, -300)
    g.connect(sample, "RGB", color, "A")
    g.connect(tint_rgb, "", color, "B")

    emissive = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionMultiply, -150, 0)
    g.connect(color, "", emissive, "A")
    g.connect(glow, "", emissive, "B")

    roughness = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionConstant, -150, 150, r=0.9)

    g.to_property(color, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_BASE_COLOR)
    g.to_property(emissive, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_EMISSIVE_COLOR)
    g.to_property(roughness, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_ROUGHNESS)

    errors = MEL.recompile_material(material)
    if errors:
        for error in errors:
            unreal.log_error("make_materials: ERROR compile: %s" % error)
        fail("%s has compile errors" % PIXEL_SURFACE)
    bad_nodes = MEL.get_material_error_expressions(material)
    if bad_nodes:
        fail("%s: %d expressions report errors: %s" % (
            PIXEL_SURFACE, len(bad_nodes), [n.get_class().get_name() for n in bad_nodes]))

    path = MATERIAL_DIR + "/" + PIXEL_SURFACE
    if not unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.save_asset(path, only_if_is_dirty=False):
        fail("could not save %s" % path)
    log("saved %s (params: %s %s %s)" % (
        path,
        list(MEL.get_texture_parameter_names(material)) if hasattr(MEL, "get_texture_parameter_names") else "",
        list(MEL.get_scalar_parameter_names(material)) if hasattr(MEL, "get_scalar_parameter_names") else "",
        list(MEL.get_vector_parameter_names(material)) if hasattr(MEL, "get_vector_parameter_names") else ""))


def main():
    build_pixel_surface()
    log("OK")


main()

void AGBPlayerCharacter::FellOutOfWorld(const UDamageType& /*DmgType*/)
{
	// Never Super: the engine would destroy the pawn. The checkpoint respawn brings the player back.
	if (IsDead())
	{
		FreezeMovement(); // already dead (or killed by the first call): just keep it from falling forever
		return;
	}
	if (!Attributes)
	{
		return;
	}

	EndDash();
	FreezeMovement();

	// Friendly fire and invulnerability do not matter here: the hit goes straight to the attributes.
	const bool bWasInvulnerable = Attributes->IsInvulnerable();
	Attributes->SetInvulnerable(false);
	FGBDamageInfo Fall;
	Fall.Amount = FallOutOfWorldDamage;
	Fall.Element = EGBElement::Physical;
	Fall.SourceId = FellOutOfWorldSourceId;
	Attributes->ApplyDamage(Fall); // HitDirection is zero, so a raised shield cannot block it
	Attributes->SetInvulnerable(bWasInvulnerable);

	if (!IsDead())
	{
		// Only a data setup with DamageTaken 0 gets here: respawn directly rather than fall forever.
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: fell out of the world but could not be killed (DamageTaken 0?); respawning at the checkpoint."));
		if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
		{
			if (Progress->RestoreCheckpoint(this))
			{
				return;
			}
		}
		UnfreezeMovement();
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::FreezeMovement()
{
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		MoveComp->StopMovementImmediately();
		if (!bMovementFrozen)
		{
			MoveComp->DisableMovement();
		}
	}
	bMovementFrozen = true;
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::UnfreezeMovement()
{
	if (!bMovementFrozen)
	{
		return;
	}
	bMovementFrozen = false;
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		// Falling settles into walking on the first floor check.
		MoveComp->SetMovementMode(MOVE_Falling);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.bValid = true;
	Snapshot.Transform = GetActorTransform();
	Snapshot.ControlRotation = Controller ? Controller->GetControlRotation() : GetActorRotation();
	if (const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Snapshot.Era = Era->GetCurrentEra();
	}
	Snapshot.Health = Attributes->GetHealth();
	Snapshot.Mana = Attributes->GetMana();

	Equipment->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Spellbook->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Inventory->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
	SkillTree->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{	if (QTE->IsActive())
	{
		QTE->CancelQTE();
	}
	EndDash();

	SetActorTransform(Snapshot.Transform, false, nullptr, ETeleportType::TeleportPhysics);
	if (Controller)
	{
		Controller->SetControlRotation(Snapshot.ControlRotation);
	}
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->SetEraImmediate(Snapshot.Era);
	}

	// Components first: armor and skill nodes change MaxHealth / MaxMana, which clamp the values set below.
	Equipment->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Spellbook->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Inventory->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
	SkillTree->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);

	Attributes->RestoreAll(); // revives
	const float Health = Snapshot.Health > 0.f ? Snapshot.Health : Attributes->GetMaxHealth();
	Attributes->SetHealthAndMana(Health, Snapshot.Mana);

	UnfreezeMovement();
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		MoveComp->StopMovementImmediately();
	}
	PendingRecoil = FRotator::ZeroRotator;
	LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
	SetControlEnabled(true);
	ApplyMovementStats();

	if (AGBPlayerController* PC = Cast<AGBPlayerController>(Controller))
	{
		if (PC->GetOpenMenu() == EGBMenu::Death)
		{
			PC->SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
		}
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::RespawnAt(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{
	// Death never rolls back what the player owns: only place, era and resources are restored.
	if (QTE && QTE->IsActive())
	{
		QTE->CancelQTE();
	}
	if (Spellbook)
	{
		Spellbook->CancelSustained();
	}
	EndDash();

	SetActorTransform(Snapshot.Transform, false, nullptr, ETeleportType::TeleportPhysics);
	if (Controller)
	{
		Controller->SetControlRotation(Snapshot.ControlRotation);
	}
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->SetEraImmediate(Snapshot.Era);
	}

	if (StatusEffects)
	{
		StatusEffects->RemoveAllEffects();
	}
	RestoreAll();

	UnfreezeMovement();
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		MoveComp->StopMovementImmediately();
	}
	PendingRecoil = FRotator::ZeroRotator;
	LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
	SetControlEnabled(true);

	if (AGBPlayerController* PC = Cast<AGBPlayerController>(Controller))
	{
		if (PC->GetOpenMenu() == EGBMenu::Death)
		{
			PC->SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
		}
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::SetupPlayerInputComponent(UInputComponent* PlayerInputComponent)
{

exec
"C:\\Windows\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command 'Get-Content Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.cpp; Get-Content Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.cpp | Select-Object -Skip 365 -First 145; Get-Content Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBGun.cpp; Get-Content Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/World/GBPickup.cpp | Select-Object -Skip 170 -First 80' in C:\GameJam

#include "UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h"

#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/GameInstance.h"
#include "Engine/Texture2D.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "TextureResource.h"

namespace GBSpriteLocal
{
	const FColor MissingColor(255, 0, 255, 255);
	const FColor TransparentColor(0, 0, 0, 0);

	int32 HexValue(TCHAR C)
	{
		if (C >= TEXT('0') && C <= TEXT('9')) return C - TEXT('0');
		if (C >= TEXT('a') && C <= TEXT('f')) return 10 + C - TEXT('a');
		if (C >= TEXT('A') && C <= TEXT('F')) return 10 + C - TEXT('A');
		return -1;
	}

	/** "#RRGGBB" or "#RRGGBBAA" (the '#' is optional) -> sRGB color. Parsed here, byte-exact, so the art keeps its exact colors. */
	bool ParseHexColor(const FString& InHex, FColor& OutColor)
	{
		FString Hex = InHex.TrimStartAndEnd();
		if (Hex.StartsWith(TEXT("#")))
		{
			Hex.RightChopInline(1);
		}
		if (Hex.Len() != 6 && Hex.Len() != 8)
		{
			return false;
		}
		uint8 Bytes[4] = { 0, 0, 0, 255 };
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Hex.Len() / 2; ++i)
		{
			const int32 Hi = HexValue(Hex[i * 2]);
			const int32 Lo = HexValue(Hex[i * 2 + 1]);
			if (Hi < 0 || Lo < 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Bytes[i] = (uint8)(Hi * 16 + Lo);
		}
		OutColor = FColor(Bytes[0], Bytes[1], Bytes[2], Bytes[3]);
		return true;
	}
}

UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	const UWorld* World = (GEngine && WorldContextObject) ? GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull) : nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = World ? World->GetGameInstance() : nullptr;
	return GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBPixelSpriteLibrary>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Initialize(FSubsystemCollectionBase& Collection)
{
	Super::Initialize(Collection);

	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = Collection.InitializeDependency<UGBDataSubsystem>())
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.AddUObject(this, &UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::ClearCache);
	}
}

void UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::ClearCache()
{
	Cache.Reset();
	TileCache.Reset();
}

bool UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::BuildPixels(const FGBSpriteDefinition& Definition, TArray<FColor>& OutPixels, int32& OutWidth, int32& OutHeight)
{
	OutPixels.Reset();
	OutWidth = 0;
	OutHeight = 0;

	if (Definition.Rows.Num() == 0 || Definition.Rows[0].Len() == 0)
	{
		return false;
	}
	const int32 Width = Definition.Rows[0].Len();
	for (const FString& Row : Definition.Rows)
	{
		if (Row.Len() != Width)
		{
			return false;
		}
	}

	TMap<TCHAR, FColor> Colors;
	for (const TPair<FString, FString>& Entry : Definition.Palette)
	{
		if (Entry.Key.Len() == 0)
		{
			continue;
		}
		FColor Color;
		Colors.Add(Entry.Key[0], GBSpriteLocal::ParseHexColor(Entry.Value, Color) ? Color : GBSpriteLocal::MissingColor);
	}

	const int32 Height = Definition.Rows.Num();
	OutPixels.Reserve(Width * Height);
	for (const FString& Row : Definition.Rows)
	{
		for (int32 X = 0; X < Width; ++X)
		{
			const TCHAR C = Row[X];
			if (C == TEXT('.') || C == TEXT(' '))
			{
				OutPixels.Add(GBSpriteLocal::TransparentColor);
			}
			else if (const FColor* Found = Colors.Find(C))
			{
				OutPixels.Add(*Found);
			}
			else
			{
				OutPixels.Add(GBSpriteLocal::MissingColor);
			}
		}
	}
	OutWidth = Width;
	OutHeight = Height;
	return true;
}

UTexture2D* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreateTexture(const TArray<FColor>& Pixels, int32 Width, int32 Height, FName DebugName, bool bWrap)
{
	if (Width <= 0 || Height <= 0 || Pixels.Num() != Width * Height)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	UTexture2D* Texture = UTexture2D::CreateTransient(Width, Height, PF_B8G8R8A8);
	if (!Texture || !Texture->GetPlatformData() || Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips.Num() == 0)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Pixel sprite '%s': could not create a %dx%d texture."), *DebugName.ToString(), Width, Height);
		return nullptr;
	}

	Texture->Filter = TF_Nearest;
	Texture->SRGB = true;
	Texture->NeverStream = true;
	Texture->CompressionSettings = TC_EditorIcon;
	Texture->LODGroup = TEXTUREGROUP_Pixels2D;
	// Sprites clamp (no bleeding from the opposite edge); tiles repeat across a structure's faces.
	Texture->AddressX = bWrap ? TA_Wrap : TA_Clamp;
	Texture->AddressY = bWrap ? TA_Wrap : TA_Clamp;
#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
	Texture->MipGenSettings = TMGS_NoMipmaps;
#endif

	// FColor is laid out B, G, R, A in memory, which is exactly PF_B8G8R8A8; rows go top to bottom.
	FTexture2DMipMap& Mip = Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips[0];
	void* Data = Mip.BulkData.Lock(LOCK_READ_WRITE);
	FMemory::Memcpy(Data, Pixels.GetData(), Pixels.Num() * sizeof(FColor));
	Mip.BulkData.Unlock();
	Texture->UpdateResource();
	return Texture;
}

UTexture2D* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::GetSprite(FName SpriteId)
{
	return GetOrBuild(SpriteId, /*bWrap*/ false);
}

UTexture2D* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::GetTileTexture(FName SpriteId)
{
	return GetOrBuild(SpriteId, /*bWrap*/ true);
}

UTexture2D* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::GetOrBuild(FName SpriteId, bool bWrap)
{
	TMap<FName, TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>>& TargetCache = bWrap ? TileCache : Cache;
	if (TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>* Cached = TargetCache.Find(SpriteId))
	{
		if (*Cached)
		{
			return *Cached;
		}
	}

	TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>& Missing = bWrap ? MissingTileTexture : MissingTexture;
	if (!Missing)
	{
		constexpr int32 Size = 8;
		TArray<FColor> Checker;
		Checker.Reserve(Size * Size);
		for (int32 Y = 0; Y < Size; ++Y)
		{
			for (int32 X = 0; X < Size; ++X)
			{
				Checker.Add(((X / 2 + Y / 2) % 2) ? FColor::Black : GBSpriteLocal::MissingColor);
			}
		}
		Missing = CreateTexture(Checker, Size, Size, TEXT("Missing"), bWrap);
	}

	UTexture2D* Result = nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = GetGameInstance();
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>() : nullptr;
	const FGBSpriteDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSprite(SpriteId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Pixel sprite '%s' is not in Sprites.json; using the magenta checker."), *SpriteId.ToString());
	}
	else
	{
		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
		int32 Width = 0;
		int32 Height = 0;
		if (BuildPixels(*Definition, Pixels, Width, Height))
		{
			Result = CreateTexture(Pixels, Width, Height, SpriteId, bWrap);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Pixel sprite '%s' has no rows or rows of different lengths; using the magenta checker."), *SpriteId.ToString());
		}
	}

	if (!Result)
	{
		Result = Missing;
	}
	// Missing sprites are cached too, so the warning is logged once per id and use (until the next data reload).
	TargetCache.Add(SpriteId, Result);
	return Result;
}

FIntPoint UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::GetSpriteSize(FName SpriteId) const
{
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = GetGameInstance();
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>() : nullptr;
	const FGBSpriteDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSprite(SpriteId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition || Definition->Rows.Num() == 0)
	{
		return FIntPoint::ZeroValue;
	}
	return FIntPoint(Definition->Rows[0].Len(), Definition->Rows.Num());
}

void UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::SetupBillboard(UBillboardComponent* Billboard, FName SpriteId, float WorldHeight)
{
	if (!Billboard)
	{
		return;
	}
	UTexture2D* Texture = GetSprite(SpriteId);
	if (!Texture)
	{
		return;
	}

	// FSpriteSceneProxy: half extents = ComponentMaxAxisScale * SpriteScale * 0.25 * TextureSize (FBatchedElements
	// builds the quad from Position +- SizeX / SizeY), so the full height is 0.5 * Scale * TextureHeight.
	// In editor builds the proxy also multiplies by the owner's SpriteScale and UBillboardComponent::EditorScale.
	const float TextureHeight = (float)FMath::Max(1, Texture->GetSizeY());
	float Scale = WorldHeight > 0.f ? 2.f * WorldHeight / TextureHeight : 1.f;
#if WITH_EDITOR
	if (const AActor* Owner = Billboard->GetOwner())
	{
		if (Owner->SpriteScale > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
		{
			Scale /= Owner->SpriteScale;
		}
	}
#endif
#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
	if (UBillboardComponent::EditorScale > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		Scale /= UBillboardComponent::EditorScale;
	}
#endif

	// Billboards use absolute scale by default, so the relative scale is the world scale.
	Billboard->SetRelativeScale3D(FVector(Scale));
	Billboard->bIsScreenSizeScaled = false;
	Billboard->SetSprite(Texture);
	Billboard->SetHiddenInGame(false);
	Billboard->SetVisibility(true);
	// The proxy reads the scale only when it is created: rebuild it.
	Billboard->MarkRenderStateDirty();
}
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::HasWeaponInSlot(EGBHeldSlot Slot) const
{
	return GetItemInSlot(Slot) != nullptr;
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::GetItemInSlot(EGBHeldSlot Slot) const
{
	AGBHeldItem* Item = Items.FindRef(Slot).Get();
	return IsValid(Item) ? Item : nullptr;
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::GetActiveItem() const
{
	AGBHeldItem* Item = GetItemInSlot(ActiveSlot);
	return (Item && Item->IsEquipped()) ? Item : nullptr;
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::IsHoldingStaff() const
{
	return ActiveSlot == EGBHeldSlot::Staff && GetActiveItem() != nullptr;
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::IsHoldingGun() const
{
	return ActiveSlot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun && GetActiveItem() != nullptr;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::EquipSlot(EGBHeldSlot Slot)
{
	AGBHeldItem* Target = GetItemInSlot(Slot);
	AGBHeldItem* Current = GetItemInSlot(ActiveSlot);
	if (Current && Current != Target && Current->IsEquipped())
	{
		Current->StopPrimary();
		Current->StopSecondary();
		Current->OnUnequipped();
	}

	ActiveSlot = Slot;

	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner()))
	{
		if (UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook())
		{
			Spellbook->CancelSustained();
		}
	}

	if (Target && !Target->IsEquipped())
	{
		Target->OnEquipped();
	}

	OnActiveItemChanged.Broadcast(Target);
	OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::SwitchTo(EGBHeldSlot Slot)
{
	if (bSwitchLocked || !HasWeaponInSlot(Slot))
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (Slot == ActiveSlot && GetActiveItem() != nullptr)
	{
		return true;
	}
	EquipSlot(Slot);
	return true;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::CycleSlot(int32 Direction)
{
	if (bSwitchLocked)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 Count = static_cast<int32>(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(EquipSlotOrder));
	const int32 Step = Direction >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
	const int32 Start = EquipSlotIndex(ActiveSlot);
	for (int32 Offset = 1; Offset < Count; ++Offset)
	{
		const int32 Index = ((Start + Step * Offset) % Count + Count) % Count;
		if (HasWeaponInSlot(EquipSlotOrder[Index]))
		{
			SwitchTo(EquipSlotOrder[Index]);
			return;
		}
	}
}

// ---- Ammo ----

void UGBEquipmentComponent::RefillAllAmmo()
{
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get(); IsValid(Item))
		{
			Item->RefillAmmo();
		}
	}
	// Era travel restores everything (GDD 8), stored guns included.
	for (const FName& WeaponId : OwnedWeapons)
	{
		EGBHeldSlot Slot = EGBHeldSlot::Staff;
		if (!IsWeaponEquipped(WeaponId) && FindWeaponSlot(WeaponId, Slot) && Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun)
		{
			StoredAmmo.Add(WeaponId, ComputeFullAmmo(WeaponId));
		}
	}
	OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
}

int32 UGBEquipmentComponent::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
{
	AGBHeldItem* Gun = GetItemInSlot(EGBHeldSlot::Gun);
	return Gun ? Gun->AddSpareMagazines(Count) : 0;
}

EGBHudSkin UGBEquipmentComponent::GetHudSkin() const
{
	const AGBHeldItem* Active = GetActiveItem();
	return Active ? Active->GetHudSkin() : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::DestroyAllItems()
{
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get(); IsValid(Item))
		{
			Item->StopPrimary();
			Item->StopSecondary();
			Item->OnUnequipped();
			Item->Destroy();
		}
	}
	Items.Reset();
}

#include "Weapons/GBGun.h"

#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
