void AGBGun::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	Super::InitFromDefinition(Definition, InOwner);

	const FName Required[] = { GBStat::Damage, GBStat::FireInterval, GBStat::MagazineSize, GBStat::SpareMagazines, GBStat::ReloadTime, GBStat::Range };
	for (const FName& Stat : Required)
	{
		if (!Definition.Stats.Contains(Stat))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapons.json '%s': missing stat '%s', using a default"), *Definition.Id.ToString(), *Stat.ToString());
		}
	}

	BuildViewModel(Definition);
}

// ---- Looks ----

UStaticMeshComponent* AGBGun::AddPart(FName Name, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& Location, const FVector& Size, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bGlow)
{
	UStaticMeshComponent* Part = NewObject<UStaticMeshComponent>(this, Name);
	Part->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Part->SetRelativeLocation(Location);
	Part->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Part->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Part->SetCastShadow(false);
	Part->RegisterComponent();
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Part, Shape, Size, Color, bGlow);
	Parts.Add(Part);
	return Part;
}

void AGBGun::BuildViewModel(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition)
{
	// Placeholder viewmodel (art rule: guns are 3D). Sizes in cm are purely visual; X = forward, Z = up.
	// The parts sit forward of the held-item anchor so the whole gun reads as one small pistol in the
	// lower right of the view, roughly 25-35 cm long, pointing forward.
	const float* PelletStat = Definition.Stats.Find(GBStat::Pellets);
	const bool bShotgun = PelletStat && *PelletStat > 1.f;
	const bool bAutomaticLook = Definition.bAutomatic;

	const float BodyLength = bShotgun ? 24.f : (bAutomaticLook ? 26.f : 20.f);
	const float BarrelLength = bShotgun ? 16.f : (bAutomaticLook ? 14.f : 9.f);
	const float BarrelThickness = bShotgun ? 4.f : 3.f;
	constexpr float BodyForward = 8.f;

	const FLinearColor BodyColor(0.08f, 0.08f, 0.10f);
	const FLinearColor BarrelColor(0.03f, 0.03f, 0.035f);
	const FLinearColor GripColor(0.12f, 0.10f, 0.10f);
	const FLinearColor NeonColor(0.0f, 0.9f, 1.0f);

	AddPart(TEXT("Body"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward, 0.f, 0.f), FVector(BodyLength, 5.f, 7.5f), BodyColor, false);
	AddPart(TEXT("Barrel"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward + BodyLength * 0.5f + BarrelLength * 0.5f, 0.f, 1.5f),
		FVector(BarrelLength, BarrelThickness, BarrelThickness), BarrelColor, false);
	AddPart(TEXT("Grip"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward - BodyLength * 0.5f + 2.5f, 0.f, -7.f), FVector(4.f, 4.f, 9.f), GripColor, false);
	if (bAutomaticLook)
	{
		AddPart(TEXT("Magazine"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward + 2.f, 0.f, -7.f), FVector(3.f, 2.5f, 8.f), BodyColor, false);
	}
	// Neon on the camera-facing side (the anchor sits right of the view, so +Y faces away).
	AddPart(TEXT("Neon"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward, -2.6f, 1.f), FVector(BodyLength * 0.8f, 0.35f, 0.7f), NeonColor, true);
	// Angle the whole viewmodel slightly toward the view center so the barrel points near the crosshair.
	Root->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator(0.f, -6.f, 0.f));
}

// ---- Ammo ----

int32 AGBGun::GetMagazineSize() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::MagazineSize, 1.f)));
}

int32 AGBGun::GetMaxSpareMagazines() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::SpareMagazines, 0.f)));
}

void AGBGun::SetAmmo(const FGBAmmoState& InAmmo)
{
	CancelReload();
	Ammo.InMagazine = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.InMagazine, 0, GetMagazineSize());
	Ammo.SpareMagazines = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.SpareMagazines, 0, GetMaxSpareMagazines());
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::RefillAmmo()
{
	CancelReload();
	Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
	Ammo.SpareMagazines = GetMaxSpareMagazines();
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

int32 AGBGun::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
{
	if (Count <= 0)
	{
		return 0;
	}
	const int32 Added = FMath::Clamp(GetMaxSpareMagazines() - Ammo.SpareMagazines, 0, Count);
	if (Added > 0)
	{
		Ammo.SpareMagazines += Added;
		NotifyEquipmentChanged();
	}
	return Added;
}

// ---- Reload ----

void AGBGun::Reload()
{
	if (bReloading || !bEquipped)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (Ammo.InMagazine >= GetMagazineSize() || Ammo.SpareMagazines <= 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	bReloading = true;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	ReloadDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, GetStat(GBStat::ReloadTime, 1.f));
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::CancelReload()
{
	if (!bReloading)
	{
		return;
	}
	bReloading = false;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::FinishReload()
{
	bReloading = false;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	if (Ammo.SpareMagazines > 0)
	{
		// The rounds left in the old magazine are lost (provisional, classic tactical style).
		--Ammo.SpareMagazines;
		Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
	}
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

float AGBGun::GetReloadProgress() const
{
	if (!bReloading)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	return ReloadDuration > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? FMath::Clamp(ReloadElapsed / ReloadDuration, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
}

// ---- Aim ----

void AGBGun::StartSecondary()
{
	if (bEquipped)
	{
		SetAiming(true);
	}
}

void AGBGun::StopSecondary()
{
	SetAiming(false);
}

void AGBGun::SetAiming(bool bInAiming)
{
	if (bAiming == bInAiming)
	{
		return;
	}
	bAiming = bInAiming;
	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner())
	{
		Player->SetMoveSpeedScale(bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimMoveSpeedMult, 1.f) : 1.f);
	}
}

void AGBGun::UpdateAimFOV(float DeltaSeconds, bool bInstant)
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UCameraComponent* Camera = Player ? Player->GetCamera() : nullptr;
	if (!Camera)
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Target = bAiming ? 1.f : 0.f;
	if (AimAlpha == Target)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AimAlpha <= 0.f)
	{
		HipFOV = Camera->FieldOfView;
	}

	if (bInstant)
	{
		AimAlpha = Target;
	}
	else
	{
		const float AimTime = GetStat(GBWeaponStat::AimTime, 0.15f);
		const float Step = AimTime > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? DeltaSeconds / AimTime : 1.f;
		AimAlpha = FMath::Clamp(Target > AimAlpha ? AimAlpha + Step : AimAlpha - Step, 0.f, 1.f);
	}
	Camera->SetFieldOfView(FMath::Lerp(HipFOV, GetStat(GBStat::AimFOV, HipFOV), AimAlpha));
}

void AGBGun::OnUnequipped()
{
	CancelReload();
	SetAiming(false);
	UpdateAimFOV(0.f, true);
	Super::OnUnequipped();
}

void AGBGun::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (bReloading)
	{
		ReloadElapsed += DeltaSeconds;
		if (ReloadElapsed >= ReloadDuration)
		{
			FinishReload();
		}
	}
	if (bEquipped)
	{
		UpdateAimFOV(DeltaSeconds, false);
	}
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
}

// ---- Shooting ----

bool AGBGun::PerformAttack()
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	if (!Player || !GetWorld() || bReloading)
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
	{
		Reload();
		return false;
	}

	const FGBDamageInfo BaseInfo = MakeDamageInfo();
	const float SpreadDegrees = bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimSpread, 0.f) : GetStat(GBStat::Spread, 0.f);

	--Ammo.InMagazine;

	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	if (Def && Def->FireMode.Equals(TEXT("Projectile"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
	{
		FireProjectile(BaseInfo, SpreadDegrees);
	}
	else
	{
		FireHitscan(BaseInfo, SpreadDegrees);
	}

	const float RecoilYaw = GetStat(GBStat::RecoilYaw, 0.f);
	Player->AddRecoil(GetStat(GBStat::RecoilPitch, 0.f), Random.FRandRange(-RecoilYaw, RecoilYaw), GetStat(GBStat::RecoilRecovery, 0.f));

	NotifyEquipmentChanged();

	if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
	{
		Reload();
	}
	return true;
}

void AGBGun::FireHitscan(const FGBDamageInfo& BaseInfo, float SpreadDegrees)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();

	const FVector Origin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector AimDirection = Player->GetAimDirection();
	const FVector Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::Range, 5000.f);
	const int32 Pellets = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::Pellets, 1.f)));
	const float FalloffStart = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffStart, 0.f);
	const float FalloffEnd = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffEnd, 0.f);
	const float NearMult = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffNearMult, 1.f);
	const float FarMult = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffFarMult, 1.f);
	const float HeadshotMult = GetStat(GBStat::HeadshotMult, 1.f);

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBGunTrace), false, Player);
	Params.AddIgnoredActor(this);

	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Pellets; ++Index)
	{
		const FVector Direction = UGBCombatLibrary::ApplySpread(AimDirection, SpreadDegrees, Random);
		const FVector End = Origin + Direction * Range;

		FHitResult Hit;
		const bool bHit = World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Origin, End, GBWeapon::AimTraceChannel, Params);
		AActor* Target = bHit ? Hit.GetActor() : nullptr;

		if (Target && UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target))
		{
			FGBDamageInfo Info = BaseInfo;
			float Multiplier = UGBCombatLibrary::DistanceFalloffMultiplier(Hit.Distance, FalloffStart, FalloffEnd, NearMult, FarMult);
			if (const AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = Cast<AGBEnemyCharacter>(Target))
			{
				if (Enemy->IsHeadshotLocation(Hit.ImpactPoint))
				{
					Multiplier *= HeadshotMult;
					Info.bHeadshot = true;
				}
			}
			Info.Amount *= Multiplier;
			Info.HitLocation = Hit.ImpactPoint;
			Info.HitDirection = Direction;
			UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
		}

#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING
		// Placeholder tracer.
		DrawDebugLine(World, Muzzle, bHit ? Hit.ImpactPoint : End, FColor(255, 220, 120), false, 0.05f, 0, 0.6f);
#endif
	}
}

void AGBGun::FireProjectile(const FGBDamageInfo& BaseInfo, float SpreadDegrees)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();

	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::Range, 5000.f);
	const FVector Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	FVector AimDirection = (FindAimPoint(Range) - Muzzle).GetSafeNormal();
	if (AimDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		AimDirection = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage = BaseInfo;
	Config.Speed = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, 3000.f);
	Config.Radius = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, 5.f);
	Config.GravityScale = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
	Config.MaxRange = Range;
	Config.bPixelArt = false; // art rule: gun shots are 3D
	Config.Color = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(BaseInfo.Element));

	const int32 Pellets = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::Pellets, 1.f)));
	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Pellets; ++Index)
	{
		const FVector Direction = UGBCombatLibrary::ApplySpread(AimDirection, SpreadDegrees, Random);
		AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Muzzle, Direction, Player);
	}
}
			return false;
		}
		InOutDrop.Count = Wanted - Taken;
		bOutAllTaken = InOutDrop.Count <= 0;
		return true;
	}

	if (!InOutDrop.WeaponId.IsNone())
	{
		// Owner's rule (2026-11-12): a weapon never replaces the one in hand and nothing is left on the floor.
		// A new weapon goes to its slot if that is empty, otherwise it is stored (equipped later from the inventory).
		UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player->GetEquipment();
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(Player);
		const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(InOutDrop.WeaponId) : nullptr;
		if (!Equipment || !Def)
		{
			return false;
		}
		const bool bGun = Def->Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun;
		if (Equipment->OwnsWeapon(InOutDrop.WeaponId))
		{
			// A copy of a gun already owned is worth one spare magazine if that gun is in the slot; otherwise it is just used up.
			if (bGun && Equipment->IsWeaponEquipped(InOutDrop.WeaponId))
			{
				Equipment->AddSpareMagazines(1);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			Equipment->GiveWeapon(InOutDrop.WeaponId, true);
			if (!Equipment->OwnsWeapon(InOutDrop.WeaponId))
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		bOutAllTaken = true;
		return true;
	}

	if (!InOutDrop.SpellGroup.IsNone())
	{
		UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook();
		if (!Spellbook)
		{
			return false;
		}
		// Knowledge already owned is still "taken": the book is used up either way.
		Spellbook->LearnGroup(InOutDrop.SpellGroup);
		bOutAllTaken = true;
		return true;
	}

	return false;
}

void AGBPickup::InitPickup(const FGBDropDefinition& InDrop, FName InFlagOnPickup)
{
	Drop = InDrop;
	FlagOnPickup = InFlagOnPickup;
	if (HasActorBegunPlay())
	{
		ApplyLook();
	}
}

void AGBPickup::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	InteractVolume->OnComponentBeginOverlap.AddDynamic(this, &AGBPickup::HandleOverlap);
	ApplyLook();
}

bool AGBPickup::IsWalkOver() const
{
	return !Drop.ItemId.IsNone() && Drop.WeaponId.IsNone() && Drop.SpellGroup.IsNone();
}

{

// Spell behavior: a summoned ice shield the caster raises like a first-person RPG shield (GDD 6).
// Casting summons it for Duration (or until its Health is used up). It does nothing by itself: it registers in the
// caster's UGBSpellbookComponent, which raises / lowers it with the Block input. Only while raised it removes part

//   Duration              seconds the shield stays summoned

	float GetShieldMaxHealth() const { return MaxShieldHealth; }

	float GetTimeRemaining() const;

	/** Modifier source of the raised-shield slow on the caster's stats. */
	static const FName RaisedSource;

	/** Pure rule, also usable by tests: how much of a hit the shield removes. HitDirection travels from the source to the caster. */
	static float ComputeBlockedDamage(float Amount, const FVector& HitDirection, const FVector& AimDirection, float BlockFraction, float ShieldHealthLeft);

	virtual void StartSpell() override;
	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;

	void HandleIncomingDamage(FGBDamageInfo& Info);

	TWeakObjectPtr<UGBAttributeComponent> BoundAttributes;
	FDelegateHandle DamageHandle;
	float ShieldHealth = 0.f;
	float MaxShieldHealth = 0.f;
	float BlockFraction = 0.f;
	float RaisedMoveSpeedMult = 1.f;
	/** World time (dilated, like the end timer) when the shield expires. */
	double ExpireTime = 0.0;
	bool bRaised = false;

}

float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetShieldHealthFraction() const
{
	const AGBSpell_Shield* Current = GetActiveShield();
	if (!Current || Current->GetShieldMaxHealth() <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	return FMath::Clamp(Current->GetShieldHealth() / Current->GetShieldMaxHealth(), 0.f, 1.f);
}

float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetShieldTimeRemaining() const
{
	const AGBSpell_Shield* Current = GetActiveShield();
	return Current ? Current->GetTimeRemaining() : 0.f;
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::CancelSustained()
{
	if (IsValid(ActiveSustained))
	{
		AGBSpellActor* Sustained = ActiveSustained;
		ActiveSustained = nullptr;
		Sustained->OnCastReleased();
	}
	ActiveSustained = nullptr;
}

// ---- Cooldowns and cost ----

void UGBSpellbookComponent::StartCooldown(FName SpellId, float Seconds)
{
	if (Seconds <= 0.f)
	{
		CooldownEnd.Remove(SpellId);
		CooldownLength.Remove(SpellId);
		return;
	}
	CooldownEnd.Add(SpellId, GetNow() + Seconds);
	CooldownLength.Add(SpellId, Seconds);
}

float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetCooldownRemaining(FName SpellId) const
{
	const double* End = CooldownEnd.Find(SpellId);
	return End ? static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(0.0, *End - GetNow())) : 0.f;
}

float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetCooldownDuration(FName SpellId) const
{
	return CooldownLength.FindRef(SpellId);
}

float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetManaCost(FName SpellId) const
{
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	return FMath::Max(0.f, GBSpell::ResolveParam(*Definition, GBStat::ManaCost, 0.f, 0.f, false, GetOwnerStats(), 0.f));
}

// ---- Snapshot ----

void UGBSpellbookComponent::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.KnownSpells = KnownSpells;
	Snapshot.Loadout = Loadout;
	Snapshot.EnchantElement = EnchantElement;
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{
	CancelSustained();
	RemoveAllPassives();

	KnownSpells.Reset();
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	for (const FName SpellId : Snapshot.KnownSpells)
	{
		if (Data && Data->FindSpell(SpellId))
		{
			KnownSpells.AddUnique(SpellId);
		}
	}
	if (!EnchantSpellId.IsNone() && Data && Data->FindSpell(EnchantSpellId))
	{
		KnownSpells.AddUnique(EnchantSpellId);
	}

	Loadout.Init(NAME_None, NumSlots);
	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < NumSlots && Slot < Snapshot.Loadout.Num(); ++Slot)

	// check the variations of KillZ
	AWorldSettings* WorldSettings = MyWorld->GetWorldSettings( true );

	if (!WorldSettings->AreWorldBoundsChecksEnabled())
	{
		return true;
	}

	if( GetActorLocation().Z < WorldSettings->KillZ )
	{
		UDamageType const* const DmgType = WorldSettings->KillZDamageType ? WorldSettings->KillZDamageType->GetDefaultObject<UDamageType>() : GetDefault<UDamageType>();
		FellOutOfWorld(*DmgType);
		return false;
	}
	// Check if box has poked outside the world
	else if( ( RootComponent != nullptr ) && ( GetRootComponent()->IsRegistered() == true ) )
	{
		const FBox&	Box = GetRootComponent()->Bounds.GetBox();
		if(	Box.Min.X < -HALF_WORLD_MAX || Box.Max.X > HALF_WORLD_MAX ||
			Box.Min.Y < -HALF_WORLD_MAX || Box.Max.Y > HALF_WORLD_MAX ||
			Box.Min.Z < -HALF_WORLD_MAX || Box.Max.Z > HALF_WORLD_MAX )
		{
			UE_LOGF(LogActor, Warning, "%ls is outside the world bounds!", *GetName());
			OutsideWorldBounds();
			// not safe to use physics or collision at this point
			SetActorEnableCollision(false);
			DisableComponentsSimulatePhysics();
			return false;
		}
	}

	return true;
}

	GetComponents(PrimitiveComponents);

void UNavigationSystemV1::Build()
{
	TRACE_CPUPROFILER_EVENT_SCOPE(UNavigationSystemV1::Build);
	
	UE_LOGF(LogNavigationDataBuild, Display, "UNavigationSystemV1::Build started...");

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		UE_LOGF(LogNavigation, Error, "Unable to build navigation due to missing World pointer");
		return;
	}

	FNavigationSystem::DiscardNavigationDataChunks(*World);

	const bool bHasWork = IsThereAnywhereToBuildNavigation();
	const bool bLockedIgnoreEditor = (NavBuildingLockFlags & ~ENavigationBuildLock::NoUpdateInEditor) != 0;
	if (!bHasWork || bLockedIgnoreEditor)
	{
		UE_LOGF(LogNavigation, Warning, "%s Navigation NOT building because navigation build is locked (flags: 0x%x).", __FUNCTION__, NavBuildingLockFlags);
		return;
	}

	const double BuildStartTime = FPlatformTime::Seconds();

	if (bAutoCreateNavigationData == true
#if WITH_EDITOR
		|| FNavigationSystem::IsEditorRunMode(OperationMode)
#endif // WITH_EDITOR
		)
	{
		SpawnMissingNavigationData();
	}

	// make sure freshly created navigation instances are registered before we try to build them
	ProcessRegistrationCandidates();
	
	// update invokers in case we're not updating navmesh automatically, in which case
	// navigation generators wouldn't have up-to-date info.
	if (bGenerateNavigationOnlyAroundNavigationInvokers)
	{
		UpdateInvokers();
	}

	if (BuildBounds.IsValid)
	{
		// Prepare to build tiles overlapping the bounds
		DirtyTilesInBuildBounds();
	}

	// and now iterate through all registered and just start building them
	RebuildAll();

	// Block until build is finished
	for (ANavigationData* NavData : NavDataSet)
	{
		if (NavData)
		{
			NavData->EnsureBuildCompletion();
		}
	}

#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING
	// no longer report that navmesh needs to be rebuild
	DefaultDirtyAreasController.bDirtyAreasReportedWhileAccumulationLocked = false;
#endif // !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING

	UE_LOGF(LogNavigationDataBuild, Display, "UNavigationSystemV1::Build total execution time: %.2fs", float(FPlatformTime::Seconds() - BuildStartTime));
	UE_LOGF(LogNavigation, Display, "UNavigationSystemV1::Build total execution time: %.5fs", float(FPlatformTime::Seconds() - BuildStartTime));
}

void UNavigationSystemV1::CancelBuild()
{
	for (ANavigationData* NavData : NavDataSet)
	{
		if (NavData)
		{
			if (NavData->GetGenerator())
			{
				NavData->GetGenerator()->CancelBuild();
			}
		}
	}
}

	int32 ValidSupportedAgentsCount = 0;
	for (int32 AgentIndex = 0; AgentIndex < AllSupportedAgentsCount; ++AgentIndex)
	{
		if (SupportedAgentsMask.Contains(AgentIndex))
		{
			++ValidSupportedAgentsCount;
void UNavigationSystemV1::OnNavigationBoundsUpdated(ANavMeshBoundsVolume* NavVolume)
{
	if (NavVolume == nullptr || IsNavigationSystemStatic())
	{
		return;
	}

	FNavigationBoundsUpdateRequest UpdateRequest;
	UpdateRequest.NavBounds.UniqueID = NavVolume->GetUniqueID();
	UpdateRequest.NavBounds.AreaBox = NavVolume->GetComponentsBoundingBox(true);
	UpdateRequest.NavBounds.Level = NavVolume->GetLevel();
	UpdateRequest.NavBounds.SupportedAgents = NavVolume->SupportedAgents;
	
	if (UpdateRequest.NavBounds.AreaBox.IsValid)
	{
		UpdateRequest.UpdateRequest = FNavigationBoundsUpdateRequest::Updated;
	}
	else
	{
		// Make a removal request if the bounds are invalid.
		UpdateRequest.UpdateRequest = FNavigationBoundsUpdateRequest::Removed;
	}

	CheckToLimitNavigationBoundsToLoadedRegions(UpdateRequest.NavBounds);
	AddNavigationBoundsUpdateRequest(UpdateRequest);
}

#include "Weapons/GBHeldItem.h"

#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.h"

AGBHeldItem::AGBHeldItem()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;

	Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	RootComponent = Root;

	SetActorEnableCollision(false);
}

void AGBHeldItem::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	WeaponId = Definition.Id;
	Slot = Definition.Slot;
	Element = Definition.Element;
	PlayerOwner = InOwner;
	SetOwner(InOwner);
	SetInstigator(InOwner);
	Random.GenerateNewSeed();
}

const FGBWeaponDefinition* AGBHeldItem::GetDefinition() const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	return Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
}

FString AGBHeldItem::GetDisplayName() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : WeaponId.ToString();
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	float Base = DefaultValue;
	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const float* Value = Def->Stats.Find(Stat))
		{
			Base = *Value;
		}
	}

	if (const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats())
	{
		TArray<FName, TInlineAllocator<3>> Keys;
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponKey(WeaponId, Stat));
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponSlotKey(Slot, Stat));
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponAllKey(Stat));
		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Base);
	}
	return Base;
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnEquipped()
{
	bEquipped = true;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(false);
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnUnequipped()
{
	bEquipped = false;
	bTriggerHeld = false;
	bAttackQueued = false;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
}

EGBHudSkin AGBHeldItem::GetHudSkin() const
{
	return Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun ? EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetTimeSinceLastAttack() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? static_cast<float>(World->GetTimeSeconds() - LastAttackTime) : 1000.f;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::IsAutomatic() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return Def && Def->bAutomatic;
}

UGBStatsComponent* AGBHeldItem::GetOwnerStats() const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	return Player ? Player->GetStats() : nullptr;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::GetActiveEnchant(EGBElement& OutElement, FName& OutOnHitEffect) const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	const UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player ? Player->GetStatusEffects() : nullptr;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Effects || !Data)
	{
		return false;
	}

	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Active : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
	{
		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Def = Data->FindStatusEffect(Active.EffectId);
		if (!Def)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FString* ElementName = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponElement);
		if (!ElementName || ElementName->IsEmpty())
		{
			continue;
		}
		const int64 Value = StaticEnum<EGBElement>()->GetValueByNameString(*ElementName);
		if (Value == INDEX_NONE)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Status effect '%s': unknown WeaponElement '%s'"), *Active.EffectId.ToString(), **ElementName);
			continue;
		}
		OutElement = static_cast<EGBElement>(Value);
		const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponOnHitEffect);
		OutOnHitEffect = (Effect && !Effect->IsEmpty()) ? FName(**Effect) : NAME_None;
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

FGBDamageInfo AGBHeldItem::MakeDamageInfo() const
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = GetStat(GBStat::Damage);
	Info.Element = Element;
	Info.Instigator = GetPlayerOwner();
	Info.Causer = const_cast<AGBHeldItem*>(this);
	Info.SourceId = WeaponId;

	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
		{
			if (!Effect->IsEmpty())
			{
				Info.OnHitEffect = FName(**Effect);
			}
		}
	}

	// The enchant's damage multiplier arrives by itself through its "Weapon.Gun.Damage" modifier.
	EGBElement EnchantElement = EGBElement::Physical;
	FName EnchantEffect = NAME_None;
	if (AcceptsEnchant() && GetActiveEnchant(EnchantElement, EnchantEffect))
	{
		Info.Element = EnchantElement;
		if (!EnchantEffect.IsNone())
		{
			Info.OnHitEffect = EnchantEffect;
		}
	}
	return Info;
}

void AGBHeldItem::PressTrigger()
{
	bTriggerHeld = true;
	bAttackQueued = true;
	UpdateTrigger();
}

#include "AI/GBSteering.h"

TOptional<int32> GBSteering::ChooseOffset(TConstArrayView<bool> Blocked, EGBSteerMode Mode, int32 PreferredSide)
{
	if (Blocked.Num() == 0 || Blocked.Num() % 2 == 0)
	{
		return {};
	}
	const int32 N = (Blocked.Num() - 1) / 2;
	const int32 Side = PreferredSide < 0 ? -1 : 1;
	auto IsFree = [&Blocked, N](int32 Offset) { return !Blocked[Offset + N]; };

	if (Mode != EGBSteerMode::GoAround && IsFree(0))
	{
		return 0;
	}

	if (Mode == EGBSteerMode::Free)
	{
		// Closest angle to the target wins; the preferred side only breaks ties.
		for (int32 Step = 1; Step <= N; ++Step)
		{
			if (IsFree(Side * Step))
			{
				return Side * Step;
			}
			if (IsFree(-Side * Step))
			{
				return -Side * Step;
			}
		}
	}
	else
	{
		// The whole preferred side first, even at a wider angle than a free direction on the other side.
		for (int32 Step = 1; Step <= N; ++Step)
		{
			if (IsFree(Side * Step))
			{
				return Side * Step;
			}
		}
		for (int32 Step = 1; Step <= N; ++Step)
		{
			if (IsFree(-Side * Step))
			{
				return -Side * Step;
			}
		}
	}

	if (Mode == EGBSteerMode::GoAround && IsFree(0))
	{
		return 0;
	}
	return {};
}
	}

	const float Actual = FMath::Min(Amount, Health);
	Health -= Actual;
	TimeSinceDamage = 0.f;

	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnDamaged.Broadcast(Hit, Actual);

	if (Health <= 0.f && !bDead)
	{
		Health = 0.f;
		bDead = true;
		OnDeath.Broadcast(Hit);
	}
	return Actual;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::Heal(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewHealth = FMath::Min(Health + Amount, GetMaxHealth());
	if (NewHealth != Health)
	{
		Health = NewHealth;
		OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	}
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreMana(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewMana = FMath::Min(Mana + Amount, GetMaxMana());
	if (NewMana != Mana)
	{
		Mana = NewMana;
		OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	}
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::HasMana(float Amount) const
{
	return Mana >= Amount;
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::SpendMana(float Amount)
{
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}
	if (Mana < Amount)
	{
		return false;
	}
	Mana -= Amount;
	TimeSinceManaSpend = 0.f;
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	return true;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreAll()
{
	bDead = false;
	Health = GetMaxHealth();
	Mana = GetMaxMana();
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::SetHealthAndMana(float NewHealth, float NewMana)
{
	Health = FMath::Clamp(NewHealth, 0.f, GetMaxHealth());
	Mana = FMath::Clamp(NewMana, 0.f, GetMaxMana());
	// A snapshot with health revives; one at 0 leaves the owner dead (no OnDeath: nothing hit it).
	bDead = Health <= 0.f;
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::StepRegen(float Current, float Max, float RegenPerSecond, float RegenDelay, float TimeSinceLastLoss, float DeltaTime)
{
	if (Current >= Max || RegenPerSecond <= 0.f || DeltaTime <= 0.f)
	{
		return Current;
	}
	// Only the part of this step that lies past the delay regenerates, so the result does not depend on frame rate.
	const float RegenTime = FMath::Clamp(TimeSinceLastLoss + DeltaTime - FMath::Max(0.f, RegenDelay), 0.f, DeltaTime);
	return FMath::Min(Current + RegenPerSecond * RegenTime, Max);
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);

	if (!bDead)
	{
		const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();

		const float MaxHealth = GetMaxHealth();
		const float NewHealth = StepRegen(Health, MaxHealth, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::HealthRegen, 0.f),
			GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::HealthRegenDelay, 0.f), TimeSinceDamage, DeltaTime);
		if (NewHealth != Health)
		{
			Health = NewHealth;
			OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, MaxHealth);
		}

		const float MaxMana = GetMaxMana();
		const float NewMana = StepRegen(Mana, MaxMana, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::ManaRegen, 0.f),
			GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::ManaRegenDelay, 0.f), TimeSinceManaSpend, DeltaTime);
		if (NewMana != Mana)
		{
			Mana = NewMana;
			OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, MaxMana);
		}
	}

	TimeSinceDamage += DeltaTime;
	TimeSinceManaSpend += DeltaTime;
}
	for (int32 i = 0; i < PageCount; ++i)
	{
		const FVector2D Size = MeasureLabel(Tabs[i], 24.f);
		const bool bSelected = MenuState.Page == i;
		if (bSelected)
		{
			DrawRect(Style.Accent * FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, 0.45f), TabX - 10.f * S, PY + 14.f * S, Size.X + 20.f * S, Size.Y + 8.f * S);
		}
		AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::Tab, TabX - 10.f * S, PY + 14.f * S, Size.X + 20.f * S, Size.Y + 8.f * S, i);
		DrawLabel(Tabs[i], Style.Text, TabX, PY + 18.f * S, 24.f, 0.f, bShadow);
		TabX += Size.X + 44.f * S;
	}
	DrawLabel(TEXT("Q / E or click a tab: switch page   Esc: close"), Style.Text, PX + PW - 24.f * S, PY + 22.f * S, 16.f, 1.f, bShadow);

	if (!Player)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float CX = PX + 24.f * S;
	const float CY = PY + 70.f * S;
	const float CW = PW - 48.f * S;
	const float CH = PH - 100.f * S;
	if (MenuState.Page == PageItems)
	{
		DrawItemsPage(Player, Style, CX, CY, CW, CH);
	}
	else if (MenuState.Page == PageWeapons)
	{
		DrawWeaponsPage(Player, Style, CX, CY, CW, CH);
	}
	else if (MenuState.Page == PageSpells)
	{
		DrawSpellsPage(Player, Style, CX, CY, CW, CH);
	}
	else
	{
		DrawSkillTrees(Player, CY);
	}
}

void AGBHUD::DrawItemsPage(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, const FGBHudSkinStyle& Style, float X, float Y, float W, float H)
{
	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
	const float S = UIScale();
	const bool bShadow = !Style.bChunky;
	const UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player->GetInventory();
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const TArray<FName> List = GetInventoryList(Player);
	if (!Inventory || List.Num() == 0)
	{
		DrawLabel(TEXT("Your bag is empty."), Style.Text, X, Y, 22.f, 0.f, bShadow);
		return;
	}
	MenuState.Cursor = FMath::Clamp(MenuState.Cursor, 0, List.Num() - 1);

	const float ListW = W * 0.5f;
	const float RowH = 34.f * S;
	const int32 Visible = FMath::Max(1, FMath::FloorToInt(H / RowH));
	const int32 First = FMath::Clamp(MenuState.Cursor - Visible + 1, 0, FMath::Max(0, List.Num() - Visible));
	for (int32 i = First; i < List.Num() && i < First + Visible; ++i)
	{
		const FName ItemId = List[i];
		const float RowY = Y + (i - First) * RowH;
		if (i == MenuState.Cursor)
		{
			DrawRect(Style.Accent * FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, 0.35f), X - 6.f * S, RowY - 3.f * S, ListW, RowH - 2.f * S);
		}
		AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::ItemRow, X - 6.f * S, RowY - 3.f * S, ListW, RowH - 2.f * S, i);
		FString Text = FString::Printf(TEXT("%s  x%d"), *ItemName(Data, ItemId), Inventory->GetCount(ItemId));
		if (Inventory->GetEquippedArmor() == ItemId)
		{
			Text += TEXT("  [Equipped]");
		}
		if (Inventory->GetQuickUseItem() == ItemId)
		{
			Text += TEXT("  [F]");
		}
		if (MenuState.PendingCombine == ItemId)
		{
			Text = TEXT("* ") + Text;
		}
		DrawLabel(Text, Style.Text, X, RowY, 20.f, 0.f, bShadow);
	}

	// Details of the selected item.
	const FName Selected = List[MenuState.Cursor];
	const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(Selected) : nullptr;
	const float DX = X + ListW + 30.f * S;
	const float DW = W - ListW - 30.f * S;
	float DY = Y;
	DrawLabel(ItemName(Data, Selected), Style.Text, DX, DY, 28.f, 0.f, bShadow, true);
	DY += 44.f * S;
	if (Def)
	{
		DrawLabel(ItemTypeName(Def->Type), Style.Text, DX, DY, 18.f, 0.f, bShadow);
		DY += 32.f * S;
		DY += DrawWrapped(Def->Description, Style.Text, DX, DY, DW, 18.f, bShadow) + 20.f * S;

		FString Hint;
		switch (Def->Type)
		{
		case EGBItemType::Consumable: Hint = TEXT("Click or Enter: use"); break;
		case EGBItemType::Throwable: Hint = TEXT("Throw it with [F] (quick-use, Shift cycles)"); break;
		case EGBItemType::Armor: Hint = Inventory->GetEquippedArmor() == Selected ? TEXT("Click or Enter: unequip") : TEXT("Click or Enter: equip"); break;
		case EGBItemType::Material:
		case EGBItemType::KeyItem:
			if (MenuState.PendingCombine == NAME_None)
			{
				Hint = TEXT("Click or Enter: combine with another item");
			}
			else if (MenuState.PendingCombine == Selected)
			{
				Hint = TEXT("Choose the second item (Esc: cancel)");
			}
			else
			{
				Hint = FString::Printf(TEXT("Click or Enter: combine with %s"), *ItemName(Data, MenuState.PendingCombine));
			}
			break;
		default: break;
		}
		DrawLabel(Hint, Style.Accent, DX, DY, 18.f, 0.f, true);
	}
}

void AGBHUD::DrawWeaponsPage(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, const FGBHudSkinStyle& Style, float X, float Y, float W, float H)
{
	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
	const float S = UIScale();
	const bool bShadow = !Style.bChunky;
	const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player->GetEquipment();
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const TArray<FName> List = GetWeaponList(Player);
	if (!Equipment || List.Num() == 0)
	{
		DrawLabel(TEXT("You carry no weapons."), Style.Text, X, Y, 22.f, 0.f, bShadow);
		return;
	}
	MenuState.Cursor = FMath::Clamp(MenuState.Cursor, 0, List.Num() - 1);

	// Display rows: a header before each kind, then its weapons. WeaponIndex -1 = header.
	struct FRow
	{
		FString Text;
		int32 WeaponIndex = -1;
	};
	TArray<FRow> Rows;
	FString LastGroup;
	int32 CursorRow = 0;
	for (int32 i = 0; i < List.Num(); ++i)
	{
		const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(List[i]) : nullptr;
		const FString Group = Def ? SlotName(Def->Slot, true) : FString(TEXT("Other"));
		if (Group != LastGroup)
		{
			Rows.Add(FRow{ Group, -1 });
			LastGroup = Group;
		}
		if (i == MenuState.Cursor)
		{
			CursorRow = Rows.Num();
		}
		Rows.Add(FRow{ WeaponName(Data, List[i]), i });
	}

	const float ListW = W * 0.5f;
	const float RowH = 34.f * S;
	const int32 Visible = FMath::Max(1, FMath::FloorToInt(H / RowH));
	const int32 First = FMath::Clamp(CursorRow - Visible + 1, 0, FMath::Max(0, Rows.Num() - Visible));
	for (int32 r = First; r < Rows.Num() && r < First + Visible; ++r)
	{
		const FRow& Row = Rows[r];
		const float RowY = Y + (r - First) * RowH;
		if (Row.WeaponIndex < 0)
		{
			DrawLabel(Row.Text.ToUpper(), Style.Text, X, RowY + 6.f * S, 16.f, 0.f, bShadow, true);
			continue;
		}
		const FName WeaponId = List[Row.WeaponIndex];
		if (Row.WeaponIndex == MenuState.Cursor)
		{
			DrawRect(Style.Accent * FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, 0.35f), X - 6.f * S, RowY - 3.f * S, ListW, RowH - 2.f * S);
		}
		AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::WeaponRow, X - 6.f * S, RowY - 3.f * S, ListW, RowH - 2.f * S, Row.WeaponIndex);
		const bool bEquipped = Equipment->IsWeaponEquipped(WeaponId);
		DrawLabel(bEquipped ? FString::Printf(TEXT("   %s  [equipped]"), *Row.Text) : TEXT("   ") + Row.Text, Style.Text, X, RowY, 20.f, 0.f, bShadow);
		if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr)
		{
			DrawLabel(GBHudText::ElementName(Def->Element), GBHudText::ElementColor(Def->Element), X + ListW - 18.f * S, RowY + 2.f * S, 16.f, 1.f, true);
		}
	}

	// Details of the selected weapon.
	const FName Selected = List[MenuState.Cursor];
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(Selected) : nullptr;
	const float DX = X + ListW + 30.f * S;
	const float DW = W - ListW - 30.f * S;
	float DY = Y;
	DrawLabel(WeaponName(Data, Selected), Style.Text, DX, DY, 28.f, 0.f, bShadow, true);
	DY += 44.f * S;
	if (!Def)
	{
		DrawLabel(TEXT("Unknown weapon"), Style.Text, DX, DY, 18.f, 0.f, bShadow);
		return;
	}
	DrawLabel(FString::Printf(TEXT("%s   %s"), *SlotName(Def->Slot, false), *GBHudText::ElementName(Def->Element)),
		GBHudText::ElementColor(Def->Element), DX, DY, 18.f, 0.f, true);
	DY += 32.f * S;
	for (const FString& Line : WeaponStatLines(*Def))
	{
		DrawLabel(Line, Style.Text, DX, DY, 18.f, 0.f, bShadow);
		DY += 28.f * S;
	}
	DY += 16.f * S;

	if (Equipment->IsWeaponEquipped(Selected))
	{
		DrawLabel(FString::Printf(TEXT("Equipped in the %s slot"), *SlotName(Def->Slot, false).ToLower()), Style.Accent, DX, DY, 18.f, 0.f, true);
	}
	else
	{
		const AGBHeldItem* Current = Equipment->GetItemInSlot(Def->Slot);
		const FString Hint = Current ? FString::Printf(TEXT("Click or Enter: equip (replaces %s)"), *Current->GetDisplayName())
			: FString(TEXT("Click or Enter: equip"));
		DrawWrapped(Hint, Style.Accent, DX, DY, DW, 18.f, true);
	}
}

		return NewBounds;
	}
	else
	{
		return FBoxSphereBounds(LocalToWorld.GetLocation(), FVector::ZeroVector, 0.f);
	}
}

void UBrushComponent::GetUsedMaterials( TArray<UMaterialInterface*>& OutMaterials, bool bGetDebugMaterials ) const
{
#if WITH_EDITOR
	// Get the material from each polygon making up the brush.
	if( Brush && Brush->Polys )
	{
		UPolys* Polys = Brush->Polys;
		for( int32 ElementIdx = 0; ElementIdx < Polys->Element.Num(); ++ElementIdx )
		{
			OutMaterials.Add( Polys->Element[ ElementIdx ].Material );
		}
	}
#endif // WITH_EDITOR
}

void UBrushComponent::PostLoad()
{
}

AStaticMeshActor* AGBTestArena::SpawnStructure(EGBShape Shape, const FVector& Center, const FVector& Size, FName PastTexture, float PastTileSize,
	const FLinearColor& FutureColor, const FRotator& Rotation)
{
	AStaticMeshActor* Solid = SpawnShape(Shape, Center, Size, FutureColor, false, true, Rotation);
	if (Solid)
	{
		Solid->SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
		Solid->GetStaticMeshComponent()->SetCastShadow(false);
	}
	SetPresence(SpawnTexturedShape(Shape, Center, Size, PastTexture, PastTileSize, false, Rotation), EGBEraPresence::PastOnly);
	SetPresence(SpawnShape(Shape, Center, Size, FutureColor, false, false, Rotation), EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly);
	return Solid;
}

APointLight* AGBTestArena::SpawnPointLight(const FVector& Location, const FLinearColor& Color, float Candelas, float Radius)
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	APointLight* Light = World ? World->SpawnActor<APointLight>(APointLight::StaticClass(), Location, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams()) : nullptr;
	if (!Light)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (UPointLightComponent* Component = Cast<UPointLightComponent>(Light->GetLightComponent()))
	{
		Component->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
		Component->SetIntensityUnits(ELightUnits::Candelas);
		Component->SetIntensity(Candelas);
		Component->SetLightColor(Color);
		Component->SetAttenuationRadius(Radius);
		Component->SetCastShadows(false);
	}
	return Light;
}

void AGBTestArena::SetPresence(AActor* Actor, EGBEraPresence Presence, FName RequiredFlag)
{
	if (!Actor)
	{
		return;
	}
	UGBEraComponent* Era = NewObject<UGBEraComponent>(Actor);
	Actor->AddInstanceComponent(Era);
	Era->RegisterComponent();
	Era->Configure(Presence, RequiredFlag);
}

FGBDropDefinition AGBTestArena::MakeItemDrop(FName ItemId, int32 Count)
{
	if (UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment())
	{
		Equipment->SetSwitchLocked(false);
	}

	// The owner died while the QTE was running (or the cast outlived it): never spawn from a dead caster.
	bool bOwnerDead = false;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner()))
	{
		bOwnerDead = Player->IsDead();
	}
	else if (const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes())
	{
		bOwnerDead = Attributes->IsDead();
	}
	if (bOwnerDead)
	{
		return;
	}

	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition)
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell);
		return;
	}

	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats();
	StartCooldown(SpellId, GBSpell::ResolveParam(*Definition, GBStat::Cooldown, Result.Power, Result.Reach, Result.bUsedQTE, Stats, 0.f));

	if (!Result.bSuccess)
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::QTEFailed);
		return;
	}

	FGBSpellCast SpellCast = GBSpell::ResolveCast(*Definition, Result.Power, Result.Reach, Result.bUsedQTE, Stats);
	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	SpellCast.Caster = Owner;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Owner))
	{
		SpellCast.Origin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
		SpellCast.Direction = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}
	else if (Owner)
	{
		FRotator EyeRotation;
		Owner->GetActorEyesViewPoint(SpellCast.Origin, EyeRotation);
		SpellCast.Direction = EyeRotation.Vector();
	}
	if (SpellId == EnchantSpellId)
	{
		SpellCast.Element = EnchantElement;
	}

	if (Definition->ActorClass.IsEmpty())
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("Spellbook: spell '%s' has no ActorClass in Spells.json."), *SpellId.ToString());
		return;
	}
	UClass* BehaviorClass = LoadClass<AGBSpellActor>(nullptr, *Definition->ActorClass);
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!BehaviorClass || !World)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("Spellbook: spell '%s' ActorClass '%s' is not a GBSpellActor class."), *SpellId.ToString(), *Definition->ActorClass);
		return;
	}

	const FTransform SpawnTransform(SpellCast.Direction.Rotation(), SpellCast.Origin);
	AGBSpellActor* SpellActor = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBSpellActor>(BehaviorClass, SpawnTransform, Owner,
		Cast<APawn>(Owner), ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn);
	if (!SpellActor)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("Spellbook: could not spawn the behavior of spell '%s'."), *SpellId.ToString());
		return;
	}
	SpellActor->InitSpell(SpellCast);
	SpellActor->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);

	if (IsValid(SpellActor) && SpellActor->IsSustaining())
	{
		ActiveSustained = SpellActor;
		SpellActor->OnSpellEnded.AddUObject(this, &UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleSustainedEnded);
	}
	OnSpellCast.Broadcast(SpellCast);
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleSustainedEnded()
{
	ActiveSustained = nullptr;
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleActiveItemChanged(AGBHeldItem* NewItem)
AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::SpawnItem(FName WeaponId)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner());
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
	if (!Player || !World || !Def)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GiveWeapon: unknown weapon '%s' (or no player / world)"), *WeaponId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	UClass* ItemClass = nullptr;
	if (!Def->ActorClass.IsEmpty())
	{
		ItemClass = LoadClass<AGBHeldItem>(nullptr, *Def->ActorClass);
		if (!ItemClass || ItemClass->HasAnyClassFlags(CLASS_Abstract))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapon '%s': ActorClass '%s' not found or abstract, using the slot default"), *WeaponId.ToString(), *Def->ActorClass);
			ItemClass = nullptr;
		}
	}
	if (!ItemClass)
	{
		switch (Def->Slot)
		{
		case EGBHeldSlot::Staff: ItemClass = AGBStaff::StaticClass(); break;
		case EGBHeldSlot::Sword: ItemClass = AGBSword::StaticClass(); break;
		default: ItemClass = AGBGun::StaticClass(); break;
		}
	}

	// Copy the definition: spawning must not rely on a pointer into the data tables.
	const FGBWeaponDefinition Definition = *Def;

	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
	Params.Owner = Player;
	Params.Instigator = Player;
	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
	AGBHeldItem* Item = World->SpawnActor<AGBHeldItem>(ItemClass, Player->GetActorTransform(), Params);
	if (!Item)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GiveWeapon: could not spawn '%s'"), *WeaponId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	USceneComponent* Anchor = Player->GetHeldItemAnchor() ? Player->GetHeldItemAnchor() : Player->GetRootComponent();
	Item->AttachToComponent(Anchor, FAttachmentTransformRules::SnapToTargetNotIncludingScale);
	Item->InitFromDefinition(Definition, Player);
	Item->OnUnequipped();
	Item->RefillAmmo();
	return Item;
}

{

			Health = FMath::Min(Health, GetMaxHealth());
			Mana = FMath::Min(Mana, GetMaxMana());
			OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
			OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());

		InitializeFromStats();
	}

void UGBAttributeComponent::InitializeFromStats()
{
	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();
	if (!Stats || !Stats->HasBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: no MaxHealth stat in the data; using %f."), *GetNameSafe(GetOwner()), GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxHealth);
	}

	bDead = false;
	Health = GetMaxHealth();
	Mana = GetMaxMana();
	TimeSinceDamage = 1000.f;
	TimeSinceManaSpend = 1000.f;
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::ApplyDamage(const FGBDamageInfo& Info)
{
	if (bDead || bInvulnerable || Info.Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	FGBDamageInfo Hit = Info;
	OnModifyIncomingDamage.Broadcast(Hit);

	const float Amount = FMath::Max(0.f, Hit.Amount * GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::DamageTaken, 1.f));
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		// Fully blocked or cancelled: not a hit, so no damage events and the regen delay keeps running.
		return 0.f;
	}

	const float Actual = FMath::Min(Amount, Health);
	Health -= Actual;
	TimeSinceDamage = 0.f;

	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnDamaged.Broadcast(Hit, Actual);

	if (Health <= 0.f && !bDead)
	{
		Health = 0.f;
		bDead = true;
	if (!bRaised || bEnded || ShieldHealth <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	FVector HitDirection = Info.HitDirection;
	if (HitDirection.IsNearlyZero() && Caster)
	{
		const AActor* Source = Info.Causer ? Info.Causer.Get() : Info.Instigator.Get();
		if (Source)
		{
			HitDirection = Caster->GetActorLocation() - Source->GetActorLocation();
		}
	}
	FVector AimOrigin;
	FVector AimDir;
	GetCasterAim(AimOrigin, AimDir);

	const float Blocked = ComputeBlockedDamage(Info.Amount, HitDirection, AimDir, BlockFraction, ShieldHealth);
	if (Blocked <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	Info.Amount -= Blocked;
	ShieldHealth -= Blocked;
	if (ShieldHealth <= KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		ShieldHealth = 0.f;
		SetRaised(false);
		// Broken: end after this damage call has finished.
		EndSpellAfter(0.f);
	}
}
	}
	const int32 NumWaves = Def->Waves.Num();

	State = EGBEncounterState::Active;
	CurrentWave = -1;
	NextWaveTimer = -1.f;
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
	OnStarted.Broadcast(this);

	if (NumWaves == 0)
	{
		Clear();
		return;
	}
	// The first wave comes at once; DelayBefore counts from the previous wave being cleared.
	SpawnWave(0);
}

void AGBEncounter::SpawnWave(int32 WaveIndex)
{
	const FGBEncounterDefinition* Def = FindDefinition();
	if (!Def || !Def->Waves.IsValidIndex(WaveIndex))
	{
		Clear();
		return;
	}

	CurrentWave = WaveIndex;
	const FGBEncounterWave& Wave = Def->Waves[WaveIndex];
	const int32 NumWaves = Def->Waves.Num();

	int32 Total = 0;
	for (const FGBEncounterSpawn& Spawn : Wave.Spawns)
	{
		Total += FMath::Max(0, Spawn.Count);
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const float RingRadius = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEncounterKey::SpawnRingRadius, 400.f) : 400.f;
	const FVector Center = GetActorLocation();
	const FTransform& ActorTransform = GetActorTransform();

	// Copy the spawn list: spawning runs BeginPlay code that could, in theory, reload data.
	const TArray<FGBEncounterSpawn> Spawns = Wave.Spawns;
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	int32 SpawnIndex = 0;
	for (const FGBEncounterSpawn& Spawn : Spawns)
	{
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Spawn.Count; ++i, ++SpawnIndex)
		{
			FVector Location;
			if (SpawnPoints.Num() > 0)
			{
				Location = ActorTransform.TransformPosition(SpawnPoints[SpawnIndex % SpawnPoints.Num()]);
			}
			else
			{
				const float Angle = 2.f * PI * static_cast<float>(SpawnIndex) / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(Total, 1));
				Location = Center + FVector(FMath::Cos(Angle), FMath::Sin(Angle), 0.f) * RingRadius;
			}

			const FRotator Facing(0.f, (Center - Location).GetSafeNormal2D().Rotation().Yaw, 0.f);
			if (AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(World, Spawn.EnemyId, Location, Facing, Presence))
			{
				Enemy->OnDied.AddUObject(this, &AGBEncounter::HandleEnemyDied);
				AliveEnemies.Add(Enemy);
			}
		}
	}

	if (AGBHUD* HUD = AGBHUD::Get(this))
	{
		HUD->ShowMessage(FString::Printf(TEXT("Wave %d / %d"), WaveIndex + 1, NumWaves));
	}
	OnWaveStarted.Broadcast(this);
}

void AGBEncounter::HandleEnemyDied(AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy)
{
	if (Enemy)
	{
		Enemy->OnDied.RemoveAll(this);
	}
	AliveEnemies.Remove(Enemy);

	if (State == EGBEncounterState::Active && GetAliveCount() == 0)
	{
		HandleWaveDefeated();
	}
}

void AGBEncounter::HandleWaveDefeated()
{
	if (State != EGBEncounterState::Active || NextWaveTimer >= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FGBEncounterDefinition* Def = FindDefinition();
	const int32 NextWave = CurrentWave + 1;
	if (Def && Def->Waves.IsValidIndex(NextWave))
	{
		NextWaveTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, Def->Waves[NextWave].DelayBefore);
	}
	else
	{
		Clear();
	}
}

void AGBEncounter::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	switch (State)
	{
	case EGBEncounterState::Waiting:
		// Covers the player already standing inside (revived at a checkpoint in the box, encounter configured late).
		TryStartFromTrigger();
		break;

	case EGBEncounterState::Active:
	{
		const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
		if (Player && Player->IsDead())
		{
			ResetEncounter();
			break;
		}

		if (NextWaveTimer >= 0.f)
		{
			NextWaveTimer -= DeltaSeconds;
			if (NextWaveTimer <= 0.f)
			{
				NextWaveTimer = -1.f;
				SpawnWave(CurrentWave + 1);
			}
			break;
		}

		// Enemies removed without dying (fell out of the world, destroyed by a script) do not block the wave.
		AliveEnemies.RemoveAll([](const TObjectPtr<AGBEnemyCharacter>& Enemy) { return !IsValid(Enemy) || Enemy->IsDead(); });
		if (AliveEnemies.Num() == 0)
		{
			HandleWaveDefeated();
		}
		break;
	}

	case EGBEncounterState::Cleared:
	default:
		SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		break;
	}
}

void AGBEncounter::Clear()
{
	if (State == EGBEncounterState::Cleared)

// A data-driven enemy: orcs of the past (pixel-art sprites), armed orcs of the future (3D), later bosses.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "GameFramework/Character.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Data/GBDataTypes.h"
#include "GBEnemyCharacter.generated.h"

class UGBStatsComponent;
class UGBAttributeComponent;
class UGBStatusEffectComponent;
class UGBEraComponent;
class UBillboardComponent;
class UStaticMeshComponent;
class UDamageType;

UENUM(BlueprintType)
enum class EGBEnemyState : uint8
{
	Idle,
	Chase,
	Windup,
	Recover,
	Dead
};

DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_OneParam(FGBOnEnemyDied, class AGBEnemyCharacter* /*Enemy*/);

/** Data keys used by enemies besides GBStat / GBString. */
namespace GBEnemyKey
{
	// Game.json "Values"
	inline const FName CorpseTime(TEXT("CorpseTime"));                         // seconds the corpse stays after death
	inline const FName MeleeReachTolerance(TEXT("EnemyMeleeReachTolerance"));  // melee hit lands within AttackRange * this
	// Movement around obstacles (navmesh path when it works, local steering otherwise)
	inline const FName NavRetryDelay(TEXT("EnemyNavRetryDelay"));              // s of local steering after a failed or stuck navmesh move
	inline const FName SteerFeelerLength(TEXT("EnemySteerFeelerLength"));      // cm the capsule is swept along each candidate direction
	inline const FName SteerAngleStep(TEXT("EnemySteerAngleStep"));            // degrees between candidate directions
	inline const FName SteerCandidatesPerSide(TEXT("EnemySteerCandidatesPerSide")); // candidates on each side of the straight one
	inline const FName SteerSideHoldTime(TEXT("EnemySteerSideHoldTime"));      // s a chosen side is kept (no flipping at corners)
	inline const FName StuckTime(TEXT("EnemyStuckTime"));                      // s window of the stuck check
	inline const FName StuckDistance(TEXT("EnemyStuckDistance"));              // cm: moved less than this in StuckTime while moving = stuck
	inline const FName GoAroundTime(TEXT("EnemyGoAroundTime"));                // s committed to one side after being stuck
	// Enemies.json "Strings"
	inline const FName ProjectileColor(TEXT("ProjectileColor"));               // "#RRGGBB", 3D projectiles of ranged enemies
}

/**
 * Built from a FGBEnemyDefinition (Enemies.json): stats, attack mode ("Melee" / "Ranged"), look, drops.
 * Simple state machine in C++ (no behavior tree assets): Idle until the player is within AggroRange and
 * present in the same era -> Chase (navmesh path if there is one; local obstacle steering when there is no nav
 * data, the path request fails or the enemy stops making progress) -> Windup (AttackWindup seconds, telegraphed;
 * needs line of sight) -> the hit (melee check in AttackRange, or an AGBProjectile) -> Recover (AttackInterval;
 * a ranged enemy that loses sight of the player moves meanwhile) -> Chase.
 * Slows and other effects work through UGBStatsComponent modifiers (MoveSpeed).
 * Headshots: hits on the top HeadFraction of the body deal the weapon's HeadshotMult (see UGBCombatLibrary).
 * Art rule: bPixelArt enemies are camera-facing sprites; the others are 3D placeholder meshes.
 * Enemies never respawn. On death: drops are rolled and the corpse is removed after a short delay.
 */
UCLASS()
class GLYPHBOUND_API AGBEnemyCharacter : public ACharacter
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	AGBEnemyCharacter();

	/**
	 * Spawns an enemy from Enemies.json at Location (adjusted to stand on the ground), using the definition's
	 * ActorClass or this class. Presence sets in which era it exists. Returns null if the id is unknown.
	 */
	static AGBEnemyCharacter* SpawnEnemy(UWorld* World, FName EnemyId, const FVector& Location, const FRotator& Rotation, EGBEraPresence Presence);

	FName GetEnemyId() const { return EnemyId; }
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* GetDefinition() const;
	EGBEnemyState GetState() const { return State; }
	bool IsDead() const { return State == EGBEnemyState::Dead; }

	UGBStatsComponent* GetStats() const { return Stats; }
	UGBAttributeComponent* GetAttributes() const { return Attributes; }
	UGBStatusEffectComponent* GetStatusEffects() const { return StatusEffects; }
	UGBEraComponent* GetEraComponent() const { return EraComponent; }

	/** True if a world-space hit location is on the head (top HeadFraction of the capsule). */
	bool IsHeadshotLocation(const FVector& HitLocation) const;

	FGBOnEnemyDied OnDied;

	/** One line for logs and the smoke run: how the enemy is moving right now (navmesh or steering, stuck state). */
	FString DescribeMovement() const;

	virtual void Tick(float DeltaSeconds) override;
	/** Dies through the attribute component (so its encounter moves on) instead of being silently destroyed. */
	virtual void FellOutOfWorld(const UDamageType& DamageType) override;

protected:
	virtual void BeginPlay() override;

	/** Applies the definition: stats, capsule size, look. Called by SpawnEnemy before BeginPlay finishes. */
	virtual void InitFromDefinition(const FGBEnemyDefinition& Definition);
	virtual void PerformAttack();
	virtual void HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow);
	void SetState(EGBEnemyState NewState);
	void ApplyMovementStats();
	AActor* FindTarget() const;

	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;

	void HandleDamaged(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage);
	void HandlePresenceChanged();
	void HandleDataReloaded();
	void MoveTowards(AActor* Goal, float DeltaSeconds);
	/** Local steering (GBSteering): sweeps the capsule along candidate directions around the one to the goal. */
	void SteerTowards(const AActor* Goal);
	/** A shrunken capsule (lifted by the step height) swept Distance along Direction hits something that blocks pawns. Ignores pawns. */
	bool IsDirectionBlocked(const FVector& Direction, float Distance, const AActor* Goal) const;
	/** Stuck check, once per tick after the state machine: moved less than StuckDistance over StuckTime while moving. */
	void UpdateStuck(float DeltaSeconds);
	void ResetStuck();
	float GetGameValue(FName Key, float DefaultValue) const;
	void StopMoving();
	void FaceTowards(const AActor* Goal);
	bool HasLineOfSight(const AActor* Goal) const;
	FVector GetChestLocation() const;
	bool IsRanged() const;
	/** Windup telegraph: brighter tint (3D) or a slightly bigger sprite (pixel art). */
	void SetTelegraph(bool bOn);

	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<UGBStatsComponent> Stats;
	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<UGBAttributeComponent> Attributes;
	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<UGBStatusEffectComponent> StatusEffects;
	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<UGBEraComponent> EraComponent;
	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<UBillboardComponent> Sprite;
	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<UStaticMeshComponent> BodyMesh;

	UPROPERTY() FName EnemyId = NAME_None;
	EGBEnemyState State = EGBEnemyState::Idle;
	float StateTime = 0.f;
	TWeakObjectPtr<AActor> Target;

	bool bInitialized = false;
	/** Took damage: chases even outside AggroRange. */
	bool bProvoked = false;
	bool bPixelArtLook = true;
	FString AttackModeName;
	/** After a failed or stuck navmesh move, steer locally until this reaches 0. */
	float NavRetryTimer = 0.f;

	// Local steering and the stuck check.
	/** This tick the enemy asked to move (Chase, or a ranged enemy repositioning). */
	bool bWantsToMove = false;
	/** Last move was local steering (not a navmesh path). */
	bool bSteering = false;
	/** A ranged enemy moving during Recover to get line of sight again. */
	bool bRepositioning = false;
	/** +1 or -1 (see GBSteering::ChooseOffset); random at spawn so a group does not all go round the same way. */
	int32 SteerSide = 1;
	float SteerSideHoldTimer = 0.f;
	float GoAroundTimer = 0.f;
	float StuckTimer = 0.f;
	FVector StuckOrigin = FVector::ZeroVector;
	/** Totals for DescribeMovement. */
	int32 StuckCount = 0;
	float NavMoveTime = 0.f;
	float SteerMoveTime = 0.f;
	/** Fell below KillZ: no drops (they would fall forever). */
	bool bFellOutOfWorld = false;
	FLinearColor BodyColor = FLinearColor::Gray;
	FVector SpriteBaseScale = FVector::OneVector;
};

namespace
{
	// Feel and visual constants (not gameplay balance). Mouse2D values come scaled by the engine's
	// default axis sensitivity (0.07 per pixel, BaseInput.ini), hence the conversions below.
	constexpr float MouseDegreesPerUnit = 2.5f;
	constexpr float MouseUnitsPerPixel = 0.07f;
	constexpr float StickYawDegreesPerSecond = 200.f;
	constexpr float StickPitchDegreesPerSecond = 140.f;
	constexpr float MaxLookPitch = 89.f;
	/** Ignore axis noise on wheel / D-pad style 1D actions. */
	constexpr float AxisStepThreshold = 0.1f;
	/** Interactables must be roughly in front of the view. */
	constexpr float InteractFacingMinDot = 0.3f;
	/** Snappy shooter movement: acceleration and braking as multiples of the walk speed (per second). */
	constexpr float AccelerationPerSpeed = 10.f;
	constexpr float BrakingPerSpeed = 8.f;
	constexpr float GroundFrictionValue = 8.f;
	/** Held item anchor and muzzle offsets from the camera (forward, right, up), cm. */
	const FVector HeldItemOffset(30.f, 15.f, -15.f);
	const FVector MuzzleOffset(40.f, 15.f, -12.f);

	/** Not a balance number: a hit far above any health pool (KillZ death). */
	constexpr float FallOutOfWorldDamage = 1.0e9f;
	const FName FellOutOfWorldSourceId(TEXT("FellOutOfWorld"));

	TSet<FName> GWarnedMissingPlayerStats;
}

#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"

class USphereComponent;

/** Everything a projectile needs; filled from weapon stats, spell params or item stats by whoever fires it. */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBProjectileConfig
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	/** The hit dealt on impact (Amount, Element, Instigator, SourceId, OnHitEffect...). Causer is set to the projectile. */
	UPROPERTY() FGBDamageInfo Damage;

	UPROPERTY() float Speed = 3000.f;
	/** Collision radius, cm. */
	UPROPERTY() float Radius = 12.f;
	/** 0 = flies straight; 1 = normal gravity (grenades). */
	UPROPERTY() float GravityScale = 0.f;
	/** The projectile disappears after travelling this far (cm). 0 = use MaxLifetime only. */
	UPROPERTY() float MaxRange = 5000.f;
	UPROPERTY() float MaxLifetime = 10.f;
	/** > 0: on impact (or when the fuse / range ends) it deals Damage in this radius instead of a single hit. */
	UPROPERTY() float ExplosionRadius = 0.f;
	UPROPERTY() bool bExplosionFalloff = true;
	/** cm/s pushed onto hostile characters by the blast: full at the center, 0 at the edge. 0 = no push. */
	UPROPERTY() float ExplosionKnockback = 0.f;
	/** Seconds the explosion shockwave (AGBExplosionEffect) is shown. Every explosion shows it, hit or miss. */
	UPROPERTY() float ExplosionEffectTime = 0.35f;
	/** > 0: it does not explode on contact with the world; it bounces and explodes after this many seconds (grenades). */
	UPROPERTY() float FuseTime = 0.f;
	/** Keeps going through actors it damages (each actor once). */
	UPROPERTY() bool bPierce = false;

	// ---- Looks (art rule: bPixelArt = camera-facing sprite, otherwise a small 3D mesh) ----
	UPROPERTY() bool bPixelArt = true;
	/** Sprite id from Sprites.json (pixel art). */
	UPROPERTY() FName SpriteId = NAME_None;
	UPROPERTY() FLinearColor Color = FLinearColor::White;
	/** Visual scale relative to the collision radius. */
	UPROPERTY() float VisualScale = 1.f;
};

DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_ThreeParams(FGBOnProjectileImpact, class AGBProjectile* /*Projectile*/, AActor* /*HitActor (may be null)*/, const FVector& /*Location*/);

UCLASS()
class GLYPHBOUND_API AGBProjectile : public AActor
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	AGBProjectile();

	/**
	 * Spawns and launches a projectile. It ignores its instigator and the instigator's team.
	 * Direction does not need to be normalized. Returns null if the spawn fails.
	 */
	static AGBProjectile* SpawnProjectile(UWorld* World, const FGBProjectileConfig& Config, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator);

	const FGBProjectileConfig& GetConfig() const { return Config; }

	/** Fired once, when it hits something or explodes (before it is destroyed). Spells use it to chain behavior. */
	FGBOnProjectileImpact OnImpact;

	virtual void Tick(float DeltaSeconds) override;

protected:
	virtual void BeginPlay() override;
	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;

	void InitProjectile(const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator);
		for (const FGBItemDefinition& Def : Data->GetItems())
		{
			if (Inventory->GetCount(Def.Id) > 0)
			{
				Result.Add(Def.Id);
			}
		}
	}
	// Items no longer in the data (after a reload) are still listed, at the end.
	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Entry : Inventory->GetItems())
	{
		if (Entry.Value > 0 && !Result.Contains(Entry.Key))
		{
			Result.Add(Entry.Key);
		}
	}
	return Result;
}

TArray<FName> AGBHUD::GetWeaponList(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	TArray<FName> Result;
	const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player ? Player->GetEquipment() : nullptr;
	if (!Equipment)
	{
		return Result;
	}
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	static const EGBHeldSlot Order[] = { EGBHeldSlot::Staff, EGBHeldSlot::Gun, EGBHeldSlot::Sword };
	for (const EGBHeldSlot Slot : Order)
	{
		for (const FName& WeaponId : Equipment->GetOwnedWeapons())
		{
			const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
			if (Def && Def->Slot == Slot)
			{
				Result.AddUnique(WeaponId);
			}
		}
	}
	// Weapons no longer in the data (after a reload) are still listed, at the end.
	for (const FName& WeaponId : Equipment->GetOwnedWeapons())
	{
		if (WeaponId != NAME_None)
		{
			Result.AddUnique(WeaponId);
		}
	}
	return Result;
}

TArray<FName> AGBHUD::GetAssignableSpells(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	TArray<FName> Result;
	Result.Add(NAME_None);
	const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player ? Player->GetSpellbook() : nullptr;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Spellbook || !Data)
	{
		return Result;
	}
	for (const FName& SpellId : Spellbook->GetKnownSpells())
	{
		const FGBSpellDefinition* Def = Data->FindSpell(SpellId);
		if (Def && !Def->bPassive && !Def->bRequiresGun && SpellId != Spellbook->GetEnchantSpell())
		{
			Result.Add(SpellId);
		}
	}
	return Result;
}

TArray<AGBHUD::FSkillNodeRef> AGBHUD::GetSkillNodes() const
{
	TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Result;
	if (const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		for (const FGBSkillNodeDefinition& Def : Data->GetSkillNodes())
		{
			FSkillNodeRef Ref;
			Ref.Id = Def.Id;
			Ref.Tree = GBHudMenuLocal::IsWeaponTree(Def.Tree) ? 1 : 0;
			Ref.Column = FMath::Max(0, Def.Column);
			Ref.Row = FMath::Max(0, Def.Row);
			Result.Add(Ref);
		}
	}
	return Result;
}

void AGBHUD::NavigateSkills(FIntPoint Direction)
{
	const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
	if (Nodes.Num() == 0 || Direction == FIntPoint::ZeroValue)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FSkillNodeRef* Current = Nodes.FindByPredicate([this](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Id == MenuState.SkillCursor; });
	if (!Current)
	{
		MenuState.SkillCursor = Nodes[0].Id;
		return;
	}

	// Both trees on one grid (the weapon tree to the right of the spell tree); pick the nearest node in the pressed direction.
	int32 MaxSpellColumn = 0;
	for (const FSkillNodeRef& Node : Nodes)
	{
		if (Node.Tree == 0)
		{
			MaxSpellColumn = FMath::Max(MaxSpellColumn, Node.Column);
		}
	}
	auto GridPos = [MaxSpellColumn](const FSkillNodeRef& Node)
	{
		return FVector2D(Node.Tree * (MaxSpellColumn + 2) + Node.Column, Node.Row);
	};
	const FVector2D Dir(Direction.X, Direction.Y);
	const FVector2D From = GridPos(*Current);
	float BestCost = TNumericLimits<float>::Max();
	FName Best = NAME_None;
	for (const FSkillNodeRef& Node : Nodes)
	{
		if (Node.Id == Current->Id)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FVector2D Delta = GridPos(Node) - From;
		const float Along = FVector2D::DotProduct(Delta, Dir);
		if (Along <= 0.f)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const float Across = FMath::Abs(FVector2D::CrossProduct(Delta, Dir));
		const float Cost = Along + 2.f * Across;
		if (Cost < BestCost)
		{
			BestCost = Cost;
			Best = Node.Id;
		}
	}
	if (Best != NAME_None)
	{
		MenuState.SkillCursor = Best;
	}
}

// ======================================================================== confirm actions

void AGBHUD::ConfirmItem(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
	UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player ? Player->GetInventory() : nullptr;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const TArray<FName> List = GetInventoryList(Player);
			Player->GetEquipment()->SwitchTo(EGBHeldSlot::Staff);
			if (!Player->GetSpellbook()->KnowsSpell(TEXT("IceShield")))
			{
				Player->GetSpellbook()->LearnSpell(TEXT("IceShield"));
			}
			Player->GetSpellbook()->EquipSpell(4, TEXT("IceShield"));
			const bool bOk = Player->GetSpellbook()->TryCastSlot(4);
			Player->GetSpellbook()->SetBlockHeld(true);
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shield cast=%d hasShield=%d raised=%d"),
				bOk ? 1 : 0,
				Player->GetSpellbook()->HasShield() ? 1 : 0,
				Player->GetSpellbook()->IsBlockRaised() ? 1 : 0);
			Shot(TEXT("9_shield"));
		});
		Add(27.6f + S, TEXT("lower the shield"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
		{
			Player->GetSpellbook()->SetBlockHeld(false);
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shield lowered hasShield=%d raised=%d"),
				Player->GetSpellbook()->HasShield() ? 1 : 0,
				Player->GetSpellbook()->IsBlockRaised() ? 1 : 0);
		});
		Add(28.8f + S, TEXT("quit"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter*)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE DONE"));
			FPlatformMisc::RequestExit(false);
		});
	}

	static bool Tick(float)
	{
		UWorld* World = SmokeWorld.Get();
		if (!World)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE aborted: the world went away"));
			return false;
		}
		const float Elapsed = static_cast<float>(FPlatformTime::Seconds() - StartSeconds);
		while (NextStep < Steps.Num() && Steps[NextStep].Time <= Elapsed)
		{
			const FStep& Step = Steps[NextStep++];
			AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(World);
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE step %d/%d (%.1f s): %s"), NextStep, Steps.Num(), Step.Time, Step.Name);
			if (!Player)
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE step skipped: there is no player character"));
				continue;
			}
			Step.Run(World, Player);
		}
		return NextStep < Steps.Num();
	}

	static void Start(UWorld* World)
	{
		if (!World || !World->IsGameWorld())
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.Smoke needs a running game world"));
			return;
		}
		BuildSteps();
		NextStep = 0;
		StartSeconds = FPlatformTime::Seconds();
		SmokeWorld = World;
		FTSTicker::GetCoreTicker().RemoveTicker(TickerHandle);
		TickerHandle = FTSTicker::GetCoreTicker().AddTicker(FTickerDelegate::CreateStatic(&Tick));
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE started: %d steps"), Steps.Num());
	}

	static FAutoConsoleCommandWithWorld SmokeCommand(
		TEXT("gb.Smoke"),
		TEXT("Scripted run of the main systems: logs lines starting with SMOKE, saves screenshots to Saved/Screenshots/Smoke and quits."),
		FConsoleCommandWithWorldDelegate::CreateStatic(&Start));
}

#endif // !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING

codex
I found a concrete line-of-sight bug: a trace that hits an allied enemy immediately counts as clear, even if a wall is behind that ally. That can make ranged enemies stay in place firing into cover, or let melee attacks reach through it. The Weapons page also displays the definition’s default element rather than the forged element saved on the owned weapon.

<#
Make-Materials.ps1 - Builds the project's materials from text (Tools/Jam/make_materials.py) with the Editor's
Python commandlet. The material .uasset files are not in git, so run this after a fresh clone and whenever
make_materials.py changes. The Editor must be closed (it would hold the asset files).

Usage:
  powershell -File Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1
Output: OK and the saved assets, or the error lines of the log.
Exit code: 0 = every material saved without Python errors.
#>
param(
    [string]$Project = (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..\..\Glyphbound\Glyphbound.uproject'),
    [string]$EngineDir = 'C:\Program Files\Epic Games\UE_5.9',
    [int]$TimeoutSeconds = 900
)
$Project = (Resolve-Path $Project).Path
$projDir = Split-Path $Project -Parent
$script = (Resolve-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'make_materials.py')).Path
$exe = Join-Path $EngineDir 'Engine\Binaries\Win64\UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe'
if (-not (Test-Path $exe)) { Write-Host "No editor at $exe"; exit 2 }

$stamp = [DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString('yyyyMMdd-HHmmss')
$logDir = Join-Path $projDir 'Saved\Logs'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $logDir | Out-Null
$log = Join-Path $logDir "make_materials_$stamp.log"

# Compiling a material needs a renderer: no -NullRHI here, and commandlets only start one with -AllowCommandletRendering.
$argList = @("`"$Project`"", '-run=pythonscript', "-script=`"$script`"", "-abslog=`"$log`"",
    '-AllowCommandletRendering', '-unattended', '-nopause', '-nosplash', '-nosound', '-nocrashreports')

$sw = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
$p = Start-Process -FilePath $exe -ArgumentList $argList -PassThru -WindowStyle Hidden
$timedOut = -not $p.WaitForExit($TimeoutSeconds * 1000)
if ($timedOut) {
    # A commandlet, not the interactive Editor: stopping it is safe.
    Write-Host "TIMEOUT after $TimeoutSeconds s: stopping the commandlet."
    Stop-Process -Id $p.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
}
$sw.Stop()

if (-not (Test-Path $log)) { Write-Host "No log at $log (exit $($p.ExitCode))"; exit 2 }
$lines = Get-Content $log -Encoding UTF8
$ours = @($lines | Where-Object { $_ -match 'make_materials:' })
$errors = @($lines | Where-Object { $_ -match 'LogPython: Error|make_materials: ERROR|Traceback|Fatal error|Assertion failed|Unhandled Exception' })
$ok = @($ours | Where-Object { $_ -match 'make_materials: OK' }).Count -gt 0

$asset = Join-Path $projDir 'Content\Glyphbound\Materials\M_PixelSurface.uasset'
$assetExists = Test-Path $asset

$ours | ForEach-Object { ($_ -replace '^\[[^\]]*\]\[[^\]]*\]', '').Trim() }
if ($errors.Count -gt 0) {
    Write-Host '---- errors ----'
    $errors | Select-Object -First 60 | ForEach-Object { ($_ -replace '^\[[^\]]*\]\[[^\]]*\]', '').Trim() }
}
Write-Host ("Materials: ok={0} asset={1} errors={2} exit={3} wall={4:N0} s  log: {5}" -f $ok, $assetExists, $errors.Count, $p.ExitCode, $sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, $log)
if ($ok -and $assetExists -and $errors.Count -eq 0 -and -not $timedOut) { Write-Host 'OK'; exit 0 }
Write-Host 'FAILED'
exit 1

455:void AGBEnemyCharacter::SteerTowards(const AActor* Goal)
456:{
457:	const FVector ToGoal = (Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation()).GetSafeNormal2D();
458:	if (ToGoal.IsNearlyZero())
459:	{
460:		return;
461:	}
462:
463:	const float Feeler = FMath::Max(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerFeelerLength, 150.f), 1.f);
464:	const float AngleStep = FMath::Clamp(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerAngleStep, 25.f), 1.f, 180.f);
465:	const int32 PerSide = FMath::Clamp(FMath::RoundToInt(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerCandidatesPerSide, 6.f)), 1, GBEnemyCharacterLocal::MaxSteerCandidatesPerSide);
466:
467:	TArray<bool, TInlineAllocator<2 * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::MaxSteerCandidatesPerSide + 1>> Blocked;
468:	Blocked.SetNum(2 * PerSide + 1);
469:	for (int32 Offset = -PerSide; Offset <= PerSide; ++Offset)
470:	{
471:		Blocked[Offset + PerSide] = IsDirectionBlocked(ToGoal.RotateAngleAxis(Offset * AngleStep, FVector::UpVector), Feeler, Goal);
472:	}
473:
474:	const EGBSteerMode Mode = GoAroundTimer > 0.f ? EGBSteerMode::GoAround
475:		: (SteerSideHoldTimer > 0.f ? EGBSteerMode::HoldSide : EGBSteerMode::Free);
476:	const TOptional<int32> Choice = GBSteering::ChooseOffset(Blocked, Mode, SteerSide);
477:	if (!Choice.IsSet())
478:	{
479:		// Boxed in on every side but the back: step back; the stuck check then commits to a side.
480:		AddMovementInput(-ToGoal, 1.f);
481:		return;
482:	}
483:	if (Choice.GetValue() != 0)
484:	{
485:		SteerSide = Choice.GetValue() > 0 ? 1 : -1;
486:		SteerSideHoldTimer = FMath::Max(SteerSideHoldTimer, GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerSideHoldTime, 1.f));
487:	}
488:	AddMovementInput(ToGoal.RotateAngleAxis(Choice.GetValue() * AngleStep, FVector::UpVector), 1.f);
489:}
