19790-
19791-void UMaterialExpressionVertexNormalWS::GetCaption(TArray<FString>& OutCaptions) const
{
	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
	UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player ? Player->GetInventory() : nullptr;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const TArray<FName> List = GetInventoryList(Player);
	if (!Inventory || List.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	MenuState.Cursor = FMath::Clamp(MenuState.Cursor, 0, List.Num() - 1);
	const FName ItemId = List[MenuState.Cursor];
	const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(ItemId) : nullptr;
	if (!Def)
	{
		ShowMessage(TEXT("Unknown item"), 1.5f);
		return;
	}

	switch (Def->Type)
	{
	case EGBItemType::Consumable:
		if (!Inventory->UseItem(ItemId))
		{
			ShowMessage(TEXT("Cannot use that now"), 1.5f);
		}
		break;
	case EGBItemType::Throwable:
		ShowMessage(TEXT("Throw it with [F] (quick-use)"), 2.f);
		break;
	case EGBItemType::Armor:
		if (!Inventory->EquipArmor(Inventory->GetEquippedArmor() == ItemId ? NAME_None : ItemId))
		{
			ShowMessage(TEXT("Cannot equip that"), 1.5f);
		}
		break;
	case EGBItemType::Material:
	case EGBItemType::KeyItem:
		if (MenuState.PendingCombine == NAME_None)
		{
			MenuState.PendingCombine = ItemId;
		}
		else if (MenuState.PendingCombine == ItemId)
		{
			MenuState.PendingCombine = NAME_None;
		}
		else
		{
			const FName Other = MenuState.PendingCombine;
			MenuState.PendingCombine = NAME_None;
			const FGBRecipeDefinition* Recipe = Inventory->FindRecipe(Other, ItemId);
			// Copy what the toast needs before combining: the recipe pointer belongs to the data tables.
			FString ResultName;
			if (Recipe)
			{
				if (Recipe->ResultItem != NAME_None)
				{
					ResultName = ItemName(Data, Recipe->ResultItem);
				}
				else if (Recipe->ResultWeapon != NAME_None)
				{
					const FGBWeaponDefinition* Weapon = Data->FindWeapon(Recipe->ResultWeapon);
					ResultName = (Weapon && !Weapon->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Weapon->DisplayName : Recipe->ResultWeapon.ToString();
				}
			}
			if (Recipe && Inventory->TryCombine(Other, ItemId))
			{
				ShowMessage(ResultName.IsEmpty() ? FString(TEXT("Combined")) : FString::Printf(TEXT("Created %s"), *ResultName), 2.5f);
			}
			else
			{
				ShowMessage(TEXT("These do not combine"), 1.5f);
			}
		}
		break;
	default:
		break;
	}
}

void AGBHUD::ConfirmWeapon(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player ? Player->GetEquipment() : nullptr;
	const TArray<FName> List = GetWeaponList(Player);
	if (!Equipment || List.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	MenuState.Cursor = FMath::Clamp(MenuState.Cursor, 0, List.Num() - 1);
	const FName WeaponId = List[MenuState.Cursor];
	const FString Name = GBHudMenuLocal::WeaponName(UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this), WeaponId);
	if (Equipment->IsWeaponEquipped(WeaponId))
	{
		ShowMessage(FString::Printf(TEXT("%s is already equipped"), *Name), 1.5f);
	}
	else if (Equipment->EquipOwnedWeapon(WeaponId))
	{
		ShowMessage(FString::Printf(TEXT("Equipped %s"), *Name), 2.f);
	}
	else
	{
		ShowMessage(TEXT("Cannot change weapons right now"), 1.5f);
	}
}

void AGBHUD::ConfirmSpellSlot(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player ? Player->GetSpellbook() : nullptr;
	if (!Spellbook)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 NumSlots = UGBSpellbookComponent::NumSlots;
	if (MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
	{
		const TArray<FName> Options = GetAssignableSpells(Player);
		if (Options.IsValidIndex(MenuState.SubCursor) && MenuState.Cursor < NumSlots)
		{
			if (!Spellbook->EquipSpell(MenuState.Cursor, Options[MenuState.SubCursor]))
			{
				ShowMessage(TEXT("Cannot equip that spell"), 1.5f);
			}
		}
		MenuState.bChoosingSpell = false;
	}
	else if (MenuState.Cursor < NumSlots)
void AGBPlayerCharacter::AddLookDelta(float YawDegrees, float PitchDegrees)
{
	if (!Controller)
	{
		return;
	}
	FRotator Rotation = Controller->GetControlRotation();
	Rotation.Yaw = FRotator::NormalizeAxis(Rotation.Yaw + YawDegrees);
	Rotation.Pitch = FMath::Clamp(FRotator::NormalizeAxis(Rotation.Pitch + PitchDegrees), -static_cast<double>(MaxLookPitch), static_cast<double>(MaxLookPitch));
	Rotation.Roll = 0.f;
	Controller->SetControlRotation(Rotation);
}

float AGBPlayerCharacter::GetDashCooldownRemaining() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? FMath::Max(0.f, static_cast<float>(DashReadyTime - World->GetTimeSeconds())) : 0.f;
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::EndDash()
{
	if (!bDashing)
	{
		return;
	}
	bDashing = false;
	DashTimeLeft = 0.f;

	UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement();
	if (MoveComp && MoveComp->MovementMode == MOVE_Flying)
	{
		// Changing movement mode may reset the jump count: keep it, so a dash never refills jumps.
		const int32 SavedJumpCount = JumpCurrentCount;
		MoveComp->SetMovementMode(MOVE_Falling);
		JumpCurrentCount = SavedJumpCount;
		MoveComp->Velocity = DashVelocity.GetSafeNormal() * MoveComp->MaxWalkSpeed;
	}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Aiming

FVector AGBPlayerCharacter::GetAimOrigin() const
{
	return Camera ? Camera->GetComponentLocation() : GetActorLocation();
}

FVector AGBPlayerCharacter::GetAimDirection() const
{
	// Control rotation is current this frame; the camera component only catches up when the view is computed.
	if (Controller)
	{
		return Controller->GetControlRotation().Vector();
	}
	return Camera ? Camera->GetForwardVector() : GetActorForwardVector();
}

FVector AGBPlayerCharacter::GetMuzzleLocation() const
{
	const FRotator AimRotation = GetAimDirection().Rotation();
	return GetAimOrigin() + AimRotation.RotateVector(MuzzleOffset);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::AddRecoil(float Pitch, float Yaw, float RecoverPerSecond)
{
	AddLookDelta(Yaw, Pitch);
	PendingRecoil.Pitch += Pitch;
	PendingRecoil.Yaw += Yaw;
	RecoilRecoverSpeed = FMath::Max(0.f, RecoverPerSecond);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::TickRecoil(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (IsQTEActive() || RecoilRecoverSpeed <= 0.f)
	{
		return; // the camera is locked during the QTE
	}
	const FVector2D Remaining(PendingRecoil.Pitch, PendingRecoil.Yaw);
	const float Length = static_cast<float>(Remaining.Size());
	if (Length <= KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		PendingRecoil = FRotator::ZeroRotator;
		return;
	}
	const float Step = FMath::Min(Length, RecoilRecoverSpeed * DeltaSeconds);
	const float BackPitch = static_cast<float>(Remaining.X) / Length * Step;
	const float BackYaw = static_cast<float>(Remaining.Y) / Length * Step;
	PendingRecoil.Pitch -= BackPitch;
	PendingRecoil.Yaw -= BackYaw;
	AddLookDelta(-BackYaw, -BackPitch);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Interaction

IGBInteractable* AGBPlayerCharacter::GetFocusedInteractable() const
{
	return Cast<IGBInteractable>(FocusedInteractable.Get());
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::UpdateFocusedInteractable()
{
	FocusedInteractable = nullptr;

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World || !CanUseGameplayInput())
	{
		return;
	}
	const float Range = ReadStat(GBStat::InteractRange, 250.f);
	if (Range <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector Origin = GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector ViewDir = GetAimDirection();

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldStatic);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	FCollisionQueryParams QueryParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBInteract), false, this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Range), QueryParams);

	AActor* Best = nullptr;
	float BestDot = InteractFacingMinDot;
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Actor = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (!Actor || Actor == this || Actor == Best)
		{
			continue;
		}
		IGBInteractable* Interactable = Cast<IGBInteractable>(Actor);
		if (!Interactable || !Interactable->CanInteract(this))
		{
			continue;
		}
		const float Dot = FVector::DotProduct((Actor->GetActorLocation() - Origin).GetSafeNormal(), ViewDir);
		if (Dot > BestDot)
		{
			BestDot = Dot;
			Best = Actor;
		}
	}
	FocusedInteractable = Best;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State

bool AGBPlayerCharacter::IsDead() const
{
	return Attributes && Attributes->IsDead();
}

int32 FHLSLMaterialTranslator::VertexNormal()
{
	if (ShaderFrequency != SF_Vertex)
	{
		bUsesTransformVector = true;
	}

	FString FiniteCode = FString("Parameters.TangentToWorld[2]");

	if (IsAnalyticDerivEnabled() && ShaderFrequency != SF_Vertex)
	{
		FString AnalyticCode = DerivativeAutogen.ConstructDeriv(FiniteCode, TEXT("Parameters.WorldGeoNormal_DDX"), TEXT("Parameters.WorldGeoNormal_DDY"), EDerivativeType::Float3);
		return AddCodeChunkInnerDeriv(*FiniteCode, *AnalyticCode, MCT_Float3, false, EDerivativeStatus::Valid);
	}
	else
	{
		return AddInlinedCodeChunk(MCT_Float3, *FiniteCode);
	}
}

int32 FHLSLMaterialTranslator::VertexTangent()
{
	if (ShaderFrequency != SF_Vertex)
	{
		bUsesTransformVector = true;
	}
	return AddExternalCodeChunk(TEXT("VertexTangent"));
}

	void ReportError(const FString& Message)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("%s"), *Message);
#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING
		if (GEngine)
		{
			GEngine->AddOnScreenDebugMessage(-1, 15.f, FColor::Red, Message);
		}
#endif
	}

	/** Reads and parses one file of the data folder. False (and an error) if it is missing or not valid JSON. */
	bool ReadJsonFile(const FString& FileName, TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& OutRoot)
	{
		const FString Path = UGBDataSubsystem::GetDataDir() / FileName;
		FString Text;
		if (!FFileHelper::LoadFileToString(Text, *Path))
		{
			ReportError(FString::Printf(TEXT("Data: cannot read %s"), *Path));
			return false;
		}

		const TSharedRef<TJsonReader<>> Reader = TJsonReaderFactory<>::Create(Text);
		if (!FJsonSerializer::Deserialize(Reader, OutRoot) || !OutRoot.IsValid())
		{
			ReportError(FString::Printf(TEXT("Data: %s is not valid JSON: %s"), *FileName, *Reader->GetErrorMessage()));
			OutRoot.Reset();
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	/** Loads the array field Field of FileName into Out. Out is left empty on any error. */
	template <typename T>
	void LoadArray(const FString& FileName, const TCHAR* Field, TArray<T>& Out)
	{
		Out.Reset();

		TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Root;
		if (!ReadJsonFile(FileName, Root))
		{
			return;
		}

		const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* JsonArray = nullptr;
		if (!Root->TryGetArrayField(Field, JsonArray) || !JsonArray)
		{
			ReportError(FString::Printf(TEXT("Data: %s has no array \"%s\""), *FileName, Field));
			return;
		}

		FText FailReason;
		if (!FJsonObjectConverter::JsonArrayToUStruct(*JsonArray, &Out, 0, 0, false, &FailReason))
		{
			ReportError(FString::Printf(TEXT("Data: %s \"%s\" could not be read: %s"), *FileName, Field, *FailReason.ToString()));
			Out.Reset();
		}
	}

	/** Loads the whole of FileName into Out. Out is reset to defaults on any error. */
	template <typename T>
	void LoadStruct(const FString& FileName, T& Out)
	{
		Out = T();

		TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Root;

1. **[P2] [AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:655](/C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:655)** — Hitting an allied enemy immediately counts as clear line of sight, without checking for cover behind it. Ranged enemies can stop repositioning and fire into a wall; melee attacks can pass the same visibility check through cover. **Smallest fix:** ignore the allied actor and repeat the trace until it reaches the player or blocking geometry.

2. **[P2] [AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:517–528](/C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:517)** — Steering checks obstacles ahead but never checks whether there is walkable ground ahead; the boxed-in fallback also steps backward unchecked at line 480. Unreal enables walking off ledges by default, so fallback movement can send enemies off platforms or into Kill-Z, letting players bypass fights. **Smallest fix:** reject directions without supporting walkable ground, including the backward fallback.

3. **[P2] [UI/GBHUD_Menus.cpp:1109, 1126–1127](/C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/UI/GBHUD_Menus.cpp:1109)** — The Weapons page displays `Def->Element` instead of the owned weapon’s saved element. A sword forged with fire, lightning or ice consequently still appears Physical in both its row and details, making forging look unsuccessful. **Smallest fix:** use `Equipment->GetWeaponElement(WeaponId)` for both displays.

4. **[P3] [UI/GBHUD.cpp:614–635](/C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/UI/GBHUD.cpp:614)** and [Spells/GBSpell_Wall.cpp:65](/C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Spells/GBSpell_Wall.cpp:65) — Several shipped visual settings are ignored: the shield hardcodes its sprite, size and tint despite `IceShield` defining `Sprite`, `SpriteSize`, `Distance` and `Color`; textured walls pass white instead of reading `IceWall.Color`. Editing those JSON values produces no corresponding visual change. **Smallest fix:** read the shield’s active cast settings and use `GetSpellColor()` for textured walls; remove obsolete world-space shield parameters.

I would fix line of sight, ledge safety and the weapon element display first. Static inspection found the owned-weapon ammo/element storage, shield expiry and modifier cleanup, menu-close unpausing, single death-click dispatch, damage-number delegate cleanup/cap/player filtering, and pixel-material cook path solid. No files were modified, and no build, tests or game were run.
